### Known Issues in 7000.0.0a20

- 2D: SpriteAtlasUtility.PackAllAtlases processes source textures from every Sprite Atlas V2 atlas when called without asset changes
    ([UUM-161409](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161409))

- Burst (Trunk): Burst native link fails with an "undefined symbol: strlen" error when building a Linux ARM64 dedicated server Player with IL2CPP
    ([UUM-161029](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161029))

- Core Runtime: System updates in both the "Default World" and the custom World when an ICustomBootstrap returns true from Initialize
    ([UUM-160214](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160214))

- Core Runtime: Systems update twice per frame after restarting the Worlds with World.DisposeAllWorlds and DefaultWorldInitialization.Initialize in Play Mode
    ([UUM-160220](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160220))

- Core Runtime: [CoreCLR] Editor crashes on RaiseFailFastException when an exception is thrown in an ISystem OnUpdate that runs without its Burst code
    ([UUM-160821](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160821))

- CoreCLR: Player crashes on quit and throws "TypeLoadException: Could not load type 'System.Object' from assembly 'mscorlib'" when a CoreCLR Player is built into a folder holding a Mono Player build
    ([UUM-161961](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161961))

- CoreCLR: Visual Scripting Graph window throws "VerificationException" when opening a Script Machine graph
    ([UUM-161656](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161656))

- Graphics: APV: Fixed an IndexOutOfRangeException in UpdateCellStreaming when a script called SceneManager.SetActiveScene and then ProbeReferenceVolume.SetActiveScene in the same frame.
    ([UUM-161251](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161251))    <br>*Fixed in 7000.0.0a21.*

- IL2CPP: Fixed a crash on quit when thread pool work was still running.
    (UUM-158593)    <br>*Fixed in 7000.0.0a21.*

- IL2CPP: Package up the new AOT artifacts for source code delivery
    (UUM-159570)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*    <br>*Fixed in 7000.0.0a21.*

- Mecanim Animation: Crash on UnityEngine::Animation::SetBoundCurveFloatValue when sampling an AnimationClip that has a Transform curve with an empty property name
    ([UUM-161382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161382))

- Multiplayer FPS/Third Person Templates: "contains a TextureImporter object at version 4, below the supported minimum (10)" warnings are thrown when creating a project from the Multiplayer Third Person Gameplay template
    ([UUM-156317](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156317))

- Multiplayer FPS/Third Person Templates: Compilation errors are thrown when creating a project from the "Multiplayer Third Person Gameplay" template after Netcode for GameObjects is upgraded to 3.0.0
    ([UUM-156276](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156276))

- Multiplayer FPS/Third Person Templates: Crash on memcpy when entering Play mode in a project with Multiplayer FPS template
    ([UUM-151778](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151778))

- Multiplayer FPS/Third Person Templates: First Person Multiplayer Template does not compile and prompts entering Safe mode on launch due to obsolete code being used
    ([UUM-156433](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156433))

- Multiplayer FPS/Third Person Templates: Multiplayer FPS Template has a continuous error loop when first opened on Linux
    ([UUM-148119](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148119))

- Serialization: Crash with multiple stack traces when managed serializer writes past the end of its V2ObjectFrame pool
    ([UUM-160644](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160644))

- Text (ATG): Crash on hb_font_t::get_glyph_extents when adding a line break to a new TextMeshPro text with "Advanced Text Generator" enabled
    ([UUM-162220](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-162220))

- Text (TextMeshPro): Crash on hb_font_t::get_glyph_extents when a new multi-line Text (TMP) with default settings uses the Advanced Text Generator
    ([UUM-162257](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-162257))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a NullReferenceException and the UI Builder window disappearing when docking or undocking it with unsaved changes.
    ([UUM-162236](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-162236))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a19.*    <br>*Fixed in 7000.0.0a21.*

- UI Toolkit Authoring Framework: Package Manager toolbar collapses and the sidebar "Project" menu overlaps the "+" button, making it inaccessible
    ([UUM-162043](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-162043))

- VFX Graph: Addressed issue where an open VFX Graph window could prevent that VFX from being built into MacOS Player builds.
    ([UUM-160418](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160418))    <br>*Fixed in 7000.0.0a21.*



### New 7000.0.0a20 Entries since 7000.0.0a16

#### Features

- Adaptive Performance: Added settings to the Device Simulator's Adaptive Performance section that simulate CPU and GPU utilization.

- Editor: Added a Search Online item to the Console context menus that searches the web for the log message or selected text, using the search engine set in Preferences &gt; General.

- Editor: Added support for the HelpURL attribute on structs, so data components with the attribute show a help button in the Inspector.

- Editor: Added the **Update Time Budget** preference which limits how long the Hierarchy window spends updating each frame. The default is 0.5 ms per update.

- Editor: New Sprite Mesh Module for Sprite Editor Window.

- Entities: Added an Entities Queries profiler module that shows the time spent in query calls, query creation, archetype matching and waiting for jobs, per call and per system. Also, the Entities Memory profiler module now shows archetype, query and query cache counts, and the memory used by queries.

- Entities: Data components can now use the Icon attribute to set the icon shown in the Inspector and the Add Component menu.

- IL2CPP: IOS, tvOS and visionOS builds using the Master C++ Compiler Configuration can now use link-time optimization, controlled by the IL2CPP LTO Mode Player Setting.

- Package Manager: Users on closed network will be able to export UPM packages without a signature.

- Profiler: Added Zstandard compression for saved Profiler captures, which makes .data files much smaller. To save captures that earlier versions of Unity can open, disable Compress saved captures in the Profiler Preferences.

- UI Toolkit: Added a pixel magnifier to the UI Toolkit Debugger that shows the selected element's rendered pixels at an integer zoom.

- VFX Graph: Added Sample Terrain Node to VFX Graph which simplifies the process of extracting height data from a terrain.



#### Improvements

- Adaptive Performance: The Adaptive Performance module in the Profiler now shows CPU utilization and GPU utilization.

- DX12: Reduced the render thread time of structured and raw buffers updated every frame with SetData, such as the URP Forward+ light clusters, by reusing their previous GPU resources instead of creating new ones.

- Editor: Improved Hierarchy window responsiveness in large scenes by spreading its update over multiple frames instead of completing it in a single blocking pass.
    (UUM-153593)

- Editor: Project Auditor: expose Finalizer warning to user code so Project Auditor can display it.

- Editor: Reduce the number of camera projections required to draw gizmos in scene view for rect transforms.

- Editor: Updated DefaultAsset &amp; ScriptableObject icons
    (UUM-135067)

- Editor: Use a ListView for the build profile callbacks component and display warning when build callback class is renamed or moved.

- Entities: Improved the Inspector display of FixedString and Unity.Entities.Hash128 fields, which are now shown as text instead of their raw serialized data.

- GI: Added a Ray Tracing Normal Bias setting to the Surface Cache GI volume override, which prevents dark artifacts when the rasterized geometry differs from the ray-traced geometry, for example with a coarser mesh LOD.
    (UUM-160232)

- GI: Surface Cache GI now uses the Mesh LOD for Global Illumination set on each Mesh Renderer instead of always using Mesh LOD 0.

- Graphics: Added a warning when a GraphicsBuffer is created with both GraphicsBuffer.Target.Raw and GraphicsBuffer.Target.Structured, because DirectX 11 and DirectX 12 can't bind such a buffer as a StructuredBuffer.
    (UUM-161113)

- Graphics: Added copy and paste support for Shader Constant Defines and Compiler Backend Settings in Shader Build Settings.

- Graphics: Added copy and paste support to Keyword Declaration Overrides in Shader Build Settings.

- Graphics: DirectX 12: Reduced frame hitches on some GPUs caused by re-creating command lists when the GPU falls a frame behind the CPU.
    (UUM-161319)

- Graphics: In the editor, when enabling Tile-Only Mode on a Universal Renderer Data asset with both built-in Post-processing and the On Tile Post Processing renderer feature enabled, an inspector error will show in addition to the existing console error.

- Graphics: On OpenGL ES and OpenGL, updating a GraphicsBuffer that is bound as a constant buffer no longer overwrites data the GPU may still be reading. This avoids render thread waits on some GPUs, such as PowerVR.

- Graphics: Stopped the render thread from freezing when the async upload buffer is corrupted, and logged an error in that case in release builds too.
    (UUM-150567)

- iOS: On iOS 27.1 and later, devices without a known notch entry, such as the iPhone Duo, now report the camera regions provided by iOS in `Screen.cutouts` instead of an empty list.

- iOS: Updated Swift API reference to link to low-level native plugin API docs.

- Kernel: Optimized CRC calculations to reduce time spent loading assets
    (UUM-147293)

- Kernel: Reduced copying of Collections and Mathematics structs, such as FixedList, AffineTransform and RigidTransform, when their non-mutating methods are called through in parameters, readonly fields or ref readonly references.
    ([UUM-124913](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-124913))

- Physics 2D: Reduced the code size that PhysicsCore2D adds to a build by approximately 80 KB.
    (UUM-161329)

- Prefabs: Improved the performance of Apply Selected in the Prefab Overrides window when several overrides are selected, as the Prefab is now saved and imported once.
    ([UUM-158666](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158666))

- Profiler: Improved the Jobs Profiler to label combine dependencies jobs as CombineDependencies instead of EngineJob.

- Profiler: Reduced the CPU cost of emitting profiler samples without metadata, such as native plug-in BeginSample and EndSample calls, while the Profiler is recording.
    (UUM-159025)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a5.*

- QNX: QNX builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- Serialization: Improved Editor performance when editing or applying changes to objects with many \[SerializeReference\] instances of the same type.
    (UUM-153230)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a3.*

- Shadergraph: Warn when a Screen Position node was found to erroneously be in Clip mode.
    ([UUM-159528](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159528))

- Shaders: Reduced memory usage when dynamic variant loading is enabled.

- Text: Reduced player build size by removing unused features from the bundled text libraries.

- TextMeshPro: Reduced memory spikes and CPU cost when dynamic TextMesh Pro font assets add many new characters at once, by uploading each atlas texture once instead of once per character.
    ([UUM-133781](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133781))

- UI Toolkit: Changing the Editor Font, Text Rendering Mode, or Text Generator preference now takes effect immediately instead of requiring an Editor restart.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the rendering performance of panels with many filtered elements: filter textures are now shared, and the same filter pass of many elements is drawn in one batch.
    (UUM-160498)

- UI Toolkit: Unity now reports a compile error when code replaces a visual element component that owns resources by assigning a whole new value over it, or copies a live one onto another element; remove the component and add a new one instead.

- URP: URP shaders declare their global shader variables in a constant buffer on every graphics API except OpenGL ES 3 and OpenGL Core.

- Web: Reduced Web build size by allowing the linker to strip Input System types that aren't used on the Web platform



#### API Changes

- 2D: Added: Added `ISpriteEditor.ShowNotice` and `ISpriteEditor.HideNotice`, which let Sprite Editor modules show dismissible notices in the Sprite Editor window.

- Adaptive Performance: Added: Added CpuUtilization and GpuUtilization properties to SimulatorAdaptivePerformanceSubsystem and SimulatorAdaptivePerformanceSubsystem.SimulatorProvider.

- Animation: Added: Introducing a new low-level Animation API in the Unity.Animation namespace. This changed the format of AnimationClip at runtime. Older player builds and asset bundles containing AnimationClips will still work but loading will be slower, they should be rebuilt in the last format to restore fast loading speed.

- Build Pipeline: Added: Added ContentDirectoryDiagnostics.GetContentDirectoryLoadInfo, which reports the content files in memory and the scenes loaded from a content directory in the Editor and development Players, and separates the holds that Unity keeps on root assets from those of game code. Added ContentLoadEditorUtility.TryGetSourceAssetGUID to find the source asset of each file.

- Editor: Added: Added SystemInfo.currentSwapChainPreTransform to expose UNITY_DISPLAY_ORIENTATION_PRETRANSFORM value
    (UUM-154867)

- Editor: Changed: `AssetPreview.GetMiniTypeThumbnail(Type)` now also returns icons for types that don't derive from UnityEngine.Object.

- Editor: Deprecated: Deprecated `DataMode`, `DataModeChangeEventArgs`, `IDataModeController` and `EditorWindow.dataModeController`. They will be removed in a future release.

- Entities: Added: Added GlobalEntityManager methods that read, write, add, remove, enable and list components by ComponentType, and report a component's change version, so code that only knows a component type at runtime can act on an entity in any world.

- Entities: Changed: `IconAttribute` can now be applied to structs.

- Entities Graphics: Added: Added the GlobalIlluminationMeshLod component, which selects the Mesh LOD level an entity uses for global illumination.

- GI: Changed: MeshRenderer.globalIlluminationMeshLod is now available in Players, not only in the Editor.

- Graphics: Added: Added GraphicsStateCollection.ComputeShaderVariant, AddComputeVariant, RemoveComputeVariant, ContainsComputeVariant, GetComputeVariants and computeVariantCount.

- Graphics: Deprecated: Camera and Light command buffers no longer execute when a scriptable render pipeline is active, including in the Scene view.

- Graphics: Deprecated: Deprecated the Camera and Light command buffer API \(AddCommandBuffer, AddCommandBufferAsync, RemoveCommandBuffer, RemoveCommandBuffers, RemoveAllCommandBuffers, GetCommandBuffers, commandBufferCount\) and the CameraEvent, LightEvent and ShadowMapPass enums, which only work with the Built-In Render Pipeline.

- Input System: Added: Added `Gamepad.gyroscope` and `Gamepad.accelerometer` to get the motion sensors that belong to a gamepad.

- iOS: Removed: Removed support for Intel simulators.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.ApplySnapshot", "PhysicsWorld.Create" and "PhysicsWorld.Clone" overloads with a "removeOwned" parameter that removes the owned bodies, shapes, chains and joints of the target world instead of refusing the snapshot.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.SceneLoadMode", "PhysicsWorld.sceneLoadMode" and "PhysicsCoreSettings2D.sceneLoadMode" to set whether a Single scene load destroys physics not owned by a DontDestroyOnLoad GameObject.

- Scripting: Deprecated: Deprecated `OSXStandalone.UserBuildSettings.architecture` and `OSArchitecture.x64ARM64`

- Shaders: Added: Shader.chunkEvictionStrategy can now be used to control how fast the unused shader variant chunks are unloaded.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added PanelSettings.filterTextureBudget, which sets the area of temporary textures that a panel can use at the same time to apply filters.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added PostProcessingPass.writeParametersCallback and FilterParameterWriter, so custom filter passes can write per-element parameters and be batched with GPU instancing.

- Visual Scripting: Deprecated: Deprecated AotStubWriter.GetStubStatements and MethodInfoStubWriter.operatorTypes in favor of the new AotStubWriter.GetStubStatementsCode.



#### Changes

- Asset Import: AssetImporterEditor.ApplyRevertButtonContainer now reports pending edits, including edits to extra data, through hasUnsavedChanges like ApplyRevertGUI, so a UI Toolkit importer editor that modifies its serializedObject in CreateInspectorGUI is reported as having unsaved changes.
    ([UUM-151732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151732))

- Asset Import: Projects that use AssetPostprocessors for textures, sprites, cameras, lights or scenes now reimport those assets once after upgrading, because the postprocessor dependency hash includes each postprocessor's assembly name.

- Asset Import: Reverting unapplied edits in an importer Inspector now also undoes import settings that a script changed and saved with AssetImporter.SaveAndReimport\(\) while those edits were pending.
    ([UUM-151732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151732))

- Documentation: Update iOS documentation about On Demand Resources limitations on iOS platform

- Editor: Changed the Editor Theme preference popup to cancel instead of Restart Later.
    (UUM-160024)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a15.*

- Editor: Meshes as part of the built in resources now come with colliders attached.

- Editor: Project Auditor: Removed Additional Insights from the Optimization Summary page.

- Editor: The Linux editor now requires OpenSSL \(either version 3.X or 4.X\) to be installed on the system. It is installed by default on all of our officially-supported distributions, so no action should be required for most users.

- EmbeddedLinux: GameWindow.MoveTo and window creation positions are now measured from the top-left corner of the display, the same origin as DisplayInfo.workArea, instead of from the top-left corner of the work area.

- Engine Diagnostics: Development IL2CPP builds for macOS, Windows, Linux, QNX and Embedded Linux no longer generate or upload crash report symbols when Engine Diagnostics is enabled, unless Cloud Diagnostics crash reporting is also enabled. This reduces build times.
    (UUM-159611)

- Graphics: Camera.layerCullSpherical no longer logged a warning when a Scriptable Render Pipeline was active, because Scriptable Render Pipelines already supported the property.

- Graphics: Changing the index format of a mesh through Mesh.indexFormat now resets Mesh LOD count to 1.
    ([UUM-159092](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159092))

- Graphics: GraphicsStateCollection.completedWarmupCount and isWarmedUp now include compute shader variants, so the warmup total is totalGraphicsStateCount plus computeVariantCount.

- Graphics: Player builds now fail with an error if any Render Pipeline Assets assigned in Graphic Settings or Quality Settings is missing.

- Input: A sensor whose controls are all noisy no longer becomes the current device of its type when another one is current. On Android devices with both rotation vectors, AttitudeSensor.current is the game rotation vector, because it is the only one with a control that isn't noisy.

- Input System: A Value action bound to a whole touch control, such as &lt;Touchscreen&gt;/primaryTouch, is now canceled when the touch ends instead of being performed with the Ended phase.

- Multiplayer: The Multiplayer Play Mode window now warns that it will be removed in Unity 7.1 and offers a one-click conversion of the current configuration to a Play Mode Scenario, with a link to the documentation.

- Package Manager: Changed the separator between the version and release date in the Package Manager details header from a dot to a vertical bar.

- Package Manager: Marked the WebGL Publisher package \(com.unity.connect.share\) as deprecated. Use Publish to Play in the Web Build Profile to upload Web builds to Unity Play.

- Prefabs: Changed Apply Selected in the Prefab Overrides window to apply either all selected overrides or none, and to show a dialog explaining why when they can't be applied.

- Prefabs: Changed PrefabUtility.prefabInstanceApplying and PrefabUtility.prefabInstanceApplied to be sent once per Apply Selected instead of once per applied override.

- Profiler: Changed "App Committed Memory" counter to return a sum of resident and swapped memory on Android
    (UUM-155041)

- Profiler: Changed the CPU Usage Profiler module to show the Jobs Profiler timeline by default. The previous timeline is available as Old Timeline \(Legacy\) in the view dropdown.

- Scene/Game View: Replaced the Game view Low Resolution Aspect Ratios toggle with a Pixel Size setting \(1x, 2x, 3x\) so pixel art stays sharp on fractional display scales. On high-DPI displays, Free Aspect and aspect-ratio sizes now default to whole-pixel scaling, which makes Screen.width and Screen.height smaller than before.

- Shaders: Surface shader support is now disabled.

- TextMeshPro: Marked the project-wide Use Advanced Text setting as Experimental and added a note that it applies to every TextMesh Pro component.
    (UUM-160511)

- uGUI: Changed Content Size Fitter in Clamped mode to keep a size larger than the content on a Horizontal or Vertical Layout Group with Control Child Size off, instead of shrinking the group to fit its children.
    ([UUM-159240](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159240))

- uGUI: Moved the UI/Lit shaders to the Universal Render Pipeline \(URP\) package. They now require URP. Existing materials switch to the URP versions automatically. The Refraction shaders need Opaque Texture enabled on the URP asset. UI/Lit doesn't support the 2D Renderer or HDRP.

- UI: Hide BiRP tab unless project uses it

- UI Toolkit: Made the UI Builder adopt workflows from the in-scene authoring, including the ability to set a PanelSettings asset while editing.

- XR: Added XR Foundation, a new package to replace XR Plug-in Management and XR Core Utils.



#### Fixes

- 2D: Fix crash when a Sprite is destroyed while it is being bound to a Sprite Atlas and notifying its users
    (UUM-156681)

- 2D: Fixed 2D Light shadows missing from a player built with `BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer` when the scene is not in the Build Profiles scene list and Strip Unused 2D Variants is on.
    ([UUM-160655](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160655))

- 2D: Fixed a Multiply 2D Light rendering brighter on OpenGLCore with NVIDIA GPUs when another 2D Light in view used the Additive Blend Style.
    ([UUM-161536](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161536))

- 2D: Fixed Freeform and Sprite lights that use a Light2D Shader Graph material rendering too dark under the Alpha Blend overlap operation when HDR Emulation Scale is above 1.
    ([UUM-161407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161407))

- 2D: Fixed inverted normal-map lighting on sprites rotated 180 degrees about the X or Y axis.
    (UUM-159916)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a1.*

- 2D: Fixed shadow capsules using each other's end radius when a shadow edge runs from right to left.
    ([UUM-161210](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161210))

- 2D: Fixed the Shadow Caster 2D shadow not matching a flipped Sprite Skin when bones are animated with GPU skinning.
    (UUM-159819)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a15.*

- 2D: Fixed Tilemap.DeleteCells shrinking the Tilemap bounds by one cell too many when deleting cells at the upper end of the bounds.
    ([UUM-158617](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158617))

- 2D: Fixed `Graphics.RenderSpriteInstanced` drawing the Sprite's own texture instead of a `_MainTex` set in the `MaterialPropertyBlock` of `RenderParams`.
    ([UUM-158701](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158701))

- 2D: Fixed `Graphics.RenderSprite` ignoring the `MaterialPropertyBlock` of `RenderParams` when a sprite mask interaction is set.

- 2D: Fixes instanced sprite rendering producing incorrect results when a custom `MaterialPropertyBlock` is applied to a `SpriteRenderer`.
    (UUM-147992)

- Adaptive Performance: fix the text overlapping when scrolling up the profile area.
    (UUM-152014)

- AI: Fixed NavWorld.GetAgentTypeIdForNode returning 0, the ID of the Humanoid agent type, instead of -1 for a null NavNode.
    ([UUM-160622](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160622))

- AI: Fixed safety checks on disposed `NavQueryBuffer` instances so that they always throw `ObjectDisposedException` and never throw `InvalidOperationException`.
    (UUM-161266)

- Android: Fixed Android build failure messages exposing sensitive environment variables. Now only build-related variables are shown.
    (UUM-157450)

- Android: Fixed Input.location sometimes reporting Running after Stop\(\) due to a late location update.
    (UUM-157808)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- Android: Fixed problem with Back gesture/button closing the app after returning from background when "Run In Background" and "Predictive Back Support" are both enabled.
    ([UUM-159613](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159613))

- Android: Fixed red and blue color channels being swapped in custom cursor textures set with Cursor.SetCursor on Android.
    ([UUM-159757](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159757))

- Android: Raised the Android NDK API level to 26 to match the minimum API level.
    (UUM-160285)

- Android: Update OpenJDK from 17.0.18+8 to 17.0.21+9, to fix the compromised eMudhra intermediate certificates.
    ([UUM-158390](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158390))

- Android: With GameActivity, a key typed while a script changes the input field text or selection is no longer lost.
    (UUM-155234)

- Android: With GameActivity, the on-screen keyboard is no longer reported as closed right after it opens.
    (UUM-158054)

- Android: \[Android\] Fixed a crash in PlayerPrefs.SetString that occurred when the Java heap was full or a JNI exception was pending on Unity 7.0.
    ([UUM-158759](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158759))

- Animation: Fixed a crash when destroying objects animated by a legacy Animation component after a clip was added to or removed from it while it was playing or had been sampled.
    ([UUM-161463](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161463))

- Animation: Fixed a crash when sampling an Animation Clip that has a key at the maximum float time on a GameObject with an Animator.
    ([UUM-159302](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159302))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where deleting sub-properties selected across multiple properties in the Animation window deleted only the last selected property.
    ([UUM-137365](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137365))

- Animation: Fixed Animation Rigging QuaternionExt.FromToRotation returning an identity rotation when the input vectors are very short.
    ([UUM-161377](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161377))

- Animation: Fixed Animation Rigging WeightedTransformArray.CopyTo overwriting the destination array and throwing IndexOutOfRangeException instead of ArgumentException when the destination is one element too short.
    ([UUM-161376](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161376))

- Animation: Fixed MultiReferentialConstraintData.driver throwing a NullReferenceException on default data and storing -1 when the source list is empty.
    ([UUM-161379](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161379))

- Animation: Fixed the Animation window Time Code fields reading a value such as 1.5 as 15 seconds when the operating system region uses a decimal comma.
    ([UUM-160200](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160200))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- Animation: Fixed WeightedTransformArray.RemoveAt not throwing an exception for an index at or past Count, which removed the last element or set Count to -1.
    ([UUM-161375](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161375))

- Asset Bundles: Fixed SceneManager.LoadScene not finding a scene in an AssetBundle when given a Packages/ path without the .unity extension.
    (UUM-160414)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a3.*

- Asset Import: Fixed an issue where changing GetVersion on an AssetPostprocessor did not reimport textures, sprites, cameras, lights or scenes when another AssetPostprocessor in a different assembly had the same full type name.
    (UUM-154761)

- Asset Import: Fixed the Block Asset Inspector showing block editing controls when Import Blocks are disabled.
    (UUM-155899)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a7.*

- Asset Import: Fixed the Inspector reporting unapplied import settings after a script changed importer settings and called AssetImporter.SaveAndReimport\(\) while the asset was inspected.
    ([UUM-151732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151732))

- Asset Import: Fixed the Model Importer Materials tab showing a blank Location field for models that use the obsolete external materials mode.
    ([UUM-161031](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161031))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash after a domain reload when a native importer replaced a ScriptedImporter for an asset.
    ([UUM-107764](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-107764))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash on Linux when the Editor reopened a project after restarting for a graphics API change.
    ([UUM-159784](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159784))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where all asset imports failed when the Editor fell back from Direct3D 12 to Direct3D 11 on an unsupported graphics device.
    ([UUM-146876](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146876))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where AssetDatabase.DeleteAsset, AssetDatabase.DeleteAssets and AssetDatabase.MoveAssetToTrash deleted the entire Assets folder, or another root folder, and returned true. These methods now refuse root folders and return false.
    ([UUM-159711](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159711))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where AssetDatabase.MoveAsset deleted the source asset when the destination path ended with a slash and named a folder that did not exist.
    ([UUM-159762](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159762))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where calling AssetDatabase.MoveAsset with "Assets/" as the destination path crashed the Editor.
    ([UUM-159303](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159303))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where deleting multiple assets left assets undeleted when an AssetModificationProcessor refused to delete one of them.
    ([UUM-155567](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155567))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash on every launch when a data file in the project's Library folder was damaged.
    (UUM-160491)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash on quit if an import worker message arrived during shutdown.
    (UUM-161371)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor quit with a "guid is uninitialized" fatal error when importing an asset with a missing script and an object reference that holds a malformed GUID.
    ([UUM-159779](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159779))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed the Editor quitting with a fatal error at startup when the project's Library/DataStore/uds.lock file is read-only.
    ([UUM-160173](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160173))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed the Import Activity window logging a warning when the restored layout selected an asset that is not in the project.

- Audio: Fixed a crash that could occur when an AudioClip in use as a scriptable audio generator was unloaded or destroyed, for example by AssetBundle.Unload.
    (UUM-156736)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- Audio: Fixed an issue where an Audio Source playing an Audio Random Container was silent while Time.timeScale was 0 if a Rigidbody was attached to the GameObject or one of its parents.
    ([UUM-159324](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159324))

- Audio: Fixed AudioClip.Create returning an empty clip that reported loadState Loaded, instead of throwing an ArgumentException, when the channel count exceeded the supported maximum.
    ([UUM-154941](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154941))

- Audio: Fixed AudioSource.isPlaying returning false while a one-shot or sample provider was playing on an AudioSource that had previously played an Audio Random Container.
    ([UUM-160362](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160362))

- Audio: Fixed AudioSource.isPlaying staying true, and the generator and its resources not being released, after a scriptable generator finished playing.
    ([UUM-144628](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/generators-on-audiosources-that-are-marked-as-finished-still-report-isplaying-equals-true))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed a full player data rebuild when Development Build or another platform setting was changed in an active custom build profile.
    ([UUM-156904](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156904))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Build Analysis window expanded all nested builds again after the window was reopened or scripts were reloaded.
    (UUM-158840)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Build Profiles window reported a multi-target platform's SDK package as not installed after the Editor started or scripts recompiled.

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the first row of the Asset Table in the Build Analysis window covered the column header, so columns could not be sorted.
    ([UUM-161104](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161104))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- Build Pipeline: Fixed Build Steps columns being cut off in the Build Analysis window.
    (UUM-158263)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed BuildReportSummary.json listing inactive build options, such as Il2CPP, in the BuildOptions array.
    ([UUM-150768](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150768))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed LOD stripping in Dedicated Server content directory builds using the Standalone quality levels instead of the Server quality levels.
    (UUM-161178)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed reference rows in the Build Analysis window's Assets tab showing no tooltip after scrolling.
    ([UUM-160227](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160227))

- Build System: Avoid BuildProgram hang when trying to use https:// in a local path string
    ([UUM-131187](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131187))

- Burst: Fixed "error: undefined symbol: strlen" linker error
    ([UUM-161029](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161029))

- Burst: Fixed incorrect struct size calculation for structs using StructLayoutAttribute.Pack with an under-aligned field
    ([UUM-143671](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143671))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a17.*

- Burst: Fixed that on weakly ordered memory architectures like ARM64, Burst now compiles volatile reads and writes to have acquire/release semantics
    (UUM-159683)

- Cache Server: Content Directory artifacts uploaded to the Accelerator now include the blobs they depend on. Fixes errors like `ContentFileMetadataCollection failed to load content for CAH <hash>`.
    (UUM-158843)

- Compute: Compute shaders now check for target and unrecognized pragma directives.
    ([UUM-159100](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159100))

- CoreCLR: Fixed missing script method frames in Debug.Log and uncaught exception stack traces in Release builds on CoreCLR.
    ([UUM-159945](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159945))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- CoreCLR: Updated the network statistics implementation and fixed a potential infinite loop when querying active network connections.

- DX12: Fixed a crash in batch mode when rendering a camera to the screen.
    (UUM-159254)

- DX12: Fixed a crash in the Unity Graphics Kernel D3D12 backend when graphics device creation failed.
    (UUM-160849)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- DX12: Fixed an Editor crash when capturing an editor window that uses MSAA, such as the Inspector.
    (UUM-160893)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where constant buffers used by the SRP Batcher could be released while the GPU still used them, which caused a crash when the D3D12 debug layer was enabled.
    (UUM-159253)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where GraphicsStateCollection warm-up created pipelines that did not match the draws, so those pipelines were compiled again at first draw. This affected meshes that lack a texcoord the shader reads, and blend states first used during warm-up with multithreaded rendering.
    (UUM-161348)

- DX12: Fixed D3D12 debug layer render pass errors \(9:1372 and 9:1373\) on the depth-stencil buffer when entering Play Mode with graphics jobs enabled.
    (UUM-160209)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- DX12: Fixed Direct3D 12 scenes progressively rendering as a gray void when native render passes are disabled, for example when running under Nsight Graphics.
    (UUM-160040)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a13.*

- Editor: Continue per-profile post-build callbacks after an exception outside strict mode.
    ([UUM-159550](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159550))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- Editor: DefaultAsset &amp; ScriptableObject icons now scale correctly at maximum size in Project window

- Editor: Deprecate OnPostRender\(\)

- Editor: Deprecate OnPreCull\(\)

- Editor: Deprecate OnPreRender\(\)

- Editor: Deprecate OnRenderImage\(\)

    ([UUM-136222](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136222))

- Editor: Fixed a "FormatVariableName can only be called from the main thread" exception when ObjectNames.NicifyVariableName was called from a background thread, such as during Project Auditor analysis.

    (UUM-160115)

- Editor: Fixed a crash when opening Help &gt; Report a Bug or exiting the Editor in a project with an EditorApplication.delayCall callback declared in a complex generic type.
    ([UUM-122928](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-122928))

- Editor: Fixed AddComponentMenu order not being used
    ([UUM-112973](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-112973))

- Editor: Fixed an ArgumentNullException that could be logged when packages were registered, for example when adding a macOS Server build profile.
    ([UUM-155583](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155583))

- Editor: Fixed an intermittent crash when quitting the Editor on macOS.
    ([UUM-160348](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160348))

    (UUM-158277)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- Editor: Fixed an issue where clicking a GameObject near the bottom of the Hierarchy window selected the GameObject above it when the display scale was set to a non-standard value such as 110%.
    ([UUM-156214](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156214))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where GameObjects hidden with HideFlags.HideInHierarchy could still be found and selected using the Hierarchy window search.
    ([UUM-137816](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137816))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor code analysis failed or stopped responding with the MSBuild compilation pipeline when scripts had changed since the last compilation.
    (UUM-159022)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor ignored AnalysisParams.AssemblyNames when using the MSBuild compilation pipeline, compiling and reporting every assembly instead of only the requested ones.
    (UUM-152964)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor reported no Migrate to CoreCLR issues when some assemblies failed to compile, for example in projects that use -warnaserror+.
    (UUM-156486)

- Editor: Fixed an issue where renaming a Play Mode Scenario to a name with characters that are not valid in file names, or with slashes, reverted the name without logging an error.
    ([UUM-155574](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155574))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where the previous build profile's Player Settings scripting define symbols stayed active after switching to another build profile for the same platform.
    ([UUM-159541](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159541))

- Editor: Fixed Editor preferences on Linux and macOS sometimes reverting to earlier values while asset import worker processes were running.

- Editor: Fixed issue where calling EditorWindow.docked during InitializeOnLoad, InitializeOnLoadMethod or OnCodeInitializing would always return false, regardless of the window's docked state
    (UUM-139988)

- Editor: Fixed issue where calling EditorWindow.GetWindow during InitializeOnLoad, InitializeOnLoadMethod or OnCodeInitializing would result in duplicate windows being created
    (UUM-139988)

- Editor: Fixed issue where MissingReferenceException is thrown when the Inspector preview's target is destroyed.
    ([UUM-161489](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161489))

- Editor: Fixed issues with the Sprite Editor and the Light theme. Fixed the Sprite Outline Tool overlay panel being clipped in the Northstar theme. Fixed styling issue in the Tilemap Editor.

    *First seen in 7000.0.0a13.*

- Editor: Fixed material multi-selection having UITK shader.
    ([UUM-161854](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161854))

- Editor: Fixed playmode exit callback not triggering when a mid play session domain reload is set to exit playmode.
    (UUM-141180)

- Editor: Fixed the gradient checker pattern so its no longer stretched.
    ([UUM-161441](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161441))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- Editor: Fixed the Project Auditor home page not showing a scrollbar in short or docked windows, which could hide the panel prompting to install the Rules package.
    ([UUM-161560](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161560))

- Editor: Graphics: Fixed an issue where depth-sorted entities rendered with Entities Graphics could disappear, or render with another entity's position, on Metal while an entity with an empty mesh was present. \(UUM-159040\)

- Editor: Hierarchy: Fixed an error "HierarchyNode not found" when dropping an asset between items while the Hierarchy window is updating.
    (UUM-161561)

- Editor: Hierarchy: Fixed an issue where a GameObject dropped between two others in the Hierarchy window moved to the bottom of the list when its parent or scene contained a hidden GameObject.

- Editor: Internal Unity and UnityEngine data components no longer show up in the component menu for GameObjects.
    ([UUM-160377](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160377))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- Editor: Prevent safe area objects from being manipulated by scene view gizmos
    ([UUM-144066](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144066))

- Editor: Project Auditor: correctly flag relevant issues as being Performance Critical when analyzing with MSBuild enabled.
    (UUM-161426)

- Editor: Project Auditor: fix plural in Upgrade UI
    (UUM-161498)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Fixed the Project Auditor window selecting a different issue and collapsing its group after the first domain reload following a new analysis.
    ([UUM-161504](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161504))

- Editor: Project Auditor: implement Inverted Call Hierarchies when analyzing with MSBuild enabled.
    (UUM-161425)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed an issue where Vulkan windows flickered black on Wayland compositors such as Weston on Embedded Linux.
    (UUM-153585)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed GameWindow.GetPosition returning a global position instead of the position relative to the window's display that GameWindow.MoveTo uses.
    (UUM-159872)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed GameWindow.MoveTo reporting a position change and returning the requested position from GetPosition on Wayland, where the window cannot be moved.
    (UUM-160221)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed OpenGL ES fullscreen windows stretching the image instead of letterboxing it when the resolution aspect ratio differs from the display.<br>
    Fixed Vulkan fullscreen windows below the display resolution not being upscaled to fill the display on Wayland.<br>
    Fixed mouse, touch and IMGUI positions being offset from the image in letterboxed fullscreen windows.
    (UUM-160897)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed a crash on Android when an Engine Diagnostics crash report upload failed.
    (UUM-162109)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed an issue where the Build Profiles window kept showing the previous Diagnostic Data Collection value after a script changed EngineDiagnosticsSettings.enabled.
    ([UUM-160174](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160174))

- Entities: Setting an Entity name to an empty string didn't work.
    ([UUM-161430](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161430))

- GI: Fixed a NullReferenceException and Render Graph execution errors when baking a Reflection Probe with the Screen Space Reflections renderer feature enabled and Opaque Texture disabled.
    ([UUM-159262](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159262))

- GI: Fixed a NullReferenceException when generating lighting for an Adaptive Probe Volume Baking Set after unloading one of its scenes.
    ([UUM-159309](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159309))

- GI: Fixed a typo in the Probe Volume Disable Streaming Assets tooltip in Graphics settings, where Addressables was misspelled.
    ([UUM-161558](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161558))

- GI: Fixed an issue where clicking Bake in the Inspector with multiple Reflection Probes selected only baked the first probe.
    ([UUM-159701](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159701))

- GI: Fixed out-of-process light baking on Apple silicon requiring Rosetta 2, by building the LightBaker helper natively for arm64.
    (UUM-159695)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- GI: Fixed probe occlusion that could bake incorrectly on macOS with the Unity Compute light baker.
    ([UUM-160178](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160178))

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache GI ignoring per-instance material overrides from MaterialPropertyBlock and Entities Graphics material property components, so instances that share a material now bounce and emit light with their own override colors.
    (UUM-160842)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache GI rejecting some unobstructed bounce light from punctual lights near triangle edges, which caused slight darkening.
    (UUM-160166)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed breadcrumbs that kept the old name after the subgraph node used to open them was renamed.
    ([UUM-141984](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141984))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed condition and transition icon overrides set from a visualization context disappearing from the Graph Inspector after the selection changed.
    (UUM-151358)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed Graph Toolkit Error Notifications overlay navigation arrows being barely visible in the light Editor theme.
    (UUM-160107)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a15.*

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the nested fields of a connected expandable port remaining editable in the Inspector.
    (UUM-157441)

- Graphics: Android: Fixed the sky and indirect lighting rendering magenta with the Unity Graphics Kernel on Vulkan devices that do not support HDR ASTC textures, such as Samsung Xclipse and PowerVR Rogue GPUs.
    (UUM-159653)

- Graphics: Avoid crashing when uploading skinned mesh buffers to the GPU fails using GPU Batched skinning mode. The upload failure now results in a clear error message and skinned meshes may render incorrectly each frame the failure occurs.
    (UUM-141190)

- Graphics: Component Activation/Deactivation tracked by `ObjectDispatcher`
    ([UUM-145787](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145787))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash in CommandBuffer.SetComputeConstantBufferParam and CommandBuffer.SetRayTracingConstantBufferParam when the buffer is null. A null buffer now removes the constant buffer that was set on the compute or ray tracing shader.

- Graphics: Fixed a crash in Mesh.Optimize when a submesh was set with MeshUpdateFlags.DontRecalculateBounds and its vertex range did not cover its indices.
    ([UUM-159311](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159311))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash on Metal with UnityGraphicsKernel when rendering into all slices of a 3D render texture.
    (UUM-157481)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a11.*

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when calling Mesh.GetIndices, Mesh.SetIndices or Mesh.SetTriangles after changing Mesh.indexFormat on a mesh with Mesh LODs. Changing Mesh.indexFormat now also resets Mesh.lodCount to one.
    ([UUM-159092](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159092))

- Graphics: Fixed a GPU hang that left the screen black when rendering with the Unity Graphics Kernel on Vulkan on Samsung Xclipse GPUs with Vulkan 1.1 drivers.
    (UUM-159650)

- Graphics: Fixed a player crash on quit when cache miss tracing was enabled and a scene had been loaded.
    ([UUM-154784](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154784))

- Graphics: Fixed a potential freeze or crash during scene loads when crunch-compressed cubemaps stream in at half-resolution or lower.
    (UUM-150567)

- Graphics: Fixed a rare memory corruption in the Direct3D 12 renderer when warming up a GraphicsStateCollection.
    (UUM-153131)

- Graphics: Fixed an assertion error logged to the Console when loading RenderDoc in the Editor with the Direct3D 12 graphics API.
    (UUM-115796)

- Graphics: Fixed an issue on DirectX 12 and Metal where calling ClearRenderTarget inside a render pass replaced custom view and projection matrices set with SetGlobalMatrix, such as those set by RenderingUtils.SetViewAndProjectionMatrices in URP.
    ([UUM-138530](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138530))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue on OpenGL ES and WebGL where a constant buffer bound from script with SetConstantBuffer or SetGlobalConstantBuffer stayed bound for later draws that don't bind it, which then read stale data or, on WebGL, were skipped.

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where warming up a GraphicsStateCollection created with GraphicsState.SetMeshData for a skinned mesh created a pipeline that didn't match the draw, so the pipeline was still created at first draw on Vulkan.
    (UUM-156721)

- Graphics: Fixed ARGB4444 render textures falling back to RGBA8 with the UGK graphics device.
    (UUM-158640)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a11.*

- Graphics: Fixed crash in `GraphicsCaps:IsFormatSupported` when creating a RenderTexture with an out-of-range RenderTextureFormat value.
    ([UUM-158897](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158897))

- Graphics: Fixed DirectX 12 validation errors and possible loss of render target contents in merged native render passes, such as URP Deferred, when not using native graphics jobs, including when a native plugin event flushes GPU work inside the render pass.
    (UUM-157002)

- Graphics: Fixed GraphicsStateCollection.cacheMissCollection becoming null and cache miss tracing stopping after Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets was called.
    (UUM-161021)

- Graphics: Fixed Mesh.CombineMeshes copying the wrong vertices and writing out-of-range indices when a source sub-mesh uses a non-zero baseVertex.
    ([UUM-161368](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161368))

- Graphics: Fixed multi-second stall on UI thread when backgrounding an app with Vulkan, caused by surface not being released and surfaceDestroyed waiting on the engine callbacks.
    ([UUM-149425](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149425))

- Graphics: Fixed offscreen framebuffer resolution being updated late by one frame on device rotation with OpenGLES.
    ([UUM-157974](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157974))

- Graphics: Fixed render graph errors on Vulkan when a memoryless render texture, such as the XR eye depth with Optimize Buffer Discards enabled, is used across several native render passes.
    (UUM-159183)

- Graphics: Fixed Shader Constant Defines UI ignoring first click.
    ([UUM-161026](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161026))

- Graphics: Fixed the image rendering upside down in XR with Tile-Only Mode and MSAA.
    ([UUM-158825](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158825))

- Graphics: Fixed ugEncClearRenderTargets clearing only the first slice of layered render passes on Metal with UnityGraphicsKernel.
    (UUM-157481)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a11.*

- Graphics: Fixes a crash in the Windows player with the Vulkan backend when the window is minimized.
    (UUM-160527)

- Graphics: Metal now correctly used mip_filter::nearest for inline samplers with point filtering
    (UUM-160286)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- Graphics: set inputPrimitiveTopology when creating MTLRenderPipelineState
    (UUM-160241)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- Graphics: SetVertices\(\) no longer causes a crash if the provided start and length arguments are large enough to cause an integer overflow when added together.
    ([UUM-154564](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154564))

- Graphics: SkinnedMeshRenderers added through scripting with UpdateWhenOffscreen enabled no longer calculate their bounds on the main thread every frame.

- HDRP: Six-way smoke lit particles now receive shadows cast by volumetric clouds
    ([UUM-159322](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159322))

- IL2CPP: .Equals and .GetHashCode now throw a NotSupportedException for InlineArray types

- IL2CPP: Fixed a crash on exit that could occur in development players with script debugging enabled.
    (UUM-155863)

- IL2CPP: Fixed incorrect results from type checks, casts and GetType on boxed values in shared generic code.

- Input System: Fix layout merge for controls declared for several variants.
    ([UUM-160829](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160829))

- Input System: Fixed a Hold interaction on a touch control, such as &lt;Touchscreen&gt;/primaryTouch, never being canceled or performed again after the first touch.
    ([UUM-144330](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144330))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a19.*

- Input System: Fixed an InputEventTrace with a fixed size dropping an extra event and miscounting its size when it wrapped over events whose size is not a multiple of 4 bytes, such as mouse state events.
    (UUM-159846)

- Input System: Fixed an issue on Windows where clicking or dragging the title bar to refocus a windowed Player registered as a left mouse click in the game.
    ([UUM-149740](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149740))

- Input System: Fixed InputControlList&lt;T&gt;.AddRange and InputControlList&lt;T&gt;.AddSlice copying between overlapping memory ranges when inserting controls before the end of the list.
    (UUM-159837)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a15.*

- Input System: Fixed InputSystem.onDeviceCommand subscriptions being ignored after any callback handled a device command.
    (UUM-160201)

- Input System: Fixed key names showing garbled characters instead of accented letters with the French \(Standard, AZERTY\) keyboard layout on Windows 11 version 24H2 and later.
    ([UUM-144382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144382))

- Input System: Fixed Keyboard.anyKey.wasPressedThisFrame not returning true when a key is pressed while another key is held, which means it can now be true several times without wasReleasedThisFrame in between.
    ([UUM-144317](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144317))

- Input System: Fixed sample tests compiling without the Test Framework package.
    ([UUM-161742](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161742))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a17.*

- Input System: Fixed the context menu shown when right-clicking an action map, action or composite being renamed in the Input Actions editor, which now only shows Cut, Copy and Paste.
    (UUM-160353)

- Input System: Fixed the Processors list for a composite part binding, such as the W key in a WASD composite, offering processors for the composite's value type \(Vector2\) instead of the part's value type \(float\).
    ([UUM-144319](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144319))

- Input System: Fixed UI Toolkit buttons staying pressed when the application lost focus while the mouse button was held.
    ([UUM-160160](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160160))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- Input System: Fixed UI Toolkit pointer events reporting Shift as pressed after typing a capital letter in the Device Simulator on macOS.
    ([UUM-156797](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156797))

- iOS: Fixed a crash in `UnityCheckUnloadAndQuit` when a batch-mode player quits while its host process keeps running, for example in an XCTest with a quit handler or in Unity as a Library.
    (UUM-159471)

- Kernel: Fixed a crash when an OnTransformParentChanged callback destroyed the GameObject's previous parent.
    ([UUM-160647](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160647))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- Kernel: Fixed a crash when JobsUtility.CreateJobReflectionData was called with a null Type. An ArgumentNullException is now thrown instead.
    ([UUM-150954](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150954))

- Kernel: Fixed a rare crash in the Editor on Windows when freeing memory during Asset Database startup.
    ([UUM-159502](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159502))

- Kernel: Fixed file writes reporting success when fewer bytes than requested were written, for example when the disk is full.
    (UUM-153322)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a3.*

- Kernel: Fixed FixedString CopyFrom clearing the destination string when it returns CopyError.Truncation.
    ([UUM-158961](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158961))

- Kernel: Fixed InvalidOperationException when calling SortJob on a NativeArray that a scheduled job is still writing to.
    ([UUM-158989](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158989))

- Kernel: Fixed NativeText.Substring\(int, AllocatorManager.AllocatorHandle\) ignoring the allocator argument and allocating the result with the source string's allocator.
    (UUM-159919)

- Kernel: Fixed Unity.Mathematics math.mul\(AffineTransform, float3x3\) multiplying the matrices in the wrong order. The result now matches math.mul\(a, new AffineTransform\(b\)\). Code that relied on the old result may see different output if the matrix does not commute with the AffineTransform rotation.
    ([UUM-158609](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158609))

- Kernel: pnoise, psrnoise and psrdnoise now repeat with the set period when the coordinates are negative
    ([UUM-158606](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158606))

- Kernel: Quaternion math.exp, math.unitexp, math.log and math.unitlog no longer return NaN when the vector part is zero.
    ([UUM-157957](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157957))

- Linux: Fixed a crash on Linux when the window manager closed the Editor launch screen during project load.
    ([UUM-132175](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-crash-on-editorlaunchscreen-platformeditorlaunchscreen-setprogress-when-opening-a-project))

- Linux: Fixed intermittent crash in LinuxEditor when switching focus between Editor windows and other applications.
    ([UUM-161324](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161324))

- macOS: Fixed an issue where an exported Xcode project failed to build at the code signing step when Burst debug symbols were included.
    (UUM-156483)

- macOS: Fixed an issue where background Editor windows flickered while a modal dialog was open.
    (UUM-160349)

- macOS: Fixed an issue where dragging the edge or corner of an Editor window on macOS 27 sometimes did not resize it.
    (UUM-160497)

- Multiplayer: Fixed an "Unknown pseudo class" warning being logged in the Console when opening the Multiplayer Center window.
    ([UUM-160252](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160252))

- Multiplayer: Fixed an error on Windows where Multiplayer Play Mode could fail to save player settings, such as tags, while additional Editor instances were running, because SystemData.json was in use by another process.
    (UUM-155061)

- Multiplayer: Fixed an issue where the Play Mode Scenarios window kept showing an Additional Editor Instance as active after it stopped running.
    ([UUM-150414](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150414))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Active Scenario window warning for an invalid scenario referring to an "Edit Configuration" button instead of the "Edit Scenario" button.
    ([UUM-155747](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155747))

- Multiplayer: Multiplayer Play Mode clone Editors didn't reload an open scene after an external tool modified the scene file on disk.

- Multiplayer: Multiplayer Play Mode: Fixed the Unity Netcode Play Mode Tools layout option not opening the Play Mode Tools window, in Virtual Players for Netcode 7.0+.
    (UUM-155623)

- N/A \(internal\): Engine Diagnostics: The Editor now saves the latest build settings answer from the remote settings service, encrypted, to Library/Insights/build-settings.bin. It reuses that answer for 24 hours while the service URL, project ID and Editor version stay the same, instead of fetching it again. Android and iOS builds fall back to it when offline, or before the first fetch of a session completes. \(JIRA-ID\)'.

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed the input gathering and GhostOwner code samples in the Netcode for Entities manual throwing an InvalidOperationException every frame when a client connects to a single-world host.
    ([UUM-159972](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159972))

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed the Netcode for Entities documentation to explain that on a single-world host, the SimulationSystemGroup updates once per frame and only the PredictedSimulationSystemGroup updates at the server tick rate.
    ([UUM-159940](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159940))

- Networking: Fixed an issue where the Web Requests Profiler module neither listed some requests nor counted them as dropped when more than 1024 requests were in flight at once.
    ([UUM-159267](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159267))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- Networking: Unity Transport: Fix a stack corruption vulnerability in the WebSocket code.
    (UUM-160664)

- Networking: Unity Transport: Fix out-of-bound memory accesses after receiving an invalid handshake message.
    (UUM-160664)

- Package Manager: Fix nested asset packages from trusted upm packages show untrusted popup \(UUM-159886\)
    (UUM-159886)

- Package Manager: Fixed the issue where non discoverable packages show up in Unity Registry page incorrectly.
    (UUM-159783)

- Package Manager: Fixed the issue where unity lifecycle deprecation information was not shown correctly in the Package Manager window.
    (UUM-155435)

- Particles: Fix issue where mesh bounds were not updating.
    ([UUM-160229](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160229))

- Particles: No longer ignores custom data during particle prewarm.
    ([UUM-160600](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160600))

- Physics: Fixed a typo in the Broadphase Type tooltip in Project Settings &gt; Physics.
    ([UUM-161553](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161553))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where enabling Bake Convex Collision Mesh or Bake Triangle Collision Mesh in the Inspector for a Mesh asset was discarded after changing the selection.
    ([UUM-160148](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160148))

- Physics: Fixed the "Solver Velocity Iterations" label being cut off in the Info tab of the Physics Debugger window.
    ([UUM-161716](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161716))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsDrawOverride.changed" losing its handlers when entering or exiting Play mode, which stopped Editor tooling such as the 2D physics editing tools from following later changes to the Editor draw override.

- Physics 2D: Fixed 2D physics drawing still showing in Scene and Game views with Gizmos turned off after exiting Play mode, when the 2D physics draw override has "Hide With Gizmos" selected.

- Physics 2D: Fixed a "PolygonGeometry" getting smaller and moving each time its Shape is changed in the Inspector.
    (UUM-160158)

- Physics 2D: Fixed an assertion when calling "Physics2D.CircleCast" with a NaN radius; 2D physics queries now log a warning and return no results when given a NaN argument, or an infinite argument other than a distance.
    ([UUM-161335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161335))

- Physics 2D: Fixed contact events in 2D Physics Core reporting a group pair's begin as the first and its end as the last when one of its contacts stopped touching as another started touching in the same step.
    (UUM-161120)

- Physics 2D: Fixed fields of the same 2D physics type, such as list elements, sharing one expanded state in the Inspector.

- Physics 2D: Fixed handlers added to the "PhysicsEvents" static events by code that had since been unloaded still being called after a code reload.

- Physics 2D: Fixed physics callbacks being sent to a destroyed MonoBehaviour set as a "callbackTarget" or "transformWriteCallbackTarget", which threw a "MissingReferenceException".
    (UUM-160675)

- Physics 2D: Fixed physics owned by a DontDestroyOnLoad GameObject being destroyed when a scene was loaded with "LoadSceneMode.Single".
    (UUM-160675)

- Physics 2D: Fixed text typed into the "PolygonGeometry" size and offset fields, and into physics layer names, being overwritten while typing.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PhysicsRotate" field, the "PhysicsUserData" object field and the 32-bit layer mask fields in the Inspector missing the mixed value, prefab override styling or context menu when several objects are selected.
    ([UUM-161477](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161477))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PolygonGeometry" property drawer throwing an exception and the Inspector stopping drawing the component when the vertex count is outside the range a polygon allows. A warning is now shown instead.
    ([UUM-160184](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160184))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the IMGUI "PhysicsUserData" drawer not showing its "Vector3Int" field, and IMGUI 2D physics drawers marking every field of a struct as overridden when only one field was.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the layer masks of "PhysicsShape.ContactFilter" and "PhysicsQuery.QueryFilter" showing a mixed value when only another field of the filter differed, and Revert on a mask reverting the whole filter.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the owner and user data of PhysicsCore2D bodies, shapes, chains and joints being lost when a world snapshot is applied, or when a world is created or cloned from one.
    ([UUM-159887](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159887))

- Prefabs: Fixed a crash when accessing a VFX Renderer after undoing a Visual Effect component removal that was applied to a Prefab.
    (UUM-155270)

- Prefabs: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory access and possible data corruption when merging prefab instances whose source objects have missing scripts and were saved by very old Unity versions.
    (UUM-159827)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a15.*

- Profiler: Sum float/double ProfilerRecorder samples correctly.
    ([UUM-161095](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161095))

- QNX: Fixed GameWindow.GetPosition not returning the position passed to GameWindow.MoveTo, and a window wider than its display being reported as on the next display.
    (UUM-159872)

- Services: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when RemoteConfigSettings raised its Updated event in the CoreCLR Editor.
    (UUM-160422)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- Services: Fixed the Organization and Cloud project dropdowns in Project Settings &gt; Services opening away from their fields on Windows when monitors use different display scales.

- Shadergraph: Fixed "Missing vertex input" errors logged on Metal when a UI Shader Graph that reads UV1, UV2 or UV3 is open in the Shader Graph window.
    (UUM-158525)

- Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where an empty reflected function body could be mishandled.
    ([UUM-161374](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161374))

- Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where materials lost their property values when using Shader Graphs last saved before Shader Graph 5.1 that have properties without a reference name. Values set on such materials while using 7000.0.0a11 or earlier 7000.0 alphas might need to be set again.
    (UUM-153642)

- Shaders: **XR/Vulkan/DXC:** Fixed multiview shaders failing to compile or bind correctly when using the DXC shader compiler on Vulkan. The view index is now read from the SPIR-V ViewIndex builtin, so existing shaders work without changes.

- Shaders: Fixed a crash when executing a command buffer whose compute or ray tracing shader was destroyed after commands such as `CommandBuffer.SetKeyword` or `CommandBuffer.DispatchCompute` were recorded.
    ([UUM-161535](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161535))

- Shaders: Fixed an assertion failure when calling Shader.SetGlobalVectorArray with an invalid property ID that was previously used with a different property type.
    ([UUM-161397](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161397))

- Shaders: Fixed an Editor crash on Metal when a shader pass raised its target for a fragment-only keyword \(for example \#pragma target 5.0 with a keyword\) and the GPU Resident Drawer was enabled.
    (UUM-156494)

- Shaders: Fixed an Editor crash when importing a shader file with CR-only line endings that contains a block comment.
    (UUM-159807)

- Shaders: Fixed frame time spikes during scene load caused by shadow caster shader variants being compiled before they were drawn.
    ([UUM-139592](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139592))

- Shaders: Shader_feature are not overridden to dynamic_branch anymore in fast build mode to avoid hitting resource limits with the shader. Added also possibility to disable fast build mode per shader in the shader inspector.
    ([UUM-157180](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157180))

- SRP Core: Fixed DynamicArray.Insert throwing an ArgumentException and corrupting the array when the array was full or had only one free slot.
    ([UUM-159071](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159071))

- SRP Core: Fixed the Rendering Debugger Volume panel showing no volume data when the camera's Volume Trigger is a different GameObject than the camera.
    ([UUM-160872](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160872))

- SRP Core: Fixed use of incorrect API define for DX12 in Common.hlsl. Incorrect SHADER_API_D3D12 to correct SHADER_API_DX12.
    (UUM-159608)

- SRP Core: Fixed `TextureCurve.Evaluate` and `TextureCurve.GetTexture` throwing a `NullReferenceException` for a looping curve that was loaded, cloned or deserialized.
    ([UUM-161213](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161213))

- Terrain: Fixed brush Opacity showing 0.2 to 100 in the Terrain Inspector while the Scene view Brush Attributes overlay shows 0 to 1.
    (UUM-159193)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a17.*

- Terrain: Fixed the Edit Tree window keeping the "Tree has already been selected as a prototype" error and a disabled Apply button after the conflicting tree was removed.
    ([UUM-158698](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158698))

- Terrain: Fixed the scene being marked as modified when the Edit Tree window was opened and closed without making changes.
    ([UUM-158703](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158703))

- Tests: Reenabled fixed gfx-tests
    (UUM-158639)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a11.*

- Tests: Reenabled fixed gfx-tests
    (UUM-158643)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a11.*

- Text: Fixed a missing word in the error message logged when importing a font file whose format is unsupported.
    ([UUM-161481](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161481))

- Text: Fixed Chinese characters not rendering in legacy text on iOS 18.4 and later.
    ([UUM-121660](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-121660))

- Text: Fixed slightly blurry glyphs for font assets using the COLOR_HINTED render mode with the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextCore: Fixed a trailing line break eliding the previous line with Ellipsis overflow.
    (UUM-160446)

- TextCore: Fixed text without a line break opportunity wrapping at punctuation instead of at the character that overflows.
    (UUM-160445)

- TextCore: Fixed the &lt;space&gt; rich text tag being ignored and stripped from the text when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160487)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed an IndexOutOfRangeException thrown continuously by the font asset Inspector when an older font asset stores an invalid Atlas Render Mode value.
    ([UUM-150239](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150239))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed an issue where searching for "ATG" in Project Settings didn't find the TextMesh Pro page with the Advanced Text Generator settings.

    *First seen in 7000.0.0a14.*

- TextMeshPro: Fixed centered and right aligned synthetic bold text being shifted by the extra advance of the last character of each line when using Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed GetPreferredValues measuring a single line when given an infinite height when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed Max Visible Characters, Max Visible Words and Max Visible Lines being ignored, and TMP_TextInfo.wordInfo not being filled, when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed synthetic bold text being clipped when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the Baseline, Midline and Capline vertical alignments being laid out as Middle when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the empty line after a final line break counting in the text height when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the Sprite Asset Inspector glyph preview not updating while editing the Glyph Rect values of a sprite character.
