    ([UUM-159525](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159525))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the End key moving the caret to the start of the next line in multiline text fields that use the Advanced Text Generator.
    ([UUM-157916](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157916))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the Filter Texel Size node of UI Toolkit filters in Shader Graph, which returned a size that included the filter margins instead of the size of the filtered area.
    ([UUM-160541](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160541))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the first click in a window being ignored after a ListView item is dragged out of the window.
    ([UUM-160478](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160478))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the Inspector help button on UI Animation Clip assets opening a missing documentation page.
    ([UUM-153694](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153694))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the Localization settings asset being rewritten with its table addresses reordered every time a string table was reimported.
    (UUM-158880)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the Localization submenu showing in its own group, out of alphabetical order, in the Window menu.
    (UUM-160030)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the move and resize overlay in the UI Viewport being offset from the selection outline on elements that use translate or a pixel transform origin.
    (UUM-157283)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the ProgressBar fill being too wide when its background has no border, such as in the Color field alpha bar.
    (UUM-160038)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the Smart Strings persistent variables example in the manual, which failed because it added a second PersistentVariablesSource.
    ([UUM-158917](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158917))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the Startup Selectors list in the Localization Settings asset Inspector adding an empty element instead of offering a selector type menu.
    ([UUM-160775](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160775))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the style transition performance test running out of Graphics Ring Buffer space on iOS.
    (UUM-160332)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a17.*

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the TextMeshPro `<material>` rich text tag displaying as literal text when the material is not already loaded.
    ([UUM-158921](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158921))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the UI Preview overlay in the Scene view logging the same error every frame when an element in the previewed document throws while attaching to its panel.
    (UUM-160306)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed the USS importer warning "Expected \(&lt;time&gt;\#\) but found '1E-05s'" for time and number values below 0.0001.
    (UUM-161092)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed TMP_Text.text returning stale text after repeated reads following SetText in player builds.
    ([UUM-91834](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-91834))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed Toggle, EnumField and ToggleButtonGroup displaying the rejected value when an onValidateValue callback changes it.
    ([UUM-160340](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160340))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed TreeView ScrollToItemById not revealing the item when root items are replaced and virtualization is DynamicHeight.
    ([UUM-158559](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158559))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed TreeView.SetSelectionById never returning when an item has the same id as one of its ancestors; such items are now ignored and an error is logged.
    ([UUM-160913](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160913))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Toolkit UI in the scene not responding to input after entering Play mode while a prefab with a screen-space UI Document or Panel Renderer was open in Prefab Mode.
    ([UUM-158909](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158909))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed Undo restoring a partially typed value instead of the previous value in some UI Builder Inspector style fields.
    ([UUM-159163](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159163))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed USS box-shadow being ignored when the inset keyword is not lowercase.
    ([UUM-161304](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161304))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a12.*

- UI Toolkit: Fixed USS stylesheets in the Package Manager and Scene Template windows referencing removed theme variables, which caused style resolution errors in the console.
    ([UUM-160222](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160222))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VisualElement.generateVisualContent allocating memory on every read on a TextElement with an added handler.
    ([UUM-158709](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158709))

- Unity Physics: Fixed a NullReferenceException when calling Collider.ToMesh\(\) on a sphere or capsule collider, or a compound collider containing one, in Edit Mode or when entering Play Mode.
    (UUM-160642)

- URP: Fixed "use of potentially uninitialized variable \(VisualizeWorldCulling\)" shader warnings in the Surface Cache GI debug compute shader when compiling for Metal.
    ([UUM-160257](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160257))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a16.*

- URP: Fixed a performance regression on tile-based GPUs when a camera stack contains overlay cameras that render no geometry.

- URP: Fixed a typo in the Camera Inspector message shown when a Pixel Perfect Camera overrides the projection settings.
    ([UUM-161661](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161661))

- URP: Fixed ambient occlusion settings being lost after upgrading a project in which the Screen Space Ambient Occlusion Renderer Feature is only on a non-default Renderer.
    ([UUM-159977](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159977))

- URP: Fixed an issue where a prefab opened in Prefab Mode kept contributing to Surface Cache global illumination after exiting Prefab Mode.
    (UUM-151920)

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter reported prefabs as converted, but lost their changes when it also converted objects in scenes that were not open.
    ([UUM-160400](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160400))

- URP: Fixed baked main light shadows in Distance Shadowmask mode appearing close to the camera, instead of at the shadow Max Distance, on frames where no realtime shadow casters are drawn.
    (UUM-159165)

- URP: Fixed emission being lost when converting a Standard \(Specular setup\) material to URP
    ([UUM-159486](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159486))

- URP: Fixed Forward+ rendering only the clear color on some OpenGL ES 3 devices when a camera has no additional lights or reflection probes.

- URP: Fixed ShadowUtils.GetMaxTileResolutionInAtlas returning a tile resolution too large to fit the requested number of tiles when the atlas dimensions are not powers of two.
    ([UUM-161073](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161073))

- URP: Fixed the Migrate to URP wizard's Fix step and the Built-in to URP batch converter modifying the scene template materials inside the URP package.
    ([UUM-161532](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161532))

- URP: Fixed the Render Pipeline Converter's Material Reference Converter failing with a NullReferenceException on Particle Systems when the replacement material is unavailable; it now reports which material could not be replaced.
    (UUM-151534)

- URP: Fixed the Screen Space Reflection volume override being available in render pipelines other than URP.
    ([UUM-161070](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161070))

- URP: Fixed transparent materials with alpha clipping rendering fully opaque when drawn by the Render Objects renderer feature with MSAA enabled.
    ([UUM-141725](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141725))

- URP: Fixed transparent objects not rendering in DirectX 12 Players when the Screen Space Shadows renderer feature is enabled and Main Light is disabled.
    ([UUM-146731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146731))

- URP: Warn and skip temporal upscalers if MSAA is enabled.
    ([UUM-160358](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160358))

- VFX Graph: Added an issue where the GPU could crash during editor time object destruction.
    (UUM-159682)

- VFX Graph: Addressed an issue where closing a window when multiple were open sometimes resulted in an exception.
    ([UUM-159289](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159289))

- VFX Graph: Addressed issue where a crash could occur when rapidly instancing / destroying new game objects with VFX components.
    ([UUM-157121](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157121))

- VFX Graph: Fixed a GPU crash on certain platforms when a Visual Effect Graph Shader Graph output draws indirectly, for example with sorting, culling, or a Mesh Count above 1.
    (UUM-161030)

- VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where duplicate enum names on a uint property in the VFX Graph Blackboard appeared only once in the dropdown, by appending a number to names that already exist.
    ([UUM-157968](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157968))

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicate property and category names that end in a number, such as "New Vector 3", having that number incremented instead of getting a counter in parentheses, such as "New Vector 3 \(1\)".
    ([UUM-157382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157382))

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a category giving its properties names longer than the property name length limit.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a custom attribute whose name starts or ends with an underscore, such as "_heat", removing that underscore from the copy's name.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a property or category with a name at the length limit leaving two spaces before the counter.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a property, category or custom attribute whose name ends in a large number, such as "Group \(2147483647\)", giving the copy a negative number.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a property, category or custom attribute whose name ends in a number almost as long as the length limit replacing the whole name with the counter.

- VFX Graph: Fixed error when trying to convert a selection to a block subgraph when that selection contains sticky notes or a group.
    ([UUM-153537](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153537))

- VFX Graph: Fixed opening a graph giving a property the name of a later property, which then got a new name instead.

- VFX Graph: Fixed opening a graph removing leading and trailing underscores from property names and giving duplicate property names an underscore counter.
    ([UUM-155586](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155586))

- VFX Graph: Fixed property and category names keeping leading and trailing whitespace other than spaces, such as tabs, which opening a graph now also removes from existing property names.

- VFX Graph: Fixed typo in sort mode tooltip.
    ([UUM-161747](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161747))

- VFX Graph: Keep space when duplicating a property node.
    ([UUM-161723](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161723))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a17.*

- VFX Graph: Preserve ordering when copying and pasting a blackboard category.
    ([UUM-161722](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161722))<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a17.*

- VFX Graph: VFX graph editor tabs now correctly lose their "dirty" asterisk when changes are undone to the last save point.
    ([UUM-153536](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153536))

- Video: Fixed a crash with DirectX 12 when changing the URL of a playing VideoPlayer on Windows.
    ([UUM-141179](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141179))

- Video: Fixed a use-after-free in VideoPlayer when an event handler called Play\(\) or Stop\(\) on the same VideoPlayer, or destroyed it.
    (UUM-159824)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a15.*

- Video: Fixed the colour tags of a VP8 video being lost when transcoding it to VP9.<br>
    VP8 videos tagged BT.709 or BT.2020 in the WebM container are now decoded with that colour standard instead of BT.601, so their colours may look different from previous versions
    (UUM-157809)<br>
    *First seen in 7000.0.0a9.*

- VisionOS: Fixed a crash in `UnityCheckUnloadAndQuit` when a batch-mode player quits while its host process keeps running, for example in an XCTest with a quit handler or in Unity as a Library.
    (UUM-159471)

- Web: Fixed AudioClip.GetData writing past the end of the destination array, which could crash the player or corrupt memory, when the clip reused the internal sound of a destroyed clip with more channels.
    ([UUM-160614](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160614))

- WebGL: Fixed issues with async GPU readback on WebGPU when source texture format was different to destination format.
    (UUM-161129)

- Windows: Builds using Visual Studio solution option now once again can be built for any architecture from Visual Studio when using IL2CPP scripting backend, rather than just the one selected at build time in Unity.

- Windows: Fixed keys staying pressed in Windows Standalone players after opening and closing the Steam Overlay.
    (UUM-154774)

- Windows: Windows: Fixed Mouse.current and Pen.current staying set when no mouse or pen is attached, or after the device is unplugged. Mouse.current is now null when no mouse is attached.
    (UUM-150403)

- Windows: Windows: Fixed Touchscreen.current being set on machines without touch hardware, and not updating when a touchscreen is attached or removed.
    (UUM-150406)

### Preview of Final 7000.0.0a20 Release Notes

#### Features

- 2D: Foundational work for Unified Render Data for 2D Renderers

- Adaptive Performance: Added settings to the Device Simulator's Adaptive Performance section that simulate CPU and GPU utilization.

- Android: Changed Android to use static TLS by default

- Animation: Added named animatable channels for script components. A MonoBehaviour exposes channels by declaring a serialized field of type AnimatableFloatChannels, AnimatableIntChannels or AnimatableObjectChannels.

- Audio: Added 7.1.4 spatialization passthrough to macOS.

- Build Pipeline: Added a Referenced By tree to the Build Analysis asset inspector, which shows which assets reference the selected asset and the paths from each of them up to the build's loadable roots.

- Burst: Added LLVM 23 support.

- Editor: Added a Search Online item to the Console context menus that searches the web for the log message or selected text, using the search engine set in Preferences &gt; General.

- Editor: Added support for the HelpURL attribute on structs, so data components with the attribute show a help button in the Inspector.

- Editor: Added the **Update Time Budget** preference which limits how long the Hierarchy window spends updating each frame. The default is 0.5 ms per update.

- Editor: Editor: RE2, SpeedTree-integration, and embree now provide builds against the dynamic C runtime

- Editor: Introduce BuildProfilePostprocessBuild component so users can add postprocess build callbacks to build profiles that will run for the active build profile being built.

- Editor: New Sprite Mesh Module for Sprite Editor Window.

- Editor: Previews for items will show when performing a Drag and Drop on macOS.

- Editor: Project Auditor: Add a package version database, so users know about package upgrades during Editor upgrades.

- Editor: Refreshed the theme of Unity Editor 7.

- Editor: Remove support for mono desktop players

- Entities: Added an Entities Queries profiler module that shows the time spent in query calls, query creation, archetype matching and waiting for jobs, per call and per system. Also, the Entities Memory profiler module now shows archetype, query and query cache counts, and the memory used by queries.

- Entities: Data components can now use the Icon attribute to set the icon shown in the Inspector and the Add Component menu.

- GI: Added an Albedo view mode to the Surface Cache Global Illumination panel in the Rendering Debugger, showing the albedo that bounce lighting uses for each surface.

- GI: Added an Emission view mode to the Surface Cache Global Illumination panel in the Rendering Debugger, showing the emission that bounce lighting uses for each surface.

- Graphics: Added a startup dialog that helps projects upgraded from the Built-In Render Pipeline migrate to the Universal Render Pipeline using Project Auditor. Graphics and Quality settings now explain why a project doesn't render and how to fix it.

- Graphics: URP: Added a Rendering Layer Mask filter to the Render Objects renderer feature, allowing a pass to be restricted to objects matching the selected rendering layers

- IL2CPP: IOS, tvOS and visionOS builds using the Master C++ Compiler Configuration can now use link-time optimization, controlled by the IL2CPP LTO Mode Player Setting.

- iOS: Introducing early callbacks for iOS/tvOS

- Package Manager: Added a Closed network environment setting in Project Settings that lets users with the packages bypass entitlement turn off package signature validation.

- Package Manager: Deprecated version of UPM packages from the Asset Store are now installable from the Package Manager window.

- Package Manager: Improved keyboard navigation in the sidebar and package list

- Package Manager: Users on closed network will be able to export UPM packages without a signature.

- Package Manager: When Closed Network users enable the Closed Network-exclusive Project Setting to disable Trust messaging on packages, all packages are considered Out of Trust.

- Profiler: Added Zstandard compression for saved Profiler captures, which makes .data files much smaller. To save captures that earlier versions of Unity can open, disable Compress saved captures in the Profiler Preferences.

- Profiler: Reimplemented an aggregate draw calls counter for profiling

- Scripting: Added code coverage support for CoreCLR desktop standalone players.

- Scripting: Added code coverage support for the CoreCLR scripting backend in the Editor.

- Scripting: Code reload now uses multiple 'AssemblyLoadContext' to minimize the amount of code that needs to reload during each code reload.

- Scripting: WebGL: WebGL IL2CPP builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- Shaders: For build targets that support shader compilation via the DirectX Shader Compiler \(DXC\) - currently D3D12 and Vulkan, shader authors can use the new \#pragma hlsl_version 2021 to enable the HLSL 2021 set of syntax enhancements detailed on the Microsoft developer blog post \(https://devblogs.microsoft.com/directx/announcing-hlsl-2021/\)

- Shaders: Updated DXC compiler to upstream main branch with Shader Model 6.10 support. Linear algebra intrinsics are now available via \#include &lt;dx/linalg.h&gt; in HLSL shaders.

- UI Toolkit: Added a pixel magnifier to the UI Toolkit Debugger that shows the selected element's rendered pixels at an integer zoom.

- UI Toolkit: Added keyboard shortcuts and context menu items to add, replace, wrap, and unwrap elements in the UI Toolkit Hierarchy and UI Viewport.

- UI Toolkit: Added the box-shadow USS property, with outer and inset shadows, blur and spread radii, transitions, and UI Builder and Inspector support.

- URP: Added a Project Auditor rule that reports Spot Lights that need a cookie to match the Built-in cone falloff when the light falloff mode is set to Quadratic.

- VFX Graph: Added Sample Terrain Node to VFX Graph which simplifies the process of extracting height data from a terrain.

- XR: Added OpenXR multiple Spatial containers support.

- XR: Added Temporal Pixel Synthesis support for XR.



#### Improvements

- 2D: Add vertices, indices and edge into SpriteImportData so that TextureGenerator can generate custom mesh.

- 2D: Added Instancing support for Deform.

- Adaptive Performance: The Adaptive Performance module in the Profiler now shows CPU utilization and GPU utilization.

- Android: Android builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- Android: Internal cleanup of APK path lookup; no behaviour change.

- Android: Removed main-thread stall when starting and stopping WebCamTexture.

- Animation: Improved the Animation Preview Model picker to preselect the model currently in use.

- Apple TV: TvOS builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- Asset Import: Improved the security of model import by updating the bundled Autodesk FBX SDK to 2020.3.11, addressing two buffer-overflow vulnerabilities in FBX file parsing.
    (UUM-155199)

- Asset Pipeline: Added a Console error when an import worker process stops before it connects to the Editor.

- Asset Pipeline: Improved Memory Profiler attribution of Asset Database native memory, which is now reported under named AssetDatabase roots instead of as unrooted memory.
    (UUM-126907)

- Asset Pipeline: Reduced Editor memory used to track script and plugin assets, by about 4 KB per script.

- Audio: Added per-platform overrides to the Audio Settings, allowing you to specify different settings based on the platform your game will run on.

- Audio: Audio Project settings panel has been reworked to be clearer.

- Build Pipeline: Build Analysis: Added a banner to builds copied in from another project, and disabled selecting their assets in the Project window.
    ([UUM-143858](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143858))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where import workers would add a large unnecessary asset refresh overhead during content directory builds.
    (UUM-158754)

- Build Pipeline: Section headers in the Build Analysis window now highlight across their full width when hovered.

- Burst: Burst now enables safety checks in builds when the managed code variant player setting is set to Debug or Checked.

- Burst: Improved the layout of the Burst Inspector window, with clearer toolbars and output tabs and a search bar that is always shown. Also added a function dropdown to the Burst Inspector that jumps to a function in the disassembly, and Previous and Next buttons to step through search matches.

- Documentation: Added a Pinned libraries section to the MSBuild and NuGet policy page that lists the libraries Unity pins and their versions.

- DX12: Improved the error messages shown when DirectX 12 runs out of memory, which previously reported an unrecoverable GPU device error.
    ([UUM-125265](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-125265))

- DX12: Reduced the render thread time of constant buffers updated every frame, which sometimes created a new GPU resource instead of reusing one of their previous ones.

- DX12: Reduced the render thread time of structured and raw buffers updated every frame with SetData, such as the URP Forward+ light clusters, by reusing their previous GPU resources instead of creating new ones.

- Editor: Added a log message to warn when the activeBuildProfile flag is used and the build target is not supported.

- Editor: Fixed reading files larger than 2GB from archives \(.apk/.obb/.zip\) on 32-bit Android devices.

- Editor: Improved ConsistencyChecker warning for inconsistent assets. The warning now includes a hyperlink that will automatically open the previous import artifact file and the current import artifact file in a diff tool, streamlining troubleshooting of inconsistent assets.

- Editor: Improved Editor startup time by avoiding an expensive full-assembly type scan during mode discovery

- Editor: Improved Hierarchy window responsiveness in large scenes by spreading its update over multiple frames instead of completing it in a single blocking pass.
    (UUM-153593)

- Editor: Improved the CacheServerV2 integration tests by spawning a single cache server for the suite of tests rather than spawning a new server for each test. This prevents the instability in restarting the server process between tests.
    (UUM-145948)

- Editor: Improved the description of what's contained in the Multiplayer sample install flow.

- Editor: Project Auditor: detect boxing of structs used in List comparisons.

- Editor: Project Auditor: expose Finalizer warning to user code so Project Auditor can display it.

- Editor: Project Auditor: redesign the home page. Move URP migration wizard to the home page.

- Editor: Project Auditor: The error shown when the rules package is missing now names the com.unity.project-auditor-rules package.
    (UUM-156488)

- Editor: Reduce the number of camera projections required to draw gizmos in scene view for rect transforms.

- Editor: Shader compilation work is now captured in the buildreport.json Chrome trace profiling file that's generated during editor player builds allowing the time taken processing shaders to be visualized and inspected.
    (UUM-134289)

- Editor: Updated DefaultAsset &amp; ScriptableObject icons
    (UUM-135067)

- Editor: Updated layout, tooltips, and reset behaviour for some fields in the Skinned Mesh Renderer component.

- Editor: Use a ListView for the build profile callbacks component and display warning when build callback class is renamed or moved.

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed performance metrics being reported without a sum, which prevented average values from being calculated.

- Entities: Calling BlobArray&lt;T&gt;.ToArray on an element type that contains BlobArray or BlobPtr fields is now reported as compile error UAC0044, in addition to the existing runtime exception.

- Entities: Improved live baking diff performance in worlds with many entities.

- Entities: Improved the Inspector display of FixedString and Unity.Entities.Hash128 fields, which are now shown as text instead of their raw serialized data.

- Entities: Improved the performance of `ComponentLookup`, `BufferLookup` and `TransformLookup` methods that check whether an entity exists or has a component.

- GI: Added a Material Resolution setting to the Surface Cache Global Illumination renderer feature. New renderer features use Medium, and existing ones keep Very High.

- GI: Added a Ray Tracing Normal Bias setting to the Surface Cache GI volume override, which prevents dark artifacts when the rasterized geometry differs from the ray-traced geometry, for example with a coarser mesh LOD.
    (UUM-160232)

- GI: Added a World Culling view, an option to exclude the Scene view from entity culling, and culled and live entity and chunk counts to the Surface Cache section of the Rendering Debugger.

- GI: Added debug visualizations for Screen Space Reflections in URP to the Rendering Debugger.

- GI: Added Entity Culling settings to the Surface Cache Global Illumination volume override, which leave out entities that are beyond a distance from every camera, or smaller than a minimum size on screen, in pixels or degrees.

- GI: Added material atlas usage to the Surface Cache panel of the Rendering Debugger.

- GI: Added support for cutout materials in Surface Cache GI.

- GI: Improved Memory Profiler attribution of lighting native memory, which was previously reported as Unrooted.
    (UUM-126924)

- GI: Surface Cache GI now uses the Mesh LOD for Global Illumination set on each Mesh Renderer instead of always using Mesh LOD 0.

- GI: Tweaked presets in Surface Cache GI to make denoised results look better.

- Graph Toolkit: Graph Toolkit editor windows no longer keep views and models alive in static caches after they are closed.

- Graphics: Added a warning when a GraphicsBuffer is created with both GraphicsBuffer.Target.Raw and GraphicsBuffer.Target.Structured, because DirectX 11 and DirectX 12 can't bind such a buffer as a StructuredBuffer.
    (UUM-161113)

- Graphics: Added copy and paste support for Shader Constant Defines and Compiler Backend Settings in Shader Build Settings.

- Graphics: Added copy and paste support to Keyword Declaration Overrides in Shader Build Settings.

- Graphics: DirectX 12: Reduced frame hitches on some GPUs caused by re-creating command lists when the GPU falls a frame behind the CPU.
    (UUM-161319)

- Graphics: Improved CPU performance of ray tracing acceleration structure builds when the structure contains many static instances or has had many instances removed.

- Graphics: Improved Render Graph Viewer empty state message when connected to a player
    (UUM-148168)

- Graphics: In the editor, when enabling Tile-Only Mode on a Universal Renderer Data asset with both built-in Post-processing and the On Tile Post Processing renderer feature enabled, an inspector error will show in addition to the existing console error.

- Graphics: Mesh-related memory should now be correctly labeled in the memory profiler
    (UUM-126926)

- Graphics: On OpenGL ES and OpenGL, updating a GraphicsBuffer that is bound as a constant buffer no longer overwrites data the GPU may still be reading. This avoids render thread waits on some GPUs, such as PowerVR.

- Graphics: Player builds no longer include the skinning and blend shape compute shaders when Mesh Deformation is set to CPU in Player Settings. This reduces build size.

- Graphics: Reduced render thread time for render passes whose renderer list is empty after filtering, when graphics jobs are enabled.

- Graphics: Reduced render thread time on Metal for buffers updated every frame, such as the constant buffers of the Universal Render Pipeline.

- Graphics: Stopped the render thread from freezing when the async upload buffer is corrupted, and logged an error in that case in release builds too.
    (UUM-150567)

- GraphView: Improved fallback search handling of special characters.
    ([UUM-152948](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152948))

- GraphView: Improved fallback search queries in the template browser.
    ([UUM-152891](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152891))

- IL2CPP: A boxing allocation is no longer performed when the boxed value is immediately discarded.

- IL2CPP: Calling GetType\(\) on a boxed value type, including comparing the result against a typeof\(\) expression, no longer causes a heap allocation.

- IL2CPP: Casting between an enum and its underlying integer type through object no longer causes a heap allocation.

- IL2CPP: Expand inlining heuristic to inline small simple methods.

- IL2CPP: Fixed static field accesses not throwing an exception when a class's cctor failed on some platforms.

- IL2CPP: GC.KeepAlive no longer causes a heap allocation when it is passed a value type.

- IL2CPP: Generated code for some cases of pointer arithmetic is now vectorized by Clang.

- IL2CPP: Implement TimerQueue's MainThreadScheduleTimer for WebGL.

- IL2CPP: Improved inlining heuristic to expand set of possibly inlined methods.

- IL2CPP: Improved performance of type checks when storing references into arrays.

- IL2CPP: Improved performance of `typeof(T)` in generic code.

- IL2CPP: Improved performance of `typeof` and `Object.GetType()`.

- IL2CPP: Removed un-needing boxing that can occur with the `if (x is Type t)` pattern.

- IL2CPP: Updated Math.Pow and MathF.Pow to match behavior of .NET.

- IL2CPP: Volatile fields and the `volatile.` prefix use acquire and release semantics instead of a full memory barrier.

- IL2CPP: `Volatile.Read` and `Volatile.Write` use acquire and release semantics instead of a full memory barrier.

- iOS: Fixed Append being allowed when changing Symlink Sources option

- iOS: iOS builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- iOS: Make AppDelegate and SceneDelegate methods in UnityPlayer public

- iOS: On iOS 27.1 and later, devices without a known notch entry, such as the iPhone Duo, now report the camera regions provided by iOS in `Screen.cutouts` instead of an empty list.

- iOS: Updated Swift API reference to link to low-level native plugin API docs.

- Kernel: Optimized CRC calculations to reduce time spent loading assets
    (UUM-147293)

- Kernel: Reduced copying of Collections and Mathematics structs, such as FixedList, AffineTransform and RigidTransform, when their non-mutating methods are called through in parameters, readonly fields or ref readonly references.
    ([UUM-124913](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-124913))

- Linux: Linux IL2CPP builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- macOS: macOS IL2CPP builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- Mono: Reduced JIT compile overhead by skipping JIT phase timing when runtime statistics are disabled.

- Package Manager: Analytics for .unityPackage signature at export time, from UI, API and CLI

- Package Manager: Improves keyboard navigation in the package manager details and toolbar

- Physics 2D: Improved performance and memory use when many physics objects share the same callback target.
    (UUM-156561)

- Physics 2D: Improved the accuracy of bouncing, which is now resolved after the rest of the contact solving each simulation step.
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: Improved the performance of contact creation and shape queries.
    (UUM-152756)

- Physics 2D: Improved the performance of contact solving and of finding new contact pairs.
    (UUM-158185)

- Physics 2D: Improved the performance of contact, trigger, body update, and joint threshold callbacks, which no longer query the engine for each event to find its callback target.
    (UUM-158710)

- Physics 2D: Improved the PhysicsCore2D Profiler to show timing information in a consistent hierarchy which indicates not only the times taken but also where in relation to other timings they occur.
    (UUM-152394)

- Physics 2D: Reduced the code size that PhysicsCore2D adds to a build by approximately 80 KB.
    (UUM-161329)

- Prefabs: Improved the performance of Apply Selected in the Prefab Overrides window when several overrides are selected, as the Prefab is now saved and imported once.
    ([UUM-158666](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158666))

- Profiler: - Improved module pinning UX in the profiler window. It is now possible to pin modules by hovering on a module chart header and clicking the pin icon, or directly from the Profiler Modules dropdown.<br>
    - Added a draggable divider below the pinned modules. When the pinned modules don't fit in the pinned area, the area scrolls and you can drag the divider to resize it.

- Profiler: Improved native memory attribution so several kernel, plugin and file system allocations are reported against a named root instead of Unrooted in the Memory Profiler.
    (UUM-126921)

- Profiler: Improved the Jobs Profiler to label combine dependencies jobs as CombineDependencies instead of EngineJob.

- Profiler: Reduced GC.Alloc hook overhead when profiler is disabled or there are no active ProfilerRecorders for GC.Alloc metric.
    (UUM-146629)

- Profiler: Reduced overhead of ProfilerMarker.Begin/End and ProfilingSampler API when the Profiler is not recording.
    (UUM-154448)

- QNX: QNX builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- Scene/Game View: Replaced the Scene View orientation gizmo with a lighter UI Toolkit implementation, reducing its per-repaint cost and giving it a new flat-icon style.

- Scripting: Improved the performance of engine calls to MonoBehaviour methods such as Update with the IL2CPP scripting backend.

- Scripting: The "IL2CPP Stacktrace Information" setting has been renamed to "Managed Stack Trace Information" and now affects the CoreCLR scripting backend too.

- Scripting: The "Managed Stack Trace Information" setting now affects stack traces printed from Burst code.

- Serialization: Improved how text-serialized files with invalid object headers are loaded: Unity now reports an error instead of reading the object's fileID from part of the header, or skipping the object without an error.

- Serialization: Improved loading of serialized data whose layout does not match the script it belongs to: the player now logs a serialization error instead of crashing.

- Shadergraph: Warn when a Screen Position node was found to erroneously be in Clip mode.
    ([UUM-159528](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159528))

- Shaders: Reduced memory usage when dynamic variant loading is enabled.

- Shaders: The editor console and shader inspector now emit warnings when invalid KEYWORD_TYPE_FLAG keywords are detected.

- Test Framework: Improved test result reporting speed for large test assemblies, where looking up each test's source file and line no longer slows the run down as the number of tests grows.

- Test Framework: Improved test result reporting speed for runs with many tests, which no longer slows down as the number of tests grows.

- Test Framework: Improved the speed of edit mode test runs while the Editor is not the active application, which previously slowed to 10 Editor updates per second.

- Test Framework: Improved the speed of edit mode test runs while the Test Runner window is open, which no longer repaints and recounts all results for every finished test.

- Test Framework: Prebuild setup, post-build cleanup and player build steps are now reported as timing spans during automated test runs.

- Test Framework: Timing spans reported during automated test runs now name their parent test or span explicitly.

- Text: Reduced player build size by removing unused features from the bundled text libraries.

- TextMeshPro: Reduced memory spikes and CPU cost when dynamic TextMesh Pro font assets add many new characters at once, by uploading each atlas texture once instead of once per character.
    ([UUM-133781](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133781))

- uGUI: 2D textures dropped onto uGUI Canvas objects in the hierarchy now create or update a RawImage, or a RawImage animation for multiple textures.

- uGUI: Added the DISABLE_UGUI_UITK_SUPPORT scripting define symbol, which excludes the uGUI integration with UI Toolkit so that player builds can strip the UI Toolkit module when it is only used in the Editor.
    (UUM-159610)

- uGUI: Sprites dropped onto uGUI Canvas objects in the hierarchy now create or update an Image, or an Image animation for multiple sprites.

- UI Toolkit: Added a one-time scan that lists themes affected by the theme priority change, with a report file written to Logs/ and an on-demand scan button in Project Settings &gt; UI Toolkit.

- UI Toolkit: Added a warning when a theme style sheet is imported by a regular style sheet, since the theme's priority does not apply in that case.

- UI Toolkit: Added nested formats to the Smart Strings time formatter, so a nested list format can choose the separators between time units. The time formatter also accepts a `DateTime` or `DateTimeOffset` without format text.

- UI Toolkit: Added Prepare Panel Renderers and Update Panel Renderers charts to the UI Toolkit Profiler module, which show the time spent processing Panel Renderer components, including UI reload callbacks.

- UI Toolkit: Changing the Editor Font, Text Rendering Mode, or Text Generator preference now takes effect immediately instead of requiring an Editor restart.

- UI Toolkit: Importing a localization table collection now warns when two of its keys hold the same ID, or when it has no shared table data.
    ([UUM-151766](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151766))

- UI Toolkit: Improved data source context caching.

- UI Toolkit: Improved dragging the label of angle fields in the UI Toolkit Authoring inspector, which now moves one degree per pixel in every unit and keeps fractional values.

- UI Toolkit: Improved Editor performance when importing style sheets, which no longer make UI Builder search the whole project for UXML files on every import.

- UI Toolkit: Improved event dispatch performance when no events are queued and when callbacks are registered or unregistered during event dispatch.

- UI Toolkit: Improved Smart Strings: plural formatting now works for languages without plural forms and for a count of two in French, conditional formats read numbers the same way in every culture, and dictionary lookups follow the case-sensitivity setting.

- UI Toolkit: Improved style resolution performance when many elements share the same cached styles.

- UI Toolkit: Improved style sheet import diagnostics: an @import placed after style rules now produces a warning explaining that its position does not affect selector priority.

- UI Toolkit: Improved style update performance for elements with transitions when an ancestor's class or pseudo-state changes without affecting their matched style rules.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of cloning UXML documents that instantiate templates.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of pointer event dispatch, especially when the pointer moves between deeply nested elements.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of removing elements from a UI Toolkit hierarchy, and of binding large inspectors and editor UIs.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of runtime data binding updates, and reduced the memory allocated when bindings are registered.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of scheduling and unscheduling actions with VisualElement.schedule in panels that have many scheduled items.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of visual element change notifications in UI Toolkit by only notifying the internal updaters that handle each type of change.

- UI Toolkit: Improved the rendering performance of panels with many filtered elements: filter textures are now shared, and the same filter pass of many elements is drawn in one batch.
    (UUM-160498)

- UI Toolkit: Reduced the amount of memory each `VisualElement` uses.

- UI Toolkit: Reduced the GPU memory used to render elements with the filter style property.

- UI Toolkit: Reduced the memory used by each component added to a VisualElement, and by component types that are registered but never added.

- UI Toolkit: Removed the per-restyle managed allocations for elements whose UXML inline style declares custom properties \(USS variables\).

- UI Toolkit: UI Builder no longer searches the whole project after every UXML or USS change when no UI Builder window is open.

- UI Toolkit: Unity now reports a compile error when code replaces a visual element component that owns resources by assigning a whole new value over it, or copies a live one onto another element; remove the component and add a new one instead.

- URP: Add Depth Pyramid for GTAO in URP

- URP: Reduced memory use for renderers that have no additional lights or don't use the reflection probe atlas, and main thread time for cameras that see no reflection probe.

- URP: The Forward+ and Deferred+ rendering paths no longer process lights and reflection probes in screen areas that only their part beyond the far plane, or in front of an orthographic camera's near plane, reaches.

- URP: URP shaders declare their global shader variables in a constant buffer on every graphics API except OpenGL ES 3 and OpenGL Core.

- Web: Improved Web Build Size by disabling Input System's Precompiled Layouts for Web Builds.

- Web: Reduced Web build size by allowing the linker to strip Input System types that aren't used on the Web platform

- Windows: Reduced Editor startup time on Windows by removing an unnecessary wait for a background device-identifier lookup.

- Windows: Windows IL2CPP builds now use .NET 10 base class libraries.

- XR: Graphics: Reduced CPU main thread time on URP XR Quad Views by having the inner view reuse the outer view's cached volume stack instead of re-evaluating the volume framework per stacked camera per frame.

- XR: Graphics: Reduced GPU frame time on Quad Views by reprojecting the outer pass's motion vectors into the inner pass instead of rendering its own motion vector pass.

- XR: Improved GPU performance when QuadViews are enabled in Forward/Forward+ \(and when not using SpaceWarp\) via reducing the amount of redundant geometry rendering in the focus-inner region during the low-resolution periphery pass.



#### API Changes

- 2D: Added: Added `ISpriteEditor.ShowNotice` and `ISpriteEditor.HideNotice`, which let Sprite Editor modules show dismissible notices in the Sprite Editor window.

- 2D: Changed: Moved EntityIdTileUtility from the UnityEditor.Tilemaps.Experimental namespace to UnityEditor.Tilemaps.

- Adaptive Performance: Added: Added CpuUtilization and GpuUtilization properties to SimulatorAdaptivePerformanceSubsystem and SimulatorAdaptivePerformanceSubsystem.SimulatorProvider.

- Android: Added: Added Application category which returns application category which settable in Player Settings.

- Animation: Added: Added AnimatableFloatChannels, AnimatableIntChannels and AnimatableObjectChannels.

- Animation: Added: Introducing a new low-level Animation API in the Unity.Animation namespace. This changed the format of AnimationClip at runtime. Older player builds and asset bundles containing AnimationClips will still work but loading will be slower, they should be rebuilt in the last format to restore fast loading speed.

- Build Pipeline: Added: Added BuildAnalysisUtility.OpenWindow, which opens the Build Analysis window with a specific build selected.

- Build Pipeline: Added: Added BuildHistory.TryGetParentBuild and BuildHistory.GetChildBuilds to find the build a build ran inside and the builds it ran, recorded in the new BuildReportSummary.ParentBuildSessionGUID.
    (UUM-153441)

- Build Pipeline: Added: Added BuildSummary.projectGuid and BuildSummary.projectName, and BuildReportSummary.ProjectGUID and BuildReportSummary.ProjectName, which record the project that made a build.

- Build Pipeline: Added: Added ContentDirectoryDiagnostics.GetContentDirectoryLoadInfo, which reports the content files in memory and the scenes loaded from a content directory in the Editor and development Players, and separates the holds that Unity keeps on root assets from those of game code. Added ContentLoadEditorUtility.TryGetSourceAssetGUID to find the source asset of each file.

- Build Pipeline: Changed: BuildPipeline.BuildContentDirectory now throws an ArgumentException if the requested compression type is not supported on the target platform.

- Code Stripping: Deprecated: Deprecated ManagedStrippingLevel.Low. Passing it to PlayerSettings.SetManagedStrippingLevel now throws a NotSupportedException; use ManagedStrippingLevel.Minimal instead.

- Editor: Added: Added ability to reflect information on structs, as one can with functions, from `.hlsl` assets.

- Editor: Added: Added AnalysisResult.Skipped, which Project Auditor reports for a module that could not run in the current context.
    (UUM-159431)

- Editor: Added: Added API to get the event that triggered the shortcut in the ShortcutArguments.
    (UUM-153126)

- Editor: Added: Added HierarchyViewColumnDescriptor.MinWidth to set the minimum width of a custom Hierarchy column.
    (UUM-155402)

- Editor: Added: Added SourceTemplateAttribute.Target and the SourceTemplateTargetExpression enum to the built-in JetBrains.Annotations, so source templates can use Target = SourceTemplateTargetExpression.Outer.
    ([UUM-84741](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-84741))

- Editor: Added: Added SystemInfo.currentSwapChainPreTransform to expose UNITY_DISPLAY_ORIENTATION_PRETRANSFORM value
    (UUM-154867)

- Editor: Added: Added the BuildProfilePlayerSettings component, so that you can create, get, and remove a build profile's own player settings with BuildProfile.CreateComponent, GetComponent, and RemoveComponent.

- Editor: Added: Expose BuildProfileGraphicsSettings and BuildProfileQualitySettings as public build profile components.

- Editor: Added: Project Auditor: Added Descriptor.HasAnyAreas and ReportItem.HasAnyAreas to filter issues by area, and made SerializableEnumExtensions public so IssueCategory arrays can be assigned to AnalysisParams.Categories.
    (UUM-156488)

- Editor: Changed: `AssetPreview.GetMiniTypeThumbnail(Type)` now also returns icons for types that don't derive from UnityEngine.Object.

- Editor: Deprecated: Deprecated `DataMode`, `DataModeChangeEventArgs`, `IDataModeController` and `EditorWindow.dataModeController`. They will be removed in a future release.

- Editor: Obsoleted: Output an error instead of a warning when ARCoreUpdate and ARCoreEnabled APIs are used.

- EmbeddedLinux: Added: Added DisplayInfo.RegisterLayoutChangedCallback and DisplayInfo.UnregisterLayoutChangedCallback, which notify the application when a display is connected, disconnected or reconfigured.

- Engine Diagnostics: Changed: EngineDiagnosticsSettings.enabled now reads and writes the Diagnostic Data Collection setting of the active build profile, or of the active platform profile if no build profile is active, and doesn't change other build profiles. To disable Diagnostic Data Collection for all users and CI, set it to Disabled in a build profile asset, commit the asset, and build with that profile by using -activeBuildProfile or BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer with BuildPlayerWithProfileOptions.

- Entities: Added: Added an implicit conversion from EntityId to Entity.

- Entities: Added: Added GlobalEntityManager methods that read, write, add, remove, enable and list components by ComponentType, and report a component's change version, so code that only knows a component type at runtime can act on an entity in any world.

- Entities: Added: Added `GlobalEntityManager`, which reads and changes an entity in whichever world owns it, and instantiates an entity from any world into a given world.

- Entities: Changed: `IconAttribute` can now be applied to structs.

- Entities: Deprecated: Deprecated the Write Groups API

- Entities: Deprecated: Deprecated the `HierarchySearchProvider` class. The standalone Entities Hierarchy window has been replaced by the integrated Editor Hierarchy window.

- Entities: Obsoleted: Marked `HierarchySearchProvider` as obsolete with an error. To find entities by component, use the `t:` filter in the Hierarchy window.

- Entities: Obsoleted: Obsoleted Unity.Assertions.Assert in favour of UnityEngine.Assertions.Assert, which it forwarded to. The API Updater converts existing uses.

- Entities: Removed: Removed the Entities Journaling window and the EntitiesJournaling API, deprecated in Unity 6.5; use the IDebugOnAdded and IDebugOnRemoved component lifecycle callbacks instead.

- Entities Graphics: Added: Added the GlobalIlluminationMeshLod component, which selects the Mesh LOD level an entity uses for global illumination.

- GI: Changed: MeshRenderer.globalIlluminationMeshLod is now available in Players, not only in the Editor.

- GI: Deprecated: Deprecated `ProbeVolumeBakingSet.sceneGUIDs` \(use scenesInBakingSet instead\), and the string overloads of `TryAddScene`, `RemoveScene`, `SetSceneBaking` \(use the GUID overloads instead\).

- GI: Deprecated: Deprecated `ProbeVolumeBakingSet.sceneGUIDs` \(use scenesInBakingSet instead\), and the string overloads of `TryAddScene`, `RemoveScene`, `SetSceneBaking` \(use the GUID overloads instead\).

- GI: Deprecated: LightingSettings.lightmapper is now deprecated. The lightmapper in use is determined by the Default Light Baker setting in the Graphics settings \(UnityEditor.Rendering.EditorGraphicsSettings.defaultLightBaker\).

- GI: Obsoleted: LightingSettings.Lightmapper.ProgressiveCPU now produces a compiler error; use LightingSettings.Lightmapper.ProgressiveGPU or LightingSettings.Lightmapper.UnityComputeGPU instead.

- Graph Toolkit: Added: Added StateMachine.OnDefineSubgraphStateOptions to define the options of subgraph states that reference a state machine.
    (UUM-155649)

- Graphics: Added: Added Graphics.disableDepthAndStencilBuffers, a read-only property that returns whether Disable Depth and Stencil Buffers is enabled in Player Settings.

- Graphics: Added: Added GraphicsStateCollection.ComputeShaderVariant, AddComputeVariant, RemoveComputeVariant, ContainsComputeVariant, GetComputeVariants and computeVariantCount.

- Graphics: Added: Added `SystemInfo.supportsPrecompiledGraphicsStateStats`, which reports whether the current graphics device can return precompiled graphics state statistics. It is false on every graphics device that does not expose them, and the statistics are all zero in that case.

- Graphics: Added: Added `UnityEngine.Rendering.PrecompiledGraphicsState.GetStats`, which returns statistics of the precompiled graphics states usage for the current graphics device \(if supported\).

- Graphics: Deprecated: Camera and Light command buffers no longer execute when a scriptable render pipeline is active, including in the Scene view.

- Graphics: Deprecated: Deprecated the C\# APIs associated with Baked Occlusion Culling. They continue to work but now produce compiler warnings and will be removed in a future release. Consider migrating to GPU Occlusion Culling where supported.

- Graphics: Deprecated: Deprecated the Camera and Light command buffer API \(AddCommandBuffer, AddCommandBufferAsync, RemoveCommandBuffer, RemoveCommandBuffers, RemoveAllCommandBuffers, GetCommandBuffers, commandBufferCount\) and the CameraEvent, LightEvent and ShadowMapPass enums, which only work with the Built-In Render Pipeline.

- Graphics: Deprecated: Deprecated the `OcclusionArea` and `OcclusionPortal` component APIs. They continue to work but will be removed in a future release. Consider migrating to GPU Occlusion Culling where supported.

- Graphics: Deprecated: Deprecated `Unsupported.useScriptableRenderPipeline` because the Built-In Render Pipeline is obsolete

- HDRP: Added: Enabled Upscaling Framework by default.

- Input System: Added: Added `Gamepad.gyroscope` and `Gamepad.accelerometer` to get the motion sensors that belong to a gamepad.

- iOS: Added: Added iPhone 18 Pro, iPhone 18 Pro Max and iPhone Duo device generation enums and screen cutouts.

- iOS: Changed: PBXProject build property setters \(SetBuildProperty, AddBuildProperty, UpdateBuildProperty<br>
    and their ForConfig variants\) now throw ArgumentNullException when given a null value.

- iOS: Changed: SDKROOT, SUPPORTED_PLATFORMS, TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY, *_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET and ARCHS are no longer defined in the trampoline Xcode templates - the player build is now their only source. In the Swift trampoline these settings are written at project level only, so the targets inherit them.

- iOS: Changed: Trampoline `DisplayConnection` is now created with `initWithScene:` and exposes a `scene` property; `DisplayManager` is keyed and subscripted by `UIWindowScene` instead of `UIScreen`.
    (PLAT-22932)

- iOS: Removed: Removed support for Intel simulators.

- Kernel: Added: Added MemoryLabel.Create

- Kernel: Added: Added Unity.Mathematics math.select overloads for bool, bool2, bool3, and bool4 values, with either a single bool or a per-component bool vector as the selection mask.

- License: Added: Added LicensingUtility.HasPro, HasEduLicense, IsPersonal, and GetAuthToken, which replace the deprecated license helpers on UnityEditorInternal.InternalEditorUtility.

- License: Added: EntitlementGroupInfo.TryGetExpirationUtc \(not yet used, except in tests\) for reading an entitlement group's expiry as a DateTime.

- License: Changed: Moved the licensing API from the UnityEditor.Experimental.Licensing namespace to UnityEditor.Licensing. Existing code is updated automatically when the project is imported.

- License: Deprecated: Deprecated the license helper methods on UnityEditorInternal.InternalEditorUtility.

- License: Removed: Removed UnityEditorInternal.InternalEditorUtility.GetLicenseFlags, which already produced a compile error when used.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "currentSpringForce" to "PhysicsDistanceJoint", "PhysicsSliderJoint" and "PhysicsWheelJoint", and "currentSpringTorque" to "PhysicsHingeJoint".
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "origin" overloads to the cast queries "PhysicsWorld.CastShape", "PhysicsWorld.CastShapeProxy" and "PhysicsWorld.CastGeometry", so the cast geometry can be authored in a local frame and positioned by a world-space point.
    (UUM-153476)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "origin" overloads to the overlap queries "PhysicsWorld.TestOverlapShape", "PhysicsWorld.OverlapShape", "PhysicsWorld.TestOverlapShapeProxy", "PhysicsWorld.OverlapShapeProxy", "PhysicsWorld.TestOverlapGeometry" and "PhysicsWorld.OverlapGeometry", matching the cast queries.
    (UUM-153476)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsBody.drawDepth" and "PhysicsBody.SetDrawDepth3D\(\)" to set the depth a body is drawn at when it has no transform object.
    (UUM-155044)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsBodyDefinition.drawTarget" and "PhysicsBodyDefinition.drawDepth" so a body can be created with its draw target and draw depth.
    (UUM-155044)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsConstants.MaxBounceIterations" and "PhysicsConstants.MinMaximumLinearSpeed".
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsDrawOverride" in "Unity.U2D.Physics.Editor", with "enabled", "settings", "defaultSettings", "changed", the "PhysicsDrawOverride.Settings" struct and the "PhysicsDrawOverride.OverrideOptions" flags, so editor tooling can read and write the Editor draw override.
    (UUM-157403)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsHingeJoint.currentSpeed", the speed body B is currently turning relative to body A.
    (UUM-158185)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsQuery.CircleAndCapsule", "PhysicsQuery.CircleAndSegment", "PhysicsQuery.CircleAndPolygon", "PhysicsQuery.CapsuleAndSegment", "PhysicsQuery.CapsuleAndPolygon" and "PhysicsQuery.PolygonAndSegment" so every pair of geometries can be queried in either order.
    ([UUM-157419](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157419))

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsShape.ContactFilterModeOverride", "PhysicsShape.ContactFilterGroupModeOverride", and the "contactFilterModeOverride" and "contactFilterGroupModeOverride" properties on "PhysicsWorld" and "PhysicsWorldDefinition", so each world can choose its own contact filter modes.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsShape.SetBatchDefinition" to set the same definition on a batch of shapes.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsShape.SetCallbackTarget", "PhysicsBody.SetCallbackTarget" and "PhysicsJoint.SetCallbackTarget" to set the same callback target on a batch of objects.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsShape.SetPhysicsGroup" to set the same physics group on a batch of shapes.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.ApplySnapshot", "PhysicsWorld.Create" and "PhysicsWorld.Clone" overloads with a "removeOwned" parameter that removes the owned bodies, shapes, chains and joints of the target world instead of refusing the snapshot.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.bounceIterations", "PhysicsWorld.bouncePropagation" and the matching "PhysicsWorldDefinition" properties, to control how bounces are resolved.
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.callbackTargetCount" to get the number of distinct callback targets in a world.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.disableDrawOverride" so a world always draws with its own draw settings while the Editor draw override is enabled.
    (UUM-157403)

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "PhysicsWorld.SceneLoadMode", "PhysicsWorld.sceneLoadMode" and "PhysicsCoreSettings2D.sceneLoadMode" to set whether a Single scene load destroys physics not owned by a DontDestroyOnLoad GameObject.

- Physics 2D: Added: Added "WorldProfile.bounceImpulses", also shown in the 2D Physics Profiler module.
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: Changed: Changed "PhysicsWorld.bounceIterations" and "PhysicsWorldDefinition.bounceIterations" to have a minimum of one, given by the new "PhysicsConstants.MinBounceIterations".

- Physics 2D: Changed: The batch and span setters on "PhysicsBody", "PhysicsShape" and "PhysicsJoint", along with "PhysicsWorld.Simulate", "PhysicsWorld.DrawShapes", "PhysicsSpace.SetBatchProxyAABB" and "PhysicsSpace.SetBatchProxyCategories", now return the number of items ignored because they were invalid and log a single warning when any are ignored.

- Physics 2D: Deprecated: Deprecated "PhysicsShape.ContactFilterGroupMode.Filtering" in favor of "PhysicsShape.ContactFilterGroupMode.Isolate".

- Physics 2D: Deprecated: Deprecated "PhysicsWorld.elementDepth" and "PhysicsWorld.SetElementDepth3D\(\)" in favor of "PhysicsWorld.drawDepth" and "PhysicsWorld.SetDrawDepth3D\(\)". The old names upgrade automatically.
    (UUM-155044)

- Physics 2D: Deprecated: Deprecated the "useScale" parameter of "PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix2D", which had no effect; use the overload without it, or "PhysicsMath.GetRelativePose2D" to build a matrix with the scale you need.
    ([UUM-157407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157407))

- Profiler: Deprecated: Marks `UnityEngine.Profiling.Recorder`, `Sampler` and `CustomSampler` `[Obsolete]` \(with warning\), in favour of `ProfilerRecorder` and `ProfilerMarker`. Also deprecated: `CommandBuffer.BeginSample/EndSample(CustomSampler)` and the matching overloads on the SRP-core command buffer wrappers \(`IBaseCommandBuffer`, `ComputeCommandBuffer`, `RasterCommandBuffer`, `UnsafeCommandBuffer`\).

- QNX: Added: Added DisplayInfo.RegisterLayoutChangedCallback and DisplayInfo.UnregisterLayoutChangedCallback, which notify the application when a display is connected, disconnected or reconfigured.

- Scripting: Changed: EditorSettings.forceAssetUnloadAndGCOnSceneLoad has been renamed to EditorSettings.unloadUnusedAssetsOnSceneLoad and no longer forces a managed garbage collection.

- Scripting: Deprecated: Deprecated `OSXStandalone.UserBuildSettings.architecture` and `OSArchitecture.x64ARM64`

- Serialization: Changed: Changed SerializedProperty.numericType to report the underlying integer width instead of Unknown for FileSize, Type*, and EntityId fields.

- Serialization: Changed: Changed SerializedProperty.propertyType to report Integer instead of Generic for fields whose serialized type is FileSize or Type*.

- Shaders: Added: Exposed additional resource binding information for shader compilation results.

- Shaders: Added: Shader.chunkEvictionStrategy can now be used to control how fast the unused shader variant chunks are unloaded.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.Experimental.Rendering.ShaderWarmup.WarmupShader method is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.Experimental.Rendering.ShaderWarmup.WarmupShaderFromCollection method is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.Experimental.Rendering.ShaderWarmupSetup struct is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.Shader.WarmupAllShaders method is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.ShaderVariantCollection.isWarmedUp property is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.ShaderVariantCollection.warmedUpVariantCount property is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.ShaderVariantCollection.WarmUp method is now deprecated.

- Shaders: Deprecated: UnityEngine.ShaderVariantCollection.WarmUpProgressively method is now deprecated.

- SRP Core: Added: Enabled Upscaling Framework by default.

- Terrain: Added: Added `TerrainData.maxHeightmapResolution`, `minAlphamapResolution`, `maxAlphamapResolution`, `minBaseMapResolution`, `maxBaseMapResolution`, `minDetailResolutionPerPatch` and `maxDetailResolutionPerPatch`, the limits that the resolution setters clamp values to.

- Terrain: Added: Added `TerrainRegion` and `TerrainEditUtility` to sculpt heights and paint terrain layers by height and slope inside a rectangle, disc or path from scripts, and to read the heightmap samples and alphamap texels such an area covers. They are designed for authoring.

- Test Framework: Removed: Removed the TestRunProfiler API from UnityEditor.TestTools.TestRunner.Api, which was added in an earlier 6000.7 pre-release.

- uGUI: Changed: Selectable.s_Selectables changed from a Selectable array to List&lt;Selectable&gt;.

- uGUI: Obsoleted: Selectable.s_SelectableCount is now obsolete. Use Selectable.allSelectableCount instead.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added LocalizedFontAsset, a localized reference to a FontAsset that lets UI Toolkit text change font with the locale.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added PanelSettings.filterTextureBudget, which sets the area of temporary textures that a panel can use at the same time to apply filters.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added PostProcessingPass.writeParametersCallback and FilterParameterWriter, so custom filter passes can write per-element parameters and be batched with GPU instancing.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added ScrollView.touchScrollDirection to make the content move opposite to the pointer when dragged with touch or a tracked device.
    ([UUM-139596](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139596))

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added the BoxShadow and StyleBoxShadow types, and the IStyle.boxShadow and IResolvedStyle.boxShadow properties.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added the PlayerLoop.PreUpdate.UIElementsPreparePanelRenderers player loop system, which attaches PanelRenderer content to its panel before Update.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `IFormattingExtensionsToggle` to Smart Strings, which lets a source skip formatter extensions for the placeholder it resolved.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `ParserSettings.SelectorCharFilter` and `SelectorFilterType` to Smart Strings. Set the filter to `VisualUnicodeChars` to use characters from any script in a selector.

- UI Toolkit: Added: Added `PluralRules.RestoreDefault` to Smart Strings, which restores the built-in pluralization rules.

- URP: Added: Enabled Upscaling Framework by default.

- Visual Scripting: Deprecated: Deprecated AotStubWriter.GetStubStatements and MethodInfoStubWriter.operatorTypes in favor of the new AotStubWriter.GetStubStatementsCode.

- Web: Deprecated: Deprecated `UnityEditor.WebGLLinkerTarget` and `WebGLWasmArithmeticExceptions` enums.

- Web: Obsoleted: Marked `PlayerSettings.WebGL.memorySize` as obsolete.

- Windows: Added: Added GameWindow API support for Windows.



#### Changes

- 2D: Fully enforce Sprite Frame editing capability. Overriding locks field are now removed.

- 2D: Updated the Universal 2D project template to include 2D Physics Core settings.

- Adaptive Performance: Adaptive Performance settings assets are now generated in Assets/Settings/AdaptivePerformance, and existing assets in Assets/Adaptive Performance are moved there automatically.

- Android: Removed support for Mono-based Android players

- Android: Renamed the "Build App Bundle \(Google Play\)" build setting to "Build App Bundle".
    (UUM-151967)

- Android: Updated the Android Gradle Plugin \(AGP\) version from 9.0.0 to 9.1.1.

- Animation: Removed support for loading animation clips saved before Unity 1.6; open and re-save such clips in an older version of Unity to keep their curves.

- Apple TV: Removed support for x64 and Universal Simulator architectures.

- Asset Import: Asset Import: Changed how Unity picks an asset's main object, so that objects an `AssetPostprocessor` adds with `AddObjectToAsset` no longer replaces the object the importer produces. To make an added object the main object, call `AssetImportContext.SetMainObject` in the postprocessor. Unity reimports all assets the first time you open an existing project, and some assets might have a different main object afterwards. Refer to the Unity 7.0 upgrade guide.
    ([UUM-153090](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153090))

- Asset Import: AssetImporterEditor.ApplyRevertButtonContainer now reports pending edits, including edits to extra data, through hasUnsavedChanges like ApplyRevertGUI, so a UI Toolkit importer editor that modifies its serializedObject in CreateInspectorGUI is reported as having unsaved changes.
    ([UUM-151732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151732))

- Asset Import: Projects that use AssetPostprocessors for textures, sprites, cameras, lights or scenes now reimport those assets once after upgrading, because the postprocessor dependency hash includes each postprocessor's assembly name.

- Asset Import: Reverting unapplied edits in an importer Inspector now also undoes import settings that a script changed and saved with AssetImporter.SaveAndReimport\(\) while those edits were pending.
    ([UUM-151732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151732))

- Asset Import: Updated the asset import pipeline, so Unity reimports all assets in a project the first time you open it in this version of the Editor.

- Build Pipeline: ContentBuildFlags.DevelopmentBuild has been removed. This flag was used to indicate whether content builds should serialize scripts with development build processor symbols. However, development builds no longer have any special preprocessor symbols and thus this flag did not do anything anymore.

- Build Pipeline: IProcessSceneWithReport and PostProcessSceneAttribute APIs are deprecated. Use AssetPostprocessor.OnProcessScene instead so that AssetImportContext and its built-in dependencies registration is available to better support incremental builds.

- Build System: bee_backend now requires glibc &gt;= 2.35 on linux \(ubuntu 22.04 or later\)

- Burst: Burst now compiles macOS players for Apple Silicon only, and the Burst AOT Settings no longer offer x64 CPU architectures for macOS.

- Burst: Changed Burst's default LLVM version to 23.

- Burst: Structs with auto-layout can now be used in Burst as long as they are not a part of a method's signature or a job's struct. This allows tuples to be used in structs that don't cross the managed-native barrier again.

- Code Stripping: Removed the Low option from the Managed Stripping Level Player setting. Projects that use Low are upgraded to Minimal when opened, which might increase build size.

- Core: Update the native Selection API to use ObjectPtr instead of ObjectRef.

- CoreCLR: Environment.Exit\(\) now throws a PlatformNotSupportedException on the CoreCLR scripting backend. Use Application.Quit\(\) instead.

- CoreCLR: Removed experimental label from CoreCLR

- Documentation: Update iOS documentation about On Demand Resources limitations on iOS platform

- Editor: Changed the Editor preferences folder to Editor-7.x, which is seeded on first launch by copying the existing Editor-5.x folder so that earlier Editor versions keep their own preferences.

- Editor: Changed the Project Auditor Suppressed Issues preference to also accept spaces as a delimiter between issue IDs, in addition to commas and semicolons.

- Editor: Changing the Editor theme, Editor font, Editor language to now prompts for restart the Editor.

- Editor: Fast Enter Play Mode is now the default for projects created with the "-createProject" editor command line argument, from script code and for projects without a `ProjectSettings/EditorSettings.asset` file.  Affected projects will now default to the "Reload Scene Only" option when entering and exiting playmode in the editor rather than "Reload Domain and Scene". Existing projects are unaffected.<br>
    For more information see the online documentation:<br>
    - \[Configuring how Unity enters Play mode\]\(https://docs.unity3d.com/7000.0/Documentation/Manual/configurable-enter-play-mode.html\)<br>
    - \[Enter Play mode without domain reload\]\(https://docs.unity3d.com/7000.0/Documentation/Manual/domain-reloading.html\)<br>
    - \[Domain and scene reload execution order reference\]\(https://docs.unity3d.com/7000.0/Documentation/Manual/configurable-enter-play-mode-details.html\).

- Editor: Meshes as part of the built in resources now come with colliders attached.

- Editor: Moved Transparency Sort settings out of the Built-In tab in Graphics settings.

- Editor: Project Auditor: Removed Additional Insights from the Optimization Summary page.

- Editor: Removed macOS x64 Editor and Player support.

- Editor: Removed support for Mono-based Editor

- Editor: Shortcut actions and global input handlers now run outside of OnGUI, so Event.current is null inside them; use ShortcutArguments.sourceEvent to read the triggering event.
    (UUM-153126)

- Editor: The Linux editor now requires OpenSSL \(either version 3.X or 4.X\) to be installed on the system. It is installed by default on all of our officially-supported distributions, so no action should be required for most users.

- Editor: The macOS character palette shortcut now only opens the palette while a text field is being edited.

- EmbeddedLinux: GameWindow.MoveTo and window creation positions are now measured from the top-left corner of the display, the same origin as DisplayInfo.workArea, instead of from the top-left corner of the work area.

- Engine Diagnostics: Development IL2CPP builds for macOS, Windows, Linux, QNX and Embedded Linux no longer generate or upload crash report symbols when Engine Diagnostics is enabled, unless Cloud Diagnostics crash reporting is also enabled. This reduces build times.
    (UUM-159611)

- Engine Diagnostics: Diagnostic Data Collection is now enabled by default for every build profile, including in upgraded projects, unless a build profile explicitly disables it.

- Engine Diagnostics: Diagnostic data is no longer collected in the Editor when the active build profile targets a platform that doesn't support Diagnostics.

- Engine Diagnostics: Editor crash and exception reporting to Diagnostics now follows the Diagnostic Data Collection setting of the active build profile.

- Engine Diagnostics: Removed the Diagnostics page from Project Settings &gt; Services. Diagnostic Data Collection is now configured for each build profile in the Build Profiles window, and Project Settings &gt; Services describes the diagnostic data that Unity collects.

- Engine Diagnostics: The Diagnostic Data Collection setting in the Build Profiles window now offers Enabled and Disabled, and disabling it no longer asks for confirmation.

- Entities: Changed the **Open in Hierarchy** item in the Entity Inspector component menu to open the Hierarchy window filtered by the component type, and removed the **Open in Search Window** item.

- Entities: EntityManager.CopyEntitiesFrom no longer makes structural changes in the source world.

- Entities: GameObjects now have entities with chunk storage in the active World, so entity queries match them like any other entity.

- Entities: Remove the obsolete HierarchyColor field in Subscene

- Entities: Removed the deprecated Entities Hierarchy window. Use the Hierarchy window to view and search entities instead.

- Entities: Removed the Entities package dependency on the Audio module. Projects that use Entities can now remove the Audio module.

- Entities: Renamed the blob asset analyzer diagnostics EA0001, EA0002, EA0003 and EA0009 to UAC0040, UAC0041, UAC0042 and UAC0043, which now also check code in the Entities engine module.

- Entities: The Unity.Entities runtime now ships in the engine as UnityEngine.EntitiesModule. Its public types are forwarded from the Unity.Entities package assembly, so existing code keeps compiling.

- Entities: UnityObjectRef&lt;T&gt; now has a public EntityId property and a FromEntityId factory method.

- Entities: When a world has several managed systems of the same type, GetExistingSystemManaged and GetOrCreateSystemManaged now return the most recently created one, matching ISystem lookups.

- GI: Bake Reflection Probes no longer bakes Custom reflection probes, because it replaced their cubemap with a generated one. To bake a Custom probe, use the Bake button in its Inspector.
    ([UUM-141083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141083))

- GI: Lightmapping.BakeReflectionProbe now returns false and logs an error for Realtime reflection probes instead of writing a cubemap.
    ([UUM-141083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141083))

- GI: Realtime Reflection Probes no longer use a baked fallback cubemap when Realtime Reflection Probes is disabled in Quality settings, including cubemaps baked before a probe was switched to Realtime.

- GI: Removed the Progressive CPU Lightmapper and the Lightmapper dropdown from the Lighting window; the lightmapper now follows the Default Light Baker setting in the Graphics settings, and scenes that used the Progressive CPU Lightmapper are automatically upgraded to the Progressive GPU Lightmapper.

- GI: Surface Cache Global Illumination debug views are now displayed as a resizable Rendering Debugger overlay and no longer replace the scene's global illumination.

- Graphics: Added an error banner in the main window if the project is using Built-in Render Pipeline

- Graphics: Camera.layerCullSpherical no longer logged a warning when a Scriptable Render Pipeline was active, because Scriptable Render Pipelines already supported the property.

- Graphics: Changed AsyncGPUReadback to reject a readback whose destination NativeArray is still in use by a running job, and report the error through the callback instead of proceeding.

- Graphics: Changing the index format of a mesh through Mesh.indexFormat now resets Mesh LOD count to 1.
    ([UUM-159092](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159092))

- Graphics: Deprecated the Baked Occlusion Culling authoring workflow in the Editor. New projects no longer expose its authoring UI, while existing projects retain the workflow and baked data and display a deprecation notice. Consider migrating to GPU Occlusion Culling where supported.

- Graphics: Disabled Built-in Render Pipeline rendering paths, render black instead.

- Graphics: GraphicsStateCollection.completedWarmupCount and isWarmedUp now include compute shader variants, so the warmup total is totalGraphicsStateCount plus computeVariantCount.

- Graphics: Player builds now fail with an error if any Render Pipeline Assets assigned in Graphic Settings or Quality Settings is missing.

- Graphics: Removed obsolete Built-in Render Pipeline components and shaders from UI, and marked them not supported in the inspector

- Graphics: Sprite Renderer no longer applies GPU blend shapes when Mesh Deformation is set to CPU in Player Settings.

- HDRP: Removed a warning displayed at build time in specific XR setup that is breaking build determinism for Content Directory builds.

- Input: A sensor whose controls are all noisy no longer becomes the current device of its type when another one is current. On Android devices with both rotation vectors, AttitudeSensor.current is the game rotation vector, because it is the only one with a control that isn't noisy.

- Input System: A Value action bound to a whole touch control, such as &lt;Touchscreen&gt;/primaryTouch, is now canceled when the touch ends instead of being performed with the Ended phase.

- iOS: On an unfolded iPhone Duo, `Display.main` was the outer panel instead of the panel the game renders on, so rendering to Display 2 replaced the game and `Screen.fullScreen` returned false.
    (PLAT-22932)

- iOS: Remove public keyword from topMostController variable as it is not part of public API

- iOS: Removed support for x64 and Universal Simulator architectures.
