- Kernel: Changed NativeList and UnsafeList to preserve only the first Length elements when capacity grows. Data written beyond Length through the raw pointer is no longer retained across a reallocation.
    ([UUM-129943](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-129943))

- Linux: Removed the license type from the Unity Editor window title on Linux, so that it matches the window title on macOS and Windows.

- Multiplayer: Changed the Multiplayer Center genre selection screen to show the game genres side by side as cards, each with its architecture, complexity, and cost, so they can be compared at a glance.

- Multiplayer: The Multiplayer Play Mode window now warns that it will be removed in Unity 7.1 and offers a one-click conversion of the current configuration to a Play Mode Scenario, with a link to the documentation.

- Networking: Removed `UnityWebRequest.enableProfilerBodyCapture`. The Web Requests Profiler module now captures request and response bodies whenever the Profiler is recording, so there is no longer a setting to turn on. Remove any assignment to this property; no replacement is needed.
    (UUM-153039)

- Package: NGO \(com.unity.netcode.gameobjects\) package was updated in the editor manifest to newest major version \(3.0.0\).

- Package: The content of com.unity.collections has been moved to an engine module, and the package is just a shim with typeforwarders, so it no longer has any dependencies. If you were relying on it having them, you'll need to depend on them yourself explicitly \(most notably, com.unity.test-framework\).

- Package Manager: Changed Package Manager to use the production environment when the Editor's cloud environment is set to a value other than production or staging.

- Package Manager: Changed the separator between the version and release date in the Package Manager details header from a dot to a vertical bar.

- Package Manager: Marked the WebGL Publisher package \(com.unity.connect.share\) as deprecated. Use Publish to Play in the Web Build Profile to upload Web builds to Unity Play.

- Performance Test Framework: PerformanceTestRunInfo.json and PerformanceTestRunSettings.json are no longer generated during player builds that do not include test assemblies, as they are only read by performance tests running inside the player. Stale files left behind by an aborted test build are removed before such builds instead of being included in them.

- Physics 2D: "ManifoldPoint.normalVelocity" is now only guaranteed to be measured when the shape is bouncy or hit events are enabled, and can read zero otherwise.
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsComposer.AddLayer", "PhysicsComposer.ToPolygons", "CircleGeometry.ToPolygons" and "CapsuleGeometry.ToPolygons" now log the standard validation warning instead of throwing "ArgumentOutOfRangeException" when given no geometry, fewer than three vertices or an invalid "curveStride".
    ([UUM-156367](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156367))

- Physics 2D: A contact cancelled by a pre-contact callback is now updated every simulation step, so the callback is called every step while the shapes stay in contact.
    (UUM-158185)

- Physics 2D: Changed the default "PhysicsShapeDefinition" to enable "contactEvents" and "triggerEvents", and the default "PhysicsChainDefinition" to enable "triggerEvents", so a shape only needs "isTrigger" enabled to detect other shapes.
    (UUM-158710)

- Physics 2D: Contact and hinge joint solving were restructured, so simulation results can differ slightly from previous versions.
    (UUM-158185)

- Physics 2D: Polygons created from vertices now keep vertices that are much closer together before welding them, and "PhysicsWorld.minPolygonEdgeLength" now reports this smaller distance.
    (UUM-158185)

- Physics 2D: Remove deprecated BIRP support for PhysicsCore2D renderer.

- Physics 2D: Setting "PhysicsWorld.maximumLinearSpeed" or "PhysicsWorldDefinition.maximumLinearSpeed" below "PhysicsConstants.MinMaximumLinearSpeed" now raises it to that minimum.
    (UUM-154721)

- Prefabs: Changed Apply Selected in the Prefab Overrides window to apply either all selected overrides or none, and to show a dialog explaining why when they can't be applied.

- Prefabs: Changed PrefabUtility.prefabInstanceApplying and PrefabUtility.prefabInstanceApplied to be sent once per Apply Selected instead of once per applied override.

- Prefabs: Loading a prefab or scene that stores prefab data in the format written by Unity 3.3 and earlier now logs a deprecation warning naming the asset. Save the asset to store it in the current format.

- Prefabs: Removed the upgrade path for prefab data saved by Unity 3.3 or earlier, so prefab instances and per-object overrides in projects that old no longer survive being opened. Unity 6.7 warns when it loads an asset in that format.

- Profiler: Changed "App Committed Memory" counter to return a sum of resident and swapped memory on Android
    (UUM-155041)

- Profiler: Changed the CPU Usage Profiler module to show the Jobs Profiler timeline by default. The previous timeline is available as Old Timeline \(Legacy\) in the view dropdown.

- Scene/Game View: Replaced the Game view Low Resolution Aspect Ratios toggle with a Pixel Size setting \(1x, 2x, 3x\) so pixel art stays sharp on fractional display scales. On high-DPI displays, Free Aspect and aspect-ratio sizes now default to whole-pixel scaling, which makes Screen.width and Screen.height smaller than before.

- Scripting: Changed the Editor so that it emits C\# compiler warnings for immutable packages, such as packages installed from a package registry. Warnings from these packages now appear in the Console window and the Editor log.

- Scripting: Removed forced managed GC after a \(single-mode\) scene load in the CoreCLR Player.

- Scripting: Removed forced managed GC after a scene load in the CoreCLR Editor.

- Scripting: UNITY_64 preprocessor symbol no longer gets defined when building for any platform.

- Scripting: `DEVELOPMENT_BUILD` preprocessor symbol no longer gets defined when building for any platform. Preprocessor symbols `DEBUG`, `ENABLE_ASSERTIONS` and `ENABLE_PROFILER` now only get defined in appropriate managed code variants \(debug, checked and instrumented, respectively\). `BuildOptions.ForceEnableAssertions`, `CodeAnalysisFlags.DevelopmentBuild`, `ScriptCompilationOptions.DevelopmentBuild`, `ScriptCompilationOptions.Assertions` and `AssemblyBuilderFlags.DevelopmentBuild` have been removed: script compilation is now only affected by the managed code variant.

- Serialization: Changed text serialized files so they begin with a %UNITY format version directive, which makes an earlier version of Unity report an explicit error when opening a file saved in this version instead of loading it with missing data.

- Serialization: Text serialized files such as scenes and prefabs no longer start with a %UNITY 7.0 line, so Unity 6 can open them again, and files saved with that line still load.

- Shadergraph: Reflected function fallback keys now use the fully qualified signature \(this will not impact correct usage of the ProviderKey hint\).

- Shadergraph: Removed the Back To Front and Front To Back Render Face options from URP Shader Graphs. Shader Graphs and materials saved with these options now render front faces only.

- Shaders: Surface shader support is now disabled.

- Shaders: Surface shaders are now marked as deprecated.

- SRP Core: `UnityEditor.Rendering.CoreBuildData.developmentBuild` is now an error to use instead of a warning. It was already obsolete since Unity 6.6 and always returned `false`. Use `UnityEditor.Rendering.CoreBuildData.useDiagnosticChecks` instead.

- Test Framework: Fixed Is.Not.AllocatingGCMemory\(\) under-reporting allocations when another allocation constraint runs inside the measured delegate.

- Text: Updated FreeType to version 2.15.0, which adds support for mirrored Apple Color Emoji glyphs on macOS and slightly changes how text renders in the Hinted Smooth and OS Default font rendering modes.

- TextMeshPro: Marked the project-wide Use Advanced Text setting as Experimental and added a note that it applies to every TextMesh Pro component.
    (UUM-160511)

- TextMeshPro: The URP sample template no longer includes a copy of the TextMesh Pro essential resources and uses the built-in resources from the uGUI package instead.

- uGUI: Changed Content Size Fitter in Clamped mode to keep a size larger than the content on a Horizontal or Vertical Layout Group with Control Child Size off, instead of shrinking the group to fit its children.
    ([UUM-159240](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159240))

- uGUI: Moved the UI/Lit shaders to the Universal Render Pipeline \(URP\) package. They now require URP. Existing materials switch to the URP versions automatically. The Refraction shaders need Opaque Texture enabled on the URP asset. UI/Lit doesn't support the 2D Renderer or HDRP.

- UI: Hide BiRP tab unless project uses it

- UI Toolkit: Built-in USS filter functions with missing or wrong-typed arguments now use the filter's default values and log a warning instead of an error.
    (UUM-155773)

- UI Toolkit: Changed Constant Physical Size panels to account for the Game view's Low Resolution Aspect Ratios setting when resolving scale.

- UI Toolkit: Changed theme style sheets to rank above Unity's built-in styles regardless of selector specificity; the previous behavior can be restored with the Legacy Theme Priority setting in Project Settings &gt; UI Toolkit.

- UI Toolkit: Changed touch dragging of ScrollView content on world-space panels so the content follows the finger pixel for pixel, regardless of the Pixels Per Unit setting.
    ([UUM-139596](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139596))

- UI Toolkit: Changed z-index transitions between auto and an integer to switch at the midpoint of the transition instead of interpolating from 0, to match CSS.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed grid line placement and -unity-slice properties not recording or playing back in UI Animation Clips.

- UI Toolkit: Importing localization shared table data that has two entries using the same key now renames the duplicate and logs a warning.
    ([UUM-151766](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151766))

- UI Toolkit: Made the UI Builder adopt workflows from the in-scene authoring, including the ability to set a PanelSettings asset while editing.

- UI Toolkit: The Editor theme style sheet is now applied to the root of the Editor window panel instead of EditorWindow.rootVisualElement, so it no longer appears in rootVisualElement.styleSheets.

- UI Toolkit: The UI Builder canvas now zooms to fit the viewport and grows to its content the first time a document opens.
    (UUM-157282)

- UI Toolkit: UI Toolkit runtime panels no longer discard input events when the application is not focused; whether input is delivered while unfocused is now controlled by the input settings.

- Unity Physics: Changed how the batching runs for the completion of any remaining DebugDraw jobs still running from the previous frame in, before the streams are cleared for re-use. This may improve performance when using DebugDraw
    (UUM-152608)

- Universal Windows Platform: In IL2CPP builds, the `il2cpp_data` folder has been moved out from the Data folder as it is now architecture specific. It now lives next to the executable.

- URP: Moved Light Falloff Mode from the URP Asset to Graphics Settings.

- URP: Removed a warning displayed at build time in specific Light setup that is breaking build determinism for Content Directory builds.

- URP: Removed a warning displayed at build time in specific XR setup that is breaking build determinism for Content Directory builds.

- URP: Removed the urp_ReflProbes_Count shader variable, which no URP shader reads. Custom shaders that read it no longer compile.

- URP: Renamed the Light Falloff Mode option from Linear to Quadratic.

- Windows: In IL2CPP builds, the `il2cpp_data` folder has been moved out from the Data folder as it is now architecture specific. It now lives next to the executable, similar to the Managed folder in CoreCLR builds.

- XR: Added XR Foundation, a new package to replace XR Plug-in Management and XR Core Utils.

- XR: `UnityEngine.XR.InputDevices.deviceDisconnected` and `UnityEngine.XR.InputDevices.deviceConnected`, and legacy `XRNode` tracking-lost notifications, no longer fire across an application pause/resume for a device that is still present. `InputDevice` handles now stay valid across pause/resume instead of going stale. If you used those callbacks to re-acquire handles or re-apply device state on resume, use `OnApplicationPause` or `Application.focusChanged` instead.



#### Fixes

- 2D: Changes to a Light2D's custom material are now applied to the lights that use it, without a domain reload or entering Play mode.
    ([UUM-152882](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152882))

- 2D: Fix ArgumentException when using overlay camera in 2D Renderer.
    ([UUM-148111](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148111))

- 2D: Fix case where crash with multiple stack traces when Sprites with a high resolution get Sliced to a Grid with a small cell size in the Sprite Editor
    ([UUM-146158](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146158))

- 2D: Fix case where ideo is not rendered when the VideoPlayer GameObject is disabled and re-enabled after playback starts while using Material Override render mode
    ([UUM-141113](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141113))

- 2D: Fix Crash on SetupMaskingStencilState when rendering a SpriteRenderer whose "maskInteraction" is set to an out-of-range value
    ([UUM-159076](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159076))

- 2D: Fix crash on SpriteAtlas::GetSpritesLegacy when GetSprite is called on a Sprite Atlas V1 Variant from a project saved in Unity 6.5 or earlier
    ([UUM-158424](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158424))

- 2D: Fix crash when a Sprite is destroyed while it is being bound to a Sprite Atlas and notifying its users
    (UUM-156681)

- 2D: Fix intermediate texture not created when requested by render pass
    ([UUM-145892](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145892))

- 2D: Fix issues where Unsaved Changes Detected pop-up are doubled after editing Sprite Atlas asset if it was open on multiple Inspector windows and choosing the Discard option
    ([UUM-151573](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151573))

- 2D: Fix section headers for 2D/Grid Brush and 2D/Tile Palette preferences
    ([UUM-149246](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149246))

- 2D: Fixed "Strip 2D Unused Variants" stripping a Light 2D shader variant that is still in use, which made some 2D lights render magenta in Player builds when another light was culled.
    ([UUM-154779](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154779))

- 2D: Fixed 2D Light shadows missing from a player built with `BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer` when the scene is not in the Build Profiles scene list and Strip Unused 2D Variants is on.
    ([UUM-160655](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160655))

- 2D: Fixed 2D lights changing position and orientation when rotating an Android device in projects built with Vulkan and Apply display rotation during rendering enabled.
    ([UUM-147359](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147359))

- 2D: Fixed 2D projected shadows not rendering on some platforms
    (UUM-151894)

- 2D: Fixed a "No script asset for ShadowCasterPath" warning when you undo or redo a Shadow Caster 2D shape edit in Play mode.
    ([UUM-152935](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152935))

- 2D: Fixed a crash that could occur when a Sprite was destroyed while the render thread was still uploading its vertex buffer.
    (UUM-151625)

- 2D: Fixed a crash when a Tilemap Prefab whose Scene instance has tile overrides was updated outside the Editor.
    ([UUM-157167](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157167))

- 2D: Fixed a crash when entering Play Mode with a Tilemap whose tiles re-enter the Tilemap API during StartUp.
    ([UUM-153542](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153542))

- 2D: Fixed a Multiply 2D Light rendering brighter on OpenGLCore with NVIDIA GPUs when another 2D Light in view used the Additive Blend Style.
    ([UUM-161536](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161536))

- 2D: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown every frame by a 2D Light of type Provider whose Light2DProvider returns no mesh.
    ([UUM-153965](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153965))

- 2D: Fixed a NullReferenceException when a Light 2D created from a script is set to Freeform without a shape path.
    ([UUM-154810](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154810))

- 2D: Fixed a performance regression in Tilemap.SetTile and other small tile batches.
    (UUM-153709)

- 2D: Fixed an "ArgumentException: Scene file not found" error when building with a deleted scene in the build list and Strip 2D Unused Variants enabled.
    ([UUM-154789](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154789))

- 2D: Fixed an "Index buffer element is out of bounds" error that removed a Shadow Caster 2D's shadow when its outline had a sub-shape with fewer than three edges and 2D Shadow Geometry was set to Enhanced.
    ([UUM-152375](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152375))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where a LineRenderer or TrailRenderer with Mask Interaction set to None was clipped by a Sprite Mask when another LineRenderer or TrailRenderer using the same material had Mask Interaction enabled.
    ([UUM-158602](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158602))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where a Point Light 2D lost its cookie sprite when a project saved in Unity 6.0 or 6.3 was opened in a newer version.
    ([UUM-156931](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156931))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where a Tile's StartUp method modified the tile's prefab asset when five or more tiles were refreshed.
    ([UUM-157629](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157629))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where a Tilemap did not instantiate the GameObjects of its tiles on every other scene load or Play mode entry.
    ([UUM-155958](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155958))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where cancelling animation creation after dragging multiple sprites onto a GameObject in the Hierarchy removed its existing Animator component.
    (UUM-155475)

- 2D: Fixed an issue where creating a Tile from the Tile Palette failed when the destination folder name contained a dot.
    ([UUM-158114](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158114))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where dragging the Sprite Editor Window Zoom Level range in Preferences could set the minimum zoom below 50%.
    ([UUM-153718](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153718))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where Light2D and Shadow2D Shader Graph assets could not be dragged onto the Light 2D Material and Shadow Material fields.
    ([UUM-153102](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153102))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where lowering the maximum zoom level in the Sprite Editor Window preferences allowed the Sprite Editor to zoom out beyond the minimum zoom level.
    ([UUM-153725](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153725))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where rotating a multi-layer brush on a Hexagon Tilemap merged its Z layers and lost tiles.
    ([UUM-156684](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156684))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where the Shadow Displacement port on the Shadow Caster 2D Shader Graph target was read in the caster's local space instead of world space.
    (UUM-152815)

- 2D: Fixed an issue where the Sprite Editor Window Zoom Level range in Preferences stayed wider after being dragged to the lower end and back.
    ([UUM-153720](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153720))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where the Tile Palette created Tile assets instead of EntityIdTile assets when the Create Tile Method preference was set to DefaultEntityIdTile.
    ([UUM-157610](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157610))

- 2D: Fixed an issue where undoing an erased Tile did not restore the Tile's GameObject.
    ([UUM-158004](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158004))

- 2D: Fixed child GameObjects of a Tile's instantiated GameObject remaining in the Scene after exiting Play mode or reloading the Scene.
    ([UUM-151719](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151719))

- 2D: Fixed crash in SpriteShapeUtility::PrepareSpriteShapeInputs when AngleRangeInfo.sprites contains an invalid index<br>
    Note: Spriteshape package uses its own internal SpriteshapeGenerator and does not use UnityEngine.U2D.SpriteShapeUtility:Generate anymore. This will soon be deprecated.
    ([UUM-156970](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156970))

- 2D: Fixed Crash on SpriteAtlas::GetSpritesScripting when passing null to SpriteAtlas.GetSprites.
    ([UUM-155330](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155330))

- 2D: Fixed duplicate global light error when building a player.
    (UUM-153879)

- 2D: Fixed editor crash in SpriteDataAccessExtensions::SetVertexCount when the count is negative.
    ([UUM-154126](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154126))

- 2D: Fixed four errors logged every frame for each Light 2D that uses a custom Material whose shader does not declare the 2D lighting keywords.
    ([UUM-152368](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152368))

- 2D: Fixed Freeform and Sprite lights that use a Light2D Shader Graph material rendering too dark under the Alpha Blend overlap operation when HDR Emulation Scale is above 1.
    ([UUM-161407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161407))

- 2D: Fixed GridLayout.GetBoundsLocal\(Vector3Int\) returning bounds that were not centered on the cell.
    ([UUM-156212](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156212))

- 2D: Fixed inconsistent icon sharpness in the Tile Palette window.
    ([UUM-151071](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151071))

- 2D: Fixed IndexOutOfRangeException errors thrown while editing a Freeform Light 2D shape whose edges overlap.
    ([UUM-153123](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153123))

- 2D: Fixed Light2D Shader Graphs gaining extra Normal, Tangent and Base Color blocks after the Material was changed in the Shader Graph Inspector.
    ([UUM-153138](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153138))

- 2D: Fixed Render Objects feature failing when depth buffer is disabled.
    ([UUM-153342](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153342))

- 2D: Fixed shadow capsules using each other's end radius when a shadow edge runs from right to left.
    ([UUM-161210](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161210))

- 2D: Fixed Shadow Caster 2D so that the shape outline is drawn in the Scene view for every Casting Option, including Self Shadow and No Shadow.
    ([UUM-152389](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152389))

- 2D: Fixed shadows from a Shadow Caster 2D that uses a Collider 2D \(for example a Tilemap Collider 2D\) as its casting source flickering in the Scene and Game views.
    ([UUM-147033](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147033))

- 2D: Fixed skinned sprites using different textures rendering with the same texture on WebGL when the SRP Batcher is enabled.
    (UUM-153147)

- 2D: Fixed slow Apply in the Sprite Editor on textures with many sprites.
    (UUM-154623)

- 2D: Fixed Sprite Renderer reporting itself as SRP Batcher compatible while a Material Property Block was set on it, which kept Sprite Skins on GPU deformation.
    (UUM-155079)

- 2D: Fixed sprites and UI not rendering on some OpenGL ES devices when the 2D Renderer Depth/Stencil Buffer setting is disabled.
    ([UUM-159093](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159093))

- 2D: Fixed the active Brush changing to the last selected Brush Pick when the Brush Pick filter was toggled on and off.
    ([UUM-151716](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151716))

- 2D: Fixed the camera sorting layer texture resolution being affected by the Render Scale setting on the 2D Renderer.
    ([UUM-151723](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151723))

- 2D: Fixed the Grid Selection Offset, Rotation and Scale fields in the Inspector not updating while dragging a Grid Selection gizmo in the Scene view.
    ([UUM-151310](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151310))

- 2D: Fixed the Grid Selection transform and color fields accepting input that was silently discarded when the selection contains no Tiles. The fields, selection tools and Delete Selection button are now disabled in that case.
    ([UUM-152962](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152962))

- 2D: Fixed the Open Tile Palette button and Tilemap Focus dropdown not filling the full width of their Scene view overlay.
    ([UUM-151417](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151417))

- 2D: Fixed the Shadow Caster 2D Casting Source dropdown showing Sprite Renderer while the shadow used the Shape Editor shape, when the Sprite Renderer was added after the Shadow Caster 2D.
    (UUM-158120)

- 2D: Fixed the Sync Active Palette button in the Tile Palette window being cut off or hidden when the window is resized horizontally.
    ([UUM-152530](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152530))

- 2D: Fixed the Tile Palette Brush Pick Overlay scrolling back to the selected Pick when you resize the Picks.
    ([UUM-147199](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147199))

- 2D: Fixed Tile Palette automatic cell size using the largest Sprite size when Sprite sizes are exact multiples of the smallest Sprite size, up to 64 times.
    ([UUM-157615](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157615))

- 2D: Fixed Tilemap Scene data changing on every save when a Tilemap contains tiles without Lock Color or Lock Transform, such as Rule Tiles.
    ([UUM-158436](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158436))

- 2D: Fixed Tilemap tile data potentially being copied incorrectly when refreshing tiles.
    (UUM-151956)

- 2D: Fixed Tilemap.DeleteCells shrinking the Tilemap bounds by one cell too many when deleting cells at the upper end of the bounds.
    ([UUM-158617](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158617))

- 2D: Fixed Tiles in the Tile Palette window rendering in solid grey after exiting Play mode when Domain Reload is disabled.
    ([UUM-152499](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152499))

- 2D: Fixed Unstable Test.
    (UUM-153644)

- 2D: Fixed URP 2D editor-only icons being included in player builds.
    (UUM-144935)

- 2D: Fixed `Graphics.RenderSpriteInstanced` drawing the Sprite's own texture instead of a `_MainTex` set in the `MaterialPropertyBlock` of `RenderParams`.
    ([UUM-158701](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158701))

- 2D: Fixed `Graphics.RenderSprite` ignoring the `MaterialPropertyBlock` of `RenderParams` when a sprite mask interaction is set.

- 2D: Fixes instanced sprite rendering producing incorrect results when a custom `MaterialPropertyBlock` is applied to a `SpriteRenderer`.
    (UUM-147992)

- 2D: Fixes LineRenderer not initializing correctly when toggling enable on game object.
    ([UUM-147747](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147747))

- 2D: Inspector - Unsaved Changes Detected pop-up shows for each Inspector window when editing Sprite Atlas asset
    ([UUM-151568](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151568))

- 2D: Platform specific overrides panel for Sprite Atlas asset does not support Undo or Redo operations
    ([UUM-151570](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151570))

- 2D: Reduced CPU cost of 2D lights and shadow casters that do not use a custom material.
    (UUM-154097)

- 2D: The Console now shows the "More than one global light on layer" error when a 2D Light is set to Global in the Light Explorer.
    ([UUM-144607](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144607))

- 2D: The Light 2D inspector now shows an error on Freeform lights when the 2D Common package \(13.0.0 or newer\) is not installed, since only the light's falloff edge renders without it.
    ([UUM-157159](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157159))

- 2D: The Search window no longer renders blank for a few seconds the first time it opens.
    ([UUM-153914](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153914))

- 2D: Tilemap: Fixed Tilemap.RefreshAllTiles calling TileBase.RefreshTile twice per Tile on Tilemaps with five or more Tiles.
    ([UUM-157402](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157402))

- 2D: Undo and Redo now fully revert a pick in the Shadow Caster 2D Casting Source dropdown and the Light 2D Light Type dropdown, so the inspector shows the right fields.
    ([UUM-152952](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152952))

- 2D: Update error log when accessing index out of bounds meaningful.
    ([UUM-154148](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154148))

- Accessibility: Fixed the Windows player crashing on quit when a UI Automation client \(e.g. a screen reader\) is running.
    ([UUM-157903](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157903))

- Adaptive Performance: fix the text overlapping when scrolling up the profile area.
    (UUM-152014)

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed Adaptive Performance samples in XR so the user's camera faces the sample content.

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed Adaptive Performance settings being deleted from a Build Profile when an invalid clipboard payload was pasted into them.
    ([UUM-152371](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152371))

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed indexer logic to handle when low utilization and thermal values cause performanceAction to become StateAction.Increase, indicating we have headroom to increase quality.

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed pasting a Build Profile with Adaptive Performance disabled not clearing the Adaptive Performance settings of the profile it was pasted into.
    ([UUM-152504](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152504))

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed pasting a clipboard that holds no Adaptive Performance settings into a Build Profile silently doing nothing instead of reporting that the clipboard cannot be pasted.
    ([UUM-152505](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152505))

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed pasting a modified clipboard payload into a Build Profile adding an unrelated ScriptableObject as a hidden sub-asset and removing the existing Adaptive Performance settings.
    ([UUM-152376](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152376))

- Adaptive Performance: Fixed the add and remove buttons in Adaptive Performance scaler profiles overlapping the scaler list in Project Settings and Build Profiles.
    (UUM-154159)

- AI: Fixed a crash in the Editor and development builds when an Experimental.AI.NavMeshQuery created with Allocator.Temp was disposed after its NavMesh had been destroyed.

- AI: Fixed a crash when a copy of a NavQueryBuffer was used for a pathfinding operation after the buffer it was copied from had been disposed. In the Editor and in development builds such a copy now reports itself as disposed, the same as a buffer that was disposed in place.
    (UUM-149883)

- AI: Fixed NavWorld.GetAgentTypeIdForNode returning 0, the ID of the Humanoid agent type, instead of -1 for a null NavNode.
    ([UUM-160622](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160622))

- AI: Fixed safety checks on disposed `NavQueryBuffer` instances so that they always throw `ObjectDisposedException` and never throw `InvalidOperationException`.
    (UUM-161266)

- AI: Fixed the order of the two portal points returned for a NavMesh link, so that the first one is on the left and the second on the right also for a path that leaves the link. This applies to the results of `NavWorld.GetPortalPoints` and `NavMeshQuery.GetPortalPoints`.
    ([UUM-152707](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152707))

- AI: The `sourceBounds` from a NavMeshData object generated far from origin were incorrectly including the origin. These bounds now wrap tightly around the meshes used for building the NavMesh.
    ([UUM-152672](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152672))

- Android: Bouncy Castle upgrade \(from 1.70 to 1.86\). New signing keys created by the Keystore Manager use 4096-bit RSA and a SHA256withRSA certificate, instead of 2048-bit RSA and SHA1withRSA. Existing keys are not changed.
    (UUM-156925)

- Android: Fix "Failed to find target with hash string android-37" when Target API Level 37 is selected.
    ([UUM-150216](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150216))

- Android: fix SIGSEGV on Application.Quit\(\) when the cursor was ever set
    ([UUM-153932](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153932))

- Android: Fixed a crash when an app is relaunched with `FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK` while it is already running.
    ([UUM-158675](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158675))

- Android: Fixed a false-positive Java heap leak failure in the Android player-unloading integration test by excluding first-reload warmup from the baseline.
    (UUM-150246)

- Android: Fixed an exception when exporting an Android project with the Visual Studio build system while the project contains Android libraries.

- Android: Fixed an issue on OpenGL where Unity's render thread would attach/detach to the JVM multiple times in a frame, causing unnecessary allocations.
    (UUM-155309)

- Android: Fixed an issue where pressing the Back key twice was required to fully close the touch screen keyboard and its input field on Android 17 when Predictive Back support was disabled.
    (UUM-153547)

- Android: Fixed an issue where the application didn't rotate to the orientation the user locked the screen to, when Default Orientation was set to Auto Rotation and Auto Rotation Behavior was set to User.
    (UUM-158455)

- Android: Fixed Android build failure messages exposing sensitive environment variables. Now only build-related variables are shown.
    (UUM-157450)

- Android: Fixed Android native libraries having a GNU_RELRO segment that was not aligned to 16 KB. `libc++_shared.so` will stay unaligned until the next NDK update.
    ([UUM-156603](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156603))

- Android: Fixed batch-mode builds on Windows that waited forever when the Compile API Level platform was not installed.
    (UUM-158297)

- Android: Fixed CanBuildWithSymlinkSources flakiness caused by parameterized runs sharing a temp export directory.
    (UUM-149303)

- Android: Fixed freeze when relaunching GameActivity immediately after unload in UaaL mode.
    (UUM-149857)

- Android: Fixed mouse movement producing scroll values when the cursor is locked.
    ([UUM-156720](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156720))

- Android: Fixed problem with Back gesture/button closing the app after returning from background when "Run In Background" and "Predictive Back Support" are both enabled.
    ([UUM-159613](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159613))

- Android: Fixed red and blue color channels being swapped in custom cursor textures set with Cursor.SetCursor on Android.
    ([UUM-159757](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159757))

- Android: Fixed system bars not hiding automatically on Android 11 when using GameActivity and System Bars Behavior is set to "Show Transient Bars By Swipe".
    ([UUM-155415](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155415))

- Android: Fixed the build stopping with "Android SDK is missing build-tools." instead of offering to install the missing build-tools.
    (UUM-157581)

- Android: Fixed the Compile SDK Version dropdown in Android Player Settings listing API levels that are lower than the minimum supported compile API level.
    (UUM-151786)

- Android: Fixed touchpad input being ignored and an error being logged when the touchpad input source was not reported by the input device.
    (UUM-159337)

- Android: Fixed WebCamTexture taking several seconds to resume after the app regains focus or another app releases the camera, for example in split-screen mode.
    (UUM-155066)

- Android: Raised the Android NDK API level to 26 to match the minimum API level.
    (UUM-160285)

- Android: Stop requiring minSdk/targetSdk platform SDK to be installed in CheckAndroidSDK.cs.
    (UUM-151750)

- Android: Update OpenJDK from 17.0.18+8 to 17.0.21+9, to fix the compromised eMudhra intermediate certificates.
    ([UUM-158390](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158390))

- Android: Updated the bundled Android SDK cmdline-tools to 21.0, so emulators for Android 17 \(API 37.0\) created with its avdmanager start correctly.
    (UUM-157110)

- Android: With GameActivity, a key typed while a script changes the input field text or selection is no longer lost.
    (UUM-155234)

- Android: With GameActivity, the on-screen keyboard is no longer reported as closed right after it opens.
    (UUM-158054)

- Android: \[Android\] Fixed a crash in PlayerPrefs.SetString that occurred when the Java heap was full or a JNI exception was pending on Unity 7.0.
    ([UUM-158759](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158759))

- Animation: Crash when calling AnimationUtility.SetAdditiveReferencePose\` with a null clip or referenceClip.
    ([UUM-150816](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150816))

- Animation: Dedicated discrete integer curve support in AnimationClip to fix support of a limited ranges of values for discrete integer properties.
    ([UUM-149600](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149600))

- Animation: Fixed a crash when destroying objects animated by a legacy Animation component after a clip was added to or removed from it while it was playing or had been sampled.
    ([UUM-161463](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161463))

- Animation: Fixed a crash when sampling an Animation Clip that has a key at the maximum float time on a GameObject with an Animator.
    ([UUM-159302](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159302))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where clicking in the "Edit Key" fields of the Animation Window curve view wouldn't deselect the text in the field.
    ([UUM-140166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140166))

- Animation: Fixed an issue where deleting sub-properties selected across multiple properties in the Animation window deleted only the last selected property.
    ([UUM-137365](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137365))

- Animation: Fixed Animation Rigging QuaternionExt.FromToRotation returning an identity rotation when the input vectors are very short.
    ([UUM-161377](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161377))

- Animation: Fixed Animation Rigging WeightedTransformArray.CopyTo overwriting the destination array and throwing IndexOutOfRangeException instead of ArgumentException when the destination is one element too short.
    ([UUM-161376](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161376))

- Animation: Fixed Animator transitions with Bool or Int conditions evaluating incorrectly after the referenced parameter was deleted and re-created with a different type.
    (UUM-149420)

- Animation: Fixed methods with an AnimationEventInfo parameter not being listed in the Function dropdown of the Animation Event Inspector.
    ([UUM-155319](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155319))

- Animation: Fixed MultiReferentialConstraintData.driver throwing a NullReferenceException on default data and storing -1 when the source list is empty.
    ([UUM-161379](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161379))

- Animation: Fixed the Select Game Object window not appearing when selecting Other... from the Animation Preview Model dropdown a second time.
    ([UUM-148566](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148566))

- Animation: Fixed WeightedTransformArray.RemoveAt not throwing an exception for an index at or past Count, which removed the last element or set Count to -1.
    ([UUM-161375](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161375))

- Apple TV: On tvOS, pressing the Menu button now exits fullscreen video playback in Full, Minimal, and CancelOnInput control modes, not just CancelOnInput.
    (UUM-149896)

- Asset Bundles: Fixed a player crash when instantiating a prefab from an AssetBundle that was reached through a reference to a child object of a prefab in another AssetBundle.
    (UUM-141605)

- Asset Bundles: Fixed an issue on Windows where an AudioClip failed to play, and errors were logged, after an AssetBundle was loaded again with AssetBundle.LoadFromStreamAsync once the Stream of the previous load was disposed.
    ([UUM-158933](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158933))

- Asset Bundles: Fixed AssetBundle build reports not recording time or log messages for pre/post-process build callbacks.
    (UUM-143933)

- Asset Bundles: Fixed AssetBundle content hashes changing between builds of unchanged content with Addressables or the Scriptable Build Pipeline, causing players to redownload unmodified bundles. Affected bundles get a new hash on the first build after upgrading.
    ([UUM-148841](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148841))

- Asset Import: Fixed a docked Import Activity window disappearing and showing as "Failed to load" after maximizing and restoring a different docked window.
    ([UUM-148146](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148146))

- Asset Import: Fixed a movie packing error when importing multiple video clips with parallel import enabled.
    ([UUM-159435](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159435))

- Asset Import: Fixed an Editor crash in Transform when an asset reimport unloaded objects that were still loaded.
    (UUM-126651)

- Asset Import: Fixed an issue where changing GetVersion on an AssetPostprocessor did not reimport textures, sprites, cameras, lights or scenes when another AssetPostprocessor in a different assembly had the same full type name.
    (UUM-154761)

- Asset Import: Fixed Asset Import Workers running on Direct3D12 when the Editor was started with a different forced graphics API, such as -force-d3d11. Workers now inherit the graphics API requested on the Editor command line.
    (UUM-147604)

- Asset Import: Fixed default presets for some importers, such as SVGImporter, no longer being applied after restarting the project.
    ([UUM-146265](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146265))

- Asset Import: Fixed ImporterGlobalSettings values silently not being synchronized to Asset Import Workers when the settings class is not declared in a script file of the same name. Unity now logs a warning, and moving the class to a correctly named file takes effect without restarting the Editor.
    ([UUM-154025](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154025))

- Asset Import: Fixed NaN tangents in animation clips imported from FBX files whose animation curves contain NaN key values.
    (UUM-1070)

- Asset Import: Fixed the Inspector reporting unapplied import settings after a script changed importer settings and called AssetImporter.SaveAndReimport\(\) while the asset was inspected.
    ([UUM-151732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151732))

- Asset Import: Fixed the Linux Editor sometimes exiting while importing crunch-compressed textures, with "crn_threading_pthreads.cpp\(194\): Failure: semaphore: sem_wait\(\) or sem_timedwait\(\) failed" in the log. The crunch compressor treated an interrupted wait as a fatal error; it now retries the wait.
    ([UUM-150055](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150055))

- Asset Import: Fixed the Model Importer Materials tab showing a blank Location field for models that use the obsolete external materials mode.
    ([UUM-161031](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161031))

- Asset Pipeline: Adding warnings when users call folders anything ending in .meta
    ([UUM-129343](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-129343))

- Asset Pipeline: Enabled 3 Directory monitoring tests with CoreCLR
    (UUM-148147)

- Asset Pipeline: Fix ActiveRefreshImportMode not returning a valid value when Editor is launched with command line argument: -refreshImportMode OutOfProcess

- Asset Pipeline: Fix warning saying added ScriptedImporter is not running out of process when it is
    ([UUM-153306](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153306))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed "ScriptableSingleton ... already exists" warnings appearing in the Console when a package creates its settings asset, for example after installing the XR Composition Layers package.
    ([UUM-147680](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147680))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash after a domain reload when a native importer replaced a ScriptedImporter for an asset.
    ([UUM-107764](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-107764))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash on Editor shutdown caused by an asset import worker timer callback running after the worker manager was destroyed.
    (UUM-134447)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash on Linux when the Editor reopened a project after restarting for a graphics API change.
    ([UUM-159784](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159784))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when an AssetModificationProcessor called AssetDatabase.Refresh from OnWillSaveAssets while the Editor wrote an asset's .meta file, for example during AssetDatabase.ForceReserializeAssets.
    ([UUM-156706](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156706))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when opening a project that contained a manager asset holding an object of the wrong type.
    ([UUM-153271](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153271))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when opening a project with a corrupted Library.
    ([UUM-153198](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153198))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a crash when passing a non-importer Type to ArtifactKey and other AssetDatabaseExperimental APIs that take an importer type, which now throw an ArgumentException instead.
    ([UUM-157001](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157001))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a performance regression that added a fixed overhead to every Asset Database refresh.
    (UUM-154636)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a random Editor crash on shutdown caused by a data race in the asset database script artifact bookkeeping.
    (UUM-142994)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a regression that slowed memory snapshot capture on Windows.
    (UUM-150454)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a regression where AssetDatabase.Refresh took longer when project settings had unsaved changes.
    (UUM-154819)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed a spurious error message "Failed to update AssetBundle names in the database" that can succeed on an automatic retry.
    (UUM-149444)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an assertion and LoadAssetAtPath returning null when reimporting an asset after it was destroyed with DestroyImmediate\(asset, true\).
    ([UUM-159082](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159082))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue on Windows where saving ProjectSettings.asset could fail while an asset import worker process was starting in a project without a ProjectVersion.txt file.
    (UUM-149390)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where a "File exists" error was shown when creating a project or duplicating a folder on an exFAT drive on macOS.
    ([UUM-150634](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150634))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where all asset imports failed when the Editor fell back from Direct3D 12 to Direct3D 11 on an unsupported graphics device.
    ([UUM-146876](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146876))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where AssetDatabase.DeleteAsset, AssetDatabase.DeleteAssets and AssetDatabase.MoveAssetToTrash deleted the entire Assets folder, or another root folder, and returned true. These methods now refuse root folders and return false.
    ([UUM-159711](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159711))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where AssetDatabase.ForceReserializeAssets became very slow when reserializing many assets that included Prefabs.
    (UUM-125054)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where AssetDatabase.MoveAsset deleted the source asset when the destination path ended with a slash and named a folder that did not exist.
    ([UUM-159762](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159762))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where calling AssetDatabase.MoveAsset with "Assets/" as the destination path crashed the Editor.
    ([UUM-159303](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159303))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where calling AssetDatabaseExperimental.ForceProduceArtifact with a default ArtifactKey made the Editor hang.
    ([UUM-151271](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151271))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where closing an Editor started with -readonly deleted files from the Temp folder of another Editor that had the same project open.
    (UUM-158283)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where content could fail to commit to the data store after the Editor was force-quit during an import.
    (UUM-151756)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where deleting multiple assets left assets undeleted when an AssetModificationProcessor refused to delete one of them.
    ([UUM-155567](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155567))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where handlers subscribed to AssetDatabase.importPackageCancelled were never invoked.
    (UUM-155758)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor committed 1 GB of memory on Windows at startup, even in an empty project.
    (UUM-159605)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash during a domain reload or on quit while asset previews were loading.
    ([UUM-141036](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141036))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash on every launch when a data file in the project's Library folder was damaged.
    (UUM-160491)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash on quit if an import worker message arrived during shutdown.
    (UUM-161371)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor could crash when loading or force-reserializing a PlayerSettings asset saved by an old version of Unity.
    ([UUM-138620](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138620))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the editor could hang if the Unity.DataStore process was killed.
    ([UUM-149968](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149968))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor crashed on launch when the project was on an exFAT drive on macOS.
    ([UUM-156738](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156738))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Editor quit with a "guid is uninitialized" fatal error when importing an asset with a missing script and an object reference that holds a malformed GUID.
    ([UUM-159779](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159779))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed asset paths with two or three leading path separators \(for example `///asset`\) being incorrectly rejected as invalid on Windows CoreCLR Editors. Drive-relative and volume-separated asset paths \(for example `C:a.prefab`\) are now validated identically on Mono and CoreCLR Editors.
    (UUM-151543)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed AssetDatabase.CreateFolder returning a GUID of all zeros instead of an empty string when the folder could not be created.
    ([UUM-154150](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154150))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed assets not reimporting when enum members change in a script, on the CoreCLR editor.
    (UUM-149233)

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed Editor crash when the Unity Version Control Diff window shows a modified prefab that contains nested prefab instances.
    ([UUM-155069](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155069))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed edits to a .meta file being overwritten when the file was changed while its asset was importing out of process.

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed IMGUI content drawn after a call to AssetPreview.GetAssetPreview being clipped away when the previewed asset had unsaved changes.
    ([UUM-112558](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-112558))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed repeated out-of-process imports and repeated version control prompts when an AssetModificationProcessor declined saving a .meta file that an importer had upgraded.

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed the Editor quitting with a fatal error at startup when the project's Library/DataStore/uds.lock file is read-only.
    ([UUM-160173](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160173))

- Asset Pipeline: Fixed the Import Activity window logging a warning when the restored layout selected an asset that is not in the project.

- Audio: Fixed a bug on macOS where audio playback would slow down when the microphone was being used by the system
    ([UUM-149880](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149880))

- Audio: Fixed a fatal error in the Editor when an audio output device was reconnected after Unity failed to resume audio output.
    ([UUM-158166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158166))

- Audio: Fixed a performance regression in audio processing caused by profiler instrumentation running when no profiler data was being captured.
    (UUM-153489)

- Audio: Fixed an issue where an Audio Source playing an Audio Random Container was silent while Time.timeScale was 0 if a Rigidbody was attached to the GameObject or one of its parents.
    ([UUM-159324](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159324))

- Audio: Fixed AudioClip.Create returning an empty clip that reported loadState Loaded, instead of throwing an ArgumentException, when the channel count exceeded the supported maximum.
    ([UUM-154941](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154941))

- Audio: Fixed AudioClips created with AudioClip.Create losing their sample data and logging "Cannot attach thread to NULL scripting domain" errors when entering Play mode with domain and scene reload disabled.
    ([UUM-156073](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156073))

- Audio: Fixed AudioSource.isPlaying returning false while a one-shot or sample provider was playing on an AudioSource that had previously played an Audio Random Container.
    ([UUM-160362](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160362))

- Audio: Fixed AudioSource.isPlaying staying true, and the generator and its resources not being released, after a scriptable generator finished playing.
    ([UUM-144628](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/generators-on-audiosources-that-are-marked-as-finished-still-report-isplaying-equals-true))

- Audio: Fixed exiting Play Mode taking several seconds on Linux when the audio output device does not block, such as an ALSA null device.
    (UUM-153880)

- Audio: Fixed Microphone.devices reporting no devices when the Editor or Player started with a Bluetooth headset connected while using the Enhanced Audio Foundation on Linux.
    (UUM-148578)

- Bug Reporter: Fixed the Player.log location

- Bug Reporter: Fixed the Player.log location by using the new company name / application name arguments passed to the Bug Reporter

- Bug Reporter: Improved the resilience pipeline when fetching the Editor version for a bug report

- Bug Reporter: Upgrade from .NET 8.0.404 to .NET 10.0.14

- Build Pipeline: Fixed a crash when a build callback created an asset during a content directory build.
    (UUM-156065)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed a crash when ContentBuildInterface.CalculatePlayerSerializationHashForType was called with a null Type. The method now throws an ArgumentNullException.
    ([UUM-152613](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152613))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue in the Build Analysis window where all columns of a table could be hidden, which left an empty list that could still be scrolled and selected.
    ([UUM-143786](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143786))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where a BuildFailedException thrown during AssetPostprocessor.OnProcessScene was not automatically failing the build.
    (UUM-153802)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where Build Details showed a "Content Options" row for Player builds and a "Build Options" row for Content Directory builds that always read "None".
    (UUM-153612)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where import workers would sometimes skip reloading loaded assets at the start of a build.

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where incremental AssetBundle builds would ignore version change on a AssetPostprocessor.OnProcessScene when deciding to rebuild scene AssetBundles or not.
    (UUM-156337)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where incremental AssetBundle builds would ignore version change on a IProcessSceneWithReport using a BuildCallbackVersion attribute when deciding to rebuild scene AssetBundles or not.
    (UUM-156336)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where incremental Player builds would ignore version change on a AssetPostprocessor.OnProcessScene when deciding to rebuild the player data or not.
    (UUM-156340)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where incremental Player builds would ignore version change on a IProcessSceneWithReport using a BuildCallbackVersion attribute when deciding to rebuild the player data or not.
    (UUM-156338)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where incremental Player builds would ignore version change on a PostProcessSceneAttribute when deciding to rebuild the player data or not.
    (UUM-156339)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Build Analysis window expanded all nested builds again after the window was reopened or scripts were reloaded.
    (UUM-158840)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Build Analysis window listed a build started during another build, such as a content directory build started during a Player build, as an unrelated build instead of nesting it under the build it ran inside.
    (UUM-153441)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Build Profiles window reported a multi-target platform's SDK package as not installed after the Editor started or scripts recompiled.

- Build Pipeline: Fixed an issue where the Build Steps section of the Build Analysis window showed a row from the previously selected build when the selected build had no step data.
    (UUM-157493)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed Build Analysis launching a macOS player instead of revealing its folder when Reveal in Finder was clicked in the Overview tab.
    ([UUM-145942](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145942))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed Build And Run launching the player before the IPostprocessBuildWithReport and IPostprocessBuildWithContext callbacks had run.
    (UUM-154495)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed build step labels in the Build Analysis window showing internal method names instead of readable text.
    ([UUM-150888](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150888))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed Build Steps columns being cut off in the Build Analysis window.
    (UUM-158263)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed BuildReportSummary.json listing inactive build options, such as Il2CPP, in the BuildOptions array.
    ([UUM-150768](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150768))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed builds added to the build history while the Build Analysis window was closed not appearing in the build list when the window was opened.

- Build Pipeline: Fixed content directory builds reusing cached scenes after the version argument of a PostProcessScene attribute changed.
    (UUM-156335)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed content directory builds reusing cached shaders after the BuildCallbackVersion of an IPreprocessShaders or IPreprocessComputeShaders callback was increased.
    (UUM-155222)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed issue where the embedded Addressables tab in the Build Analysis window copies UI elements.
    (UUM-155242)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed LOD stripping in Dedicated Server content directory builds using the Standalone quality levels instead of the Server quality levels.
    (UUM-161178)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed reference rows in the Build Analysis window's Assets tab showing no tooltip after scrolling.
    ([UUM-160227](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160227))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed sequential command line builds started with -activeBuildProfile compiling scripts for the previously active platform.
    (UUM-150775)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the Build Analysis inspector reopening on asset selection after being closed.
    ([UUM-154999](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154999))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the Build Analysis window's Asset Table showing no message when a search matched no assets.
    ([UUM-155320](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155320))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the build count in the Build Analysis window footer not following the search filter. The footer now shows the number of matching builds while searching, and shows the build limit count only from 70% of the limit.
    ([UUM-150885](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150885))

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the Build Details section of the Build Analysis window not showing a build's related builds.
    (UUM-155781)

- Build Pipeline: Fixed the warning and error counts being cut off in the Build Analysis window's Build Steps list when a step had 100 or more messages.
    ([UUM-153057](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153057))

- Build Pipeline: Set DOTNET_SYSTEM_GLOBALIZATION_USENLS in MSBuild host service to fix a random hang in roslyn

- Build System: Avoid BuildProgram hang when trying to use https:// in a local path string
    ([UUM-131187](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-131187))

- Build System: bee_backend's stale output removal should not recheck the same file indefinitely
    (UUM-158568)

- Build System: Don't hang the Editor when a previous bee_backend is still running.
    ([UUM-142773](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-mono-log-write-logfile-when-more-than-one-copy-of-bee-backend-is-running))

- Build System: Strip the AMD FidelityFX DLL from player builds when AMDUnityPlugin is not enabled.
    (UUM-156516)

- Burst: Fixed "Compilation was requested for method" Burst warnings appearing in the Console when a script has a compile error.
    ([UUM-156119](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156119))

- Burst: Fixed "error: undefined symbol: strlen" linker error
    ([UUM-161029](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161029))

- Burst: Fixed that on weakly ordered memory architectures like ARM64, Burst now compiles volatile reads and writes to have acquire/release semantics
    (UUM-159683)

- Cache Server: Content Directory artifacts uploaded to the Accelerator now include the blobs they depend on. Fixes errors like `ContentFileMetadataCollection failed to load content for CAH <hash>`.
    (UUM-158843)

- Cache Server: Fixed a cross-thread memory deallocation error that could occur when resolving content IDs through the Accelerator.
    (UUM-145808)

- Cache Server: Fixed connecting to a Unity Accelerator when no port is specified in the cache server IP address: the default port now follows the TLS/SSL setting \(10443 with TLS enabled, 10080 without\).

- Cache Server: Fixed editor hangs when using TLS connections to a Unity Accelerator. One due to always failing certificate verification, which previously made the -cacheServerIgnoreTLSVerification command line argument mandatory for TLS and the other from closing the connection without a TLS close handshake.
    (EAD-2537)

- Cinemachine: OrbitalFollow cameras using the InheritPosition blend hint with position damping now inherit the outgoing camera's direction correctly, and jerk less on the blend back.
    ([UUM-149754](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149754))

- Compute: Compute shaders now check for target and unrecognized pragma directives.
    ([UUM-159100](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159100))

- Core: Fixed Leak Detection reporting false leaks on the first Domain Reload after switching from EnabledWithStackTrace to Enabled.
    ([UUM-108870](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-108870))

- CoreCLR: Fixed a security vulnerability \(CVE-2026-111497\) in the Brotli decoder used by System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream and BrotliDecoder, where decoding untrusted data could allow remote code execution.

- CoreCLR: Fixed batch mode Editor invocations appearing to hang for up to 10 minutes when their output is captured by a parent process.
    (UUM-148363)

- CoreCLR: Fixed CoreCLR native debug symbol files being included in the Mac and Linux Editor installers and in Linux Player builds, reducing installer and build size.
    (UUM-151753)

- CoreCLR: Fixed CoreCLR player builds including runtime files that are never loaded, such as standalone garbage collectors, ETW message resources, import libraries, and a duplicate debugger access library.
    (UUM-147611)

- CoreCLR: Fixed incorrect code generation when the JIT removed a BITCAST over a narrow \(byte or ushort\) load, which could leave garbage in the upper bits instead of correctly zero- or sign-extending the value \(upstream dotnet/runtime \#133597\).
    (UUM-153231)

- CoreCLR: Updated the network statistics implementation and fixed a potential infinite loop when querying active network connections.

- Documentation: Fixed Localization and Smart Strings links in the documentation
    (UUM-152984)

- DX12: Added validation to CopyTexture fix GPU crashes that happened mismatched fallback formats while baking lightmaps for entities graphics on Windows targeting Android.
    (UUM-150236)

- DX12: Fix regex patterns in .buginfo, which fell through to the wrong team.
    (UUM-153607)

- DX12: Fixed a crash in batch mode when rendering a camera to the screen.
    (UUM-159254)

- DX12: Fixed a crash on DirectX 12 when a large texture, such as a big dynamic font atlas, was updated many times in a single frame.
    ([UUM-140070](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140070))

- DX12: Fixed a crash when a native rendering plugin event configured with kUnityD3D12EventConfigFlag_FlushCommandBuffers was issued inside a render pass.
    (UUM-156929)

- DX12: Fixed a Direct3D12 validation error caused by the Virtual Texturing GPU cache being written from the copy queue while still in a shader-read state.
    (UUM-154995)

- DX12: Fixed a DirectX 12 validation error caused by incorrect resource-state tracking on async command lists.
    (UUM-144862)

- DX12: Fixed a graphics validation error \(category 9, id 538\) at BeginRenderPass for render pass input attachments when using split-threaded native graphics jobs.
    (UUM-144553)

- DX12: Fixed a race on DX12 where a native rendering plugin could write to a texture or buffer before its initial data upload had completed.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed a race on DX12 where graphics work could change the state of a resource that async compute work was still using.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed a random crash on Windows when using the Direct3D12 graphics API, caused by a use-after-free when releasing GPU buffers.
    ([UUM-150682](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150682))

- DX12: Fixed an Editor crash when capturing an editor window that uses MSAA, such as the Inspector.
    (UUM-160893)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where constant buffers used by the SRP Batcher could be released while the GPU still used them, which caused a crash when the D3D12 debug layer was enabled.
    (UUM-159253)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where GraphicsStateCollection warm-up created pipelines that did not match the draws, so those pipelines were compiled again at first draw. This affected meshes that lack a texcoord the shader reads, and blend states first used during warm-up with multithreaded rendering.
    (UUM-161348)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where resources used only by async compute could be evicted from GPU memory while still in use with DirectX 12.
    (UUM-156116)

- DX12: Fixed an issue where the Editor leaked descriptor memory on every window repaint when using DirectX 12, which could crash long Editor sessions.
    (UUM-159625)

- DX12: Fixed ArgumentOutOfRangeException errors and a malformed filter list in the Inspector when a D3D12 Device Filter asset open in multiple Inspector windows was edited in one of them.
    ([UUM-153941](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153941))

- DX12: Fixed async compute command allocators on DX12 sometimes being reused while their GPU work was still executing.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed D3D12 async compute command buffers on the same queue reading a resource in the wrong state when a later command buffer in the same frame transitions it.
    (UUM-156477)

- DX12: Fixed D3D12 validation errors when the back buffer was recreated mid-frame with split graphics jobs in fullscreen.
    (UUM-156806)

- DX12: Fixed errors being thrown when an empty texture is displayed in the Editor while using the Direct3D12 graphics API.
    ([UUM-144444](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144444))

- DX12: Fixed fields in the D3D12 Device Filter Inspector overflowing their section when the Inspector window was resized.
    ([UUM-134380](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134380))

- DX12: Fixed foldouts on a D3D12 Device Filter List asset collapsing when the asset was deselected and reselected in the Project window.
    ([UUM-141433](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141433))

- DX12: Fixed graphics API validation errors \(invalid resource state 9:538 and resource barrier state mismatch 8:527\) on depth-stencil attachments when native graphics jobs use split threading.
    (UUM-154103)

- DX12: Fixed incorrect rendering after a native rendering plugin event that changes descriptor heaps, because Unity did not rebind its own descriptor heaps when the event was configured with kUnityD3D12EventConfigFlag_ModifiesCommandBuffersState.
    (UUM-156929)

- DX12: Fixed IUnityGraphicsD3D12::GetNextFrameFenceValue\(\) returning a fence value that could complete before the commands a native plugin recorded into Unity's current command list when graphics jobs are enabled.
    (UUM-156768)

- DX12: Fixed missing depth testing in render pass subpasses that bind depth as read-only and read it as an input attachment when graphics jobs are enabled on DX12.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed Player Settings showing unusable D3D12 device filtering settings for platforms that don't support Direct3D12.
    (UUM-149251)

- DX12: Fixed RenderTargetFlags.ReadOnlyDepth and RenderTargetFlags.ReadOnlyStencil being ignored on DX12 when not using native render passes, which caused incorrect resource states when sampling the bound depth texture.
    (UUM-137043)

- DX12: Fixed terrain ShaderGraph splatmap layers beyond the 4th failing to render on DX12 in the Deferred and Deferred+ rendering paths when Rendering Layers or Decal Layers were enabled.
    (UUM-151879)

- DX12: Fixed the -force-d3d12-debug command line argument reporting a missing Windows "Graphics Tools" optional feature when the D3D12 debug layer library was absent from the build instead.
    ([UUM-137188](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137188))

- DX12: Fixed the D3D12 Device Filter Inspector so that the Performance &amp; Capability Metrics header no longer extends past its section when the Inspector is narrow.
    ([UUM-155240](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155240))

- DX12: Handle out of memory on very large texture creations.
    ([UUM-157106](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157106))

- DX12: The Editor no longer crashes with an out-of-memory failure or a GPU device reset when a single repaint renders a scene with extreme per-frame draw volume. Direct3D 12 now submits GPU work mid-frame once more than 1 GiB of upload scratch memory has accumulated; the threshold can be changed, or set to 0 to disable it, with the d3d12-scratch-flush-threshold boot config parameter. The first time a mid-frame flush runs, the Editor or Player log names that parameter.
    (UUM-93272)

- Editor: Add copy and paste options to the hamburger menu of any settings provider that has copy and paste capabilities.
    ([UUM-147183](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147183))

- Editor: Add duospace tag support to ATG
    ([UUM-146098](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146098))

- Editor: Add inspector warning box when layout element has conflicting min and max values.
    ([UUM-144431](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144431))

- Editor: add UaaL support for Swift Project type in iOS/tvOS

- Editor: Added Caption Bar support to the Android window insets API. Use `AndroidWindowInsets.Type.CaptionBar` to query the caption bar and control whether your application window renders behind it.

- Editor: Added missing Apple and Android app support to the localization module.

- Editor: Added Platform Define Support for Multi-Target Platform.
    (UUM-151085)

- Editor: Added warning on "New Analysis" for imported Project Auditor reports.
    (UUM-153096)

- Editor: AutoStaticsCleanup generator errors are now visible in console.
    (UUM-155160)

- Editor: Avoid having multiple errors when changing the technical name of a custom package already in the project manifest.
    ([UUM-141424](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141424))

- Editor: Avoid writing to immutable materials during Render Pipeline conversion.
    (UUM-155341)

- Editor: Change max size of grid layout group to infinite. Restores previous expected behaviour.
    ([UUM-158101](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158101))

- Editor: Confirm with a dialog before assigning URP assets on the Create URP asset step.
    ([UUM-154177](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154177))

- Editor: DefaultAsset &amp; ScriptableObject icons now scale correctly at maximum size in Project window

- Editor: Deprecate OnPostRender\(\)

- Editor: Deprecate OnPreCull\(\)

- Editor: Deprecate OnPreRender\(\)

- Editor: Deprecate OnRenderImage\(\)

- Editor: Derived platform defines are now available in asmdef define constraints.
    (UUM-151203)

- Editor: Disabled Show Tutorial Window button since Universal 2D Project does not provide one.
    ([UUM-150083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150083))

- Editor: Don't include the NET analyzers twice in Project Auditor code analysis.
    (UUM-156937)

- Editor: Ensure some memory areas missing roots are now rooted. Editor: MonoScriptInfoScraper and some il2cpp initialisation.
    (UUM-126934)

- Editor: Entitlements on unnecessary files in mac installers are eliminated
    ([UUM-156884](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156884))

- Editor: Fix "Start New Analysis" discard dialog shown when no report exists
    ([UUM-157415](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157415))

- Editor: Fix broken profiles when multiple build profile variants share a package
    (UUM-148423)

- Editor: Fix EGL surface errors during URS client surface recreation
    (UUM-145511)

- Editor: Fix for iOS/visionOS build bloat caused by including ICU data as part of the IL2CPP distribution.
    (UUM-155494)

- Editor: Fix incorrect line being selected when opening exception from console with a method using UnityEngine as a parameter
    ([UUM-136222](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136222))

- Editor: Fix permissions on Burst MacOS tools, and remove chmod usages
    ([UUM-154906](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154906))

- Editor: Fix Project Auditor empty-state message for filtered vs. empty categories
    ([UUM-152980](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152980))

- Editor: Fix URS BuildProfile window issues
    (UUM-152528)

- Editor: Fix use case: reparent into a layout group, then undo
    ([UUM-130685](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-130685))

- Editor: Fixed a "FormatVariableName can only be called from the main thread" exception when ObjectNames.NicifyVariableName was called from a background thread, such as during Project Auditor analysis.
    (UUM-161327)

- Editor: FIxed a bug where the main toolbar unity logo appeared vertically stretched on mac retina display.
    ([UUM-134520](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134520))

- Editor: Fixed a bug where the prefab stage header text did not update after the asset name was modified in the project browser.
    ([UUM-148172](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148172))

- Editor: Fixed a camera with Target Eye set to None rendering with the XR eye texture's size and aspect ratio instead of the window's while an XR display is active.
    ([UUM-152503](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152503))

- Editor: Fixed a crash in the Editor when unloading an AssetBundle that contains Animation Clips used by an Animator Controller loaded from a different AssetBundle.
