    ([UUM-150704](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150704))

- Editor: Fixed a crash in the macOS asset import worker caused by a data race between analytics event registration and saving analytics session values.
    (UUM-154130)

- Editor: Fixed a crash on closing the Editor shortly after saving.
    (UUM-143428)

- Editor: Fixed a crash on startup when the Unity Editor Software Terms had not been accepted and the terms file was missing, empty, or could not be read.
    ([UUM-154911](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154911))

- Editor: Fixed a crash that could occur when undoing the addition of a Visual Effect component to a GameObject.
    ([UUM-136309](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-136309))

- Editor: Fixed a crash when an editor extension passes an invalid index to ActiveEditorTracker.GetVisible or SetVisible.

- Editor: Fixed a crash when closing the Inspector tab while an in-editor tutorial is running.
    ([UUM-153247](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153247))

- Editor: Fixed a crash when opening Help &gt; Report a Bug or exiting the Editor in a project with an EditorApplication.delayCall callback declared in a complex generic type.
    ([UUM-122928](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-122928))

- Editor: Fixed a flaky Android startup integration test \(AndroidInitActivity.TestStartup_ARM64IL2CPP\) and re-enabled it.

- Editor: Fixed a grammatical error in the warning shown on the Install URP step of the Migrate to URP wizard.
    ([UUM-154221](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154221))

- Editor: Fixed a MissingReferenceException that the Inspector logged on Play mode exit when it was scrolled down and showed a Volume component.

- Editor: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when selecting a GameObject in the Hierarchy while the Tool Contexts overlay was displayed but collapsed.
    ([UUM-141529](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141529))

- Editor: Fixed a performance regression in the Editor when Material Variants were updated after a change to a parent Material.
    (UUM-154746)

- Editor: Fixed account dropdown display when entering play mode
    ([UUM-150759](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150759))

- Editor: Fixed AddComponentMenu order not being used
    ([UUM-112973](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-112973))

- Editor: Fixed an ArgumentNullException that could be logged when packages were registered, for example when adding a macOS Server build profile.
    ([UUM-155583](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155583))

- Editor: Fixed an Editor crash when a PreviewRenderUtility was cleaned up before EndStaticPreview\(\) was called.

- Editor: Fixed an incorrect max width calculation and propagation in layout groups when there are no child elements.

- Editor: Fixed an Inspector tracker leak that logged SerializedObjectNotCreatableException errors on domain reload after closing an Inspector window with a serialized lock state.

- Editor: Fixed an intermittent crash when quitting the Editor on macOS.
    ([UUM-160348](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160348))

- Editor: Fixed an issue on macOS where dropping an asset dragged from the Project window only completed after the mouse moved again, and pressing Escape sometimes didn't cancel the drag.
    ([UUM-157673](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157673))

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Build Profile Configurations in the Platform Browser could overlap the Packages section after resizing the window.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where creating a build profile that installs packages, such as Android XR, could set up the profile before its packages finished importing, which left its quality level without a render pipeline asset.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where enabling a loader in XR Management could intermittently fail to persist it, leaving the loader list empty.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where GameObjects hidden with HideFlags.HideInHierarchy could still be found and selected using the Hierarchy window search.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor code analysis failed or stopped responding with the MSBuild compilation pipeline when scripts had changed since the last compilation.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor reported no Migrate to CoreCLR issues when some assemblies failed to compile, for example in projects that use -warnaserror+.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor showed an unnamed assembly and an unreadable compiler message with no error code when an MSBuild build failed before compiling, for example because of a circular assembly definition reference.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where Project Auditor showed the Optimization page and highlighted the wrong sidebar entry after starting an analysis from the Migrate to URP tab.

- Editor: Fixed an issue where refreshing an empty Project Auditor view switched it to a flat list and turned off Show Ignored Issues.

- Editor: Project Auditor: Fixed test assemblies that use the UNITY_INCLUDE_TESTS define constraint not being recognized as test assemblies, so they were analyzed when the Tests area was disabled.
    (UUM-157445)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Fixed the Project Auditor window selecting a different issue and collapsing its group after the first domain reload following a new analysis.
    ([UUM-161504](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161504))

- Editor: Project Auditor: Hide report info for unusable URP Migration reports.
    (UUM-155528)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Hide report info for unusable URP Migration reports.
    (UUM-157912)

- Editor: Project Auditor: implement Inverted Call Hierarchies when analyzing with MSBuild enabled.
    (UUM-161425)

- Editor: Project Auditor: initial support for Code analysis when using MSBuild.
    ([UUM-152875](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152875))

- Editor: Project Auditor: Loading a report scanned for the other URP renderer no longer treats a finished migration as incomplete.
    (UUM-157913)

- Editor: Project Auditor: preserve UI selections across Domain Reloads.
    (UUM-151301)

- Editor: Project Auditor: preserve user globalconfig files during Code Analysis.
    (UUM-156838)

- Editor: Project Auditor: remember expanded state in the issue tables.
    ([UUM-147207](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147207))

- Editor: Project Auditor: Remove unused variable that was contributing to domain reload time.
    (UUM-155384)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Rename an URP Quick Fix button to "Review in Converter", to better match its functionality.
    ([UUM-153085](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153085))

- Editor: Project Auditor: Show information about all issues on URP Migration Summary page.
    (UUM-152854)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Split Migrate to URP step 3 into 2 stages: analysis and fix issues.
    (UUM-159566)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Split Migrate to URP step 3 into 2 stages: analysis and fix issues.
    (UUM-159567)

- Editor: Project Auditor: standard UI for summary screen issue breakdown charts.
    (UUM-159723)

- Editor: Project Auditor: Support RSP files when performing Code analysis.
    ([UUM-147287](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147287))

- Editor: Project Auditor: Suppressed .NET code quality rule IDs \(CAxxxx\) are now removed from the report when analyzing code with MSBuild.
    (UUM-156995)

- Editor: Project Auditor: The BinaryFormatter is incompatible with CoreCLR so we want to flag it in the migration view.
    (UUM-156484)

- Editor: Removed empty parentheses from SRP Batcher compatibility reason in the shader Inspector.
    ([UUM-152574](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152574))

- Editor: Skip drawing fully transparent gizmos lines when RectTransforms are selected
    (UUM-156679)

- Editor: Skip post-build audit when Project Auditor Rules package isn't installed
    ([UUM-152518](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152518))

- Editor: Stop missing animation trigger warnings being logged on every transition, on every keystroke of invalid transition names.
    ([UUM-144071](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144071))

- Editor: The label for the Edit Bounds button on the Skinned Mesh Renderer component is now indented correctly.

- Editor: The Platform Browser now requires the package licenses to be agreed to before a build<br>
    profile can be created.
    (UUM-153301)

- Editor: Updated Content Directories to take Application.backgroundLoadingPriority into account when loading
    (UUM-154823)

- Editor: Updated included VCRedist to 14.51.36247 for the Windows Editor.
    (UUM-156578)

- Editor: Updated TMP assets to make the assets compatible with ATG and reduce the default build size.
    (UUM-154834)

- Editor: Volume Profile Editor - Reset was invalidating the UI. Recreate the UI when needed.
    ([UUM-150343](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150343))

- Editor: When graphics jobs is enabled the Editor now draw renderers whose shader variant compiled during the frame. Asset previews and one-shot renders which were previously blank due to a missing shader should now draw as expected via the SRP Batcher fallback path
    (UUM-147205)

- Editor: \[Android\] Display an error informing users of the reason a device went offline during apk installation on Unity 7.0.
    (UUM-151550)

- Editor: \[Android\] Fix On Demand Rendering frame and pacing times.
    (UUM-147724)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed an issue where Vulkan windows flickered black on Wayland compositors such as Weston on Embedded Linux.
    (UUM-153585)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed GameWindow.GetPosition returning a global position instead of the position relative to the window's display that GameWindow.MoveTo uses.
    (UUM-159872)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed GameWindow.MoveTo reporting a position change and returning the requested position from GetPosition on Wayland, where the window cannot be moved.
    (UUM-160221)

- EmbeddedLinux: Fixed OpenGL ES fullscreen windows stretching the image instead of letterboxing it when the resolution aspect ratio differs from the display.<br>
    Fixed Vulkan fullscreen windows below the display resolution not being upscaled to fill the display on Wayland.<br>
    Fixed mouse, touch and IMGUI positions being offset from the image in letterboxed fullscreen windows.
    (UUM-160897)

- EmbeddedLinux: GLES: Fix ScreenSpaceOverlay UI too dark with forceSRGBBlit
    (UUM-147345)

- Engine Diagnostics: ANR reports are now attributed to the last session of the process that stopped responding.
    (UUM-157717)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed a crash on Android when an Engine Diagnostics crash report upload failed.
    (UUM-162109)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed a possible crash when a crash report was logged from a worker thread while the Engine Diagnostics configuration was being applied.
    (UUM-158113)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed an issue where Engine Diagnostics events and exception reports did not report the scene that was active when they occurred, for example after the active scene changed through SceneManager.SetActiveScene or SceneManager.MergeScenes.

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed an issue where the Build Profiles window kept showing the previous Diagnostic Data Collection value after a script changed EngineDiagnosticsSettings.enabled.
    ([UUM-160174](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160174))

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed Android apps reporting an extra session at launch, which ended the first session with an invalid timestamp.
    (UUM-157715)

- Engine Diagnostics: Fixed managed stack frames in IL2CPP crash reports showing no file name or line number when Cloud Diagnostics crash reporting is enabled in non-development builds.
    (UUM-155430)

- Entities: Fixed a bug where a scene's GameObjects would disappear from the Hierarchy window after exiting Play mode when a SubScene referenced the same scene.
    ([UUM-148713](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148713))

- Entities: Fixed an ArgumentNullException during Sub Scene import after clearing the Scene Asset of a Sub Scene.

- Entities: Fixed an entity sometimes showing the name of a previously destroyed entity that reused its index, and fixed the live baking diff reporting a name change when nothing had changed.
    (UUM-146522)

- Entities: Fixed an integer overflow when an Entities allocator budget is set close to 2 GB.
    ([UUM-155036](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155036))

- Entities: Fixed an InvalidOperationException when searching by component type in the Hierarchy window while a SubScene is loading.
    ([UUM-142749](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142749))

- Entities: Fixed an issue where rebuilding an unchanged project produced different content archives, causing published remote content to be downloaded again.
    ([UUM-151571](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151571))

- Entities: Fixed an issue where the authoring representation of an object would be shown instead of the runtime representation, when selecting the object through Entities Hierarchy.
    ([UUM-152665](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152665))

- Entities: Fixed changing the unit of an Entities allocator budget in Memory Settings resetting the value to 1 MB.
    ([UUM-155118](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155118))

- Entities: Fixed EntityManager methods, component lookups and EntityCommandBuffer playback silently reading and writing an entity that belongs to another world; such an entity is now treated as not existing in this world.

- Entities: Fixed GameObjects in an open SubScene disappearing from the Hierarchy window after the SubScene GameObject was disabled and enabled.
    ([UUM-154731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154731))

- Entities: Fixed IDebugOnAdded callbacks receiving default values instead of the actual component value, and fixed ECB.Instantiate not firing OnAdded callbacks.
    ([UUM-145910](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145910))

- Entities: Fixed memory corruption in Players without safety checks when an Entities allocator budget is used up.
    ([UUM-155107](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155107))

- Entities: Fixed player builds with Entities keeping unused assemblies, such as System.Text.Json, which increased the build size.
    (UUM-156976)

- Entities: Fixed the "archetypes", "archetype budget" and "query budget" labels in the Entities Memory Profiler module not being localized.
    ([UUM-155010](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155010))

- Entities: Fixed the Components list staying hidden for every archetype in the Entities Memory Profiler module after a World row was selected.
    ([UUM-154785](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154785))

- Entities: Fixed the Entities allocator budgets in Memory Settings not being applied in Edit Mode and during baking.
    ([UUM-155114](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155114))

- Entities: Fixed the Entities Memory Profiler module showing budget percentages with a space before the percent sign.
    ([UUM-155020](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155020))

- Entities: Fixed the entity differ missing changes in dynamic buffer elements after the first one when using referential equality.

- Entities: Fixed the entity differ reporting a component as changed when only its padding bytes differed.

- Entities: Fixed unsaved changes in a Sub Scene being lost when changing its Scene Asset and pressing Cancel in the save prompt.

- Entities: Fixed unsaved changes in a Sub Scene being lost without a save prompt when a duplicate Sub Scene that references the same scene was deleted or had its Scene Asset changed.
    ([UUM-154590](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154590))

- Entities: Fixed `EntityManager.Debug.IsSharedComponentManagerEmpty` returning true while unmanaged shared component values were still in use.

- Entities: Setting an Entity name to an empty string didn't work.
    ([UUM-161430](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161430))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed GC allocations in Surface Cache GI when many entities are spawned or change materials in the same frame.
    (UUM-154447)

- Entities Graphics: Fixed instances in a subscene not rendering in the Player after another subscene that uses the same meshes and materials was unloaded.
    ([UUM-124966](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-124966))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed static entities not updating lighting when light probes change and the entity does not.
    ([UUM-151337](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151337))

- Entities Graphics: Fixed Surface Cache GI ignoring the Global Illumination LOD of static LOD Groups in closed subscenes, which made every LOD level contribute to global illumination.
    (UUM-150718)

- GI: A Terrain added to a ray-tracing acceleration structure no longer leaks a Mesh and its GPU buffers.
    (UUM-151903)

- GI: Added missing "Always Refresh info message" in Surface Cache Volume Override if Always Refresh is disabled.
    (UUM-159673)

- GI: Added RIS for Surface Cache GI punctual light contribution, and fix some issues with punctual light sample generation which result in temporally unstable contribution.
    (UUM-154613)

- GI: Added support for hardware dynamic resolution scaling in SSR.
    ([UUM-152764](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152764))

- GI: Allow disabling the Minimum Smoothness threshold for SSR in URP by setting it to 0.
    (UUM-157998)

- GI: Avoid out-of-bounds memory access in UnifiedRayTracing which caused undefined behaviour on certain platforms.
    (UUM-152895)

- GI: Fix a bug where APV bake fails when scene contains a VFXRenderer.
    ([UUM-150965](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150965))

- GI: Fix a bug where editing a shader used in a scene that uses surface cache GI causes error spam in the console.
    (UUM-153248)

- GI: Fix Clamp Reflected Color setting for SSR in URP having no effect.
    (UUM-157997)

- GI: Fix issue where Surface Cache brightness would erroneously flicker for patches that were switching from one cascade to another.
    (UUM-158977)

- GI: Fix speckles/white spots with APV
    ([UUM-133626](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133626))

- GI: Fixed a bug where mixed lights appear fully occluded when using APV and having L2 spherical harmonics disabled.
    ([UUM-156430](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156430))

- GI: Fixed a bug where reflection probes sometimes would not be re-baked when baking lighting, if only URP-specific settings were modified.
    ([UUM-133949](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133949))

- GI: Fixed a bug where Surface Cache GI did not affect Terrains using custom shader graph shaders.
    (UUM-153804)

- GI: Fixed a crash in Surface Cache Global Illumination when a scene used more unique materials than the GPU supports in a texture array.
    (UUM-157977)

- GI: Fixed a heap-buffer-overflow read when deduplicating overlapping Light Probes from multiple loaded scenes.
    (UUM-153133)

- GI: Fixed a NullReferenceException and Render Graph execution errors when baking a Reflection Probe with the Screen Space Reflections renderer feature enabled and Opaque Texture disabled.
    ([UUM-159262](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159262))

- GI: Fixed a NullReferenceException when generating lighting for an Adaptive Probe Volume Baking Set after unloading one of its scenes.
    ([UUM-159309](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159309))

- GI: Fixed a typo in the Probe Volume Disable Streaming Assets tooltip in Graphics settings, where Addressables was misspelled.
    ([UUM-161558](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161558))

- GI: Fixed Adaptive Probe Volumes baking black probes near terrain surfaces when using Vulkan on some AMD GPUs.
    (UUM-157162)

- GI: Fixed Adaptive Probe Volumes placing probes as if the scene had no geometry when baking with Vulkan on AMD GPUs.
    ([UUM-137732](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137732))

- GI: Fixed an "Unknown reflection probe mode" error when baking reflection probes in a scene with Realtime reflection probes.
    ([UUM-141083](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141083))

- GI: Fixed an error about a missing 'GetMinMaxLuminance' kernel that was logged after every lighting bake when the Editor used Vulkan or OpenGL Core.

- GI: Fixed an issue where baking lighting with the Unified lightmapper on macOS produced non-finite values in lightmaps for meshes with degenerate triangles.
    (UUM-146654)

- GI: Fixed an issue where changing the Lighting Data Asset field in the Baked Lightmaps tab of the Lighting window could not be undone and did not appear in Undo History.
    ([UUM-133695](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133695))

- GI: Fixed an issue where clicking Bake in the Inspector with multiple Reflection Probes selected only baked the first probe.
    ([UUM-159701](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159701))

- GI: Fixed an issue where Generate Lighting baked a cubemap for Realtime Reflection Probes.
    ([UUM-158749](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158749))

- GI: Fixed Editor crashes when building a GPU BVH from an empty primitive span or with a maxLeafSize of 0.
    ([UUM-154597](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154597))

- GI: Fixed GC allocations every frame in Surface Cache GI, even when nothing in the scene changed.
    (UUM-154723)

- GI: Fixed GC allocations in Surface Cache GI when many objects are added, removed, or change materials in the same frame.
    (UUM-154329)

- GI: Fixed light baking failing when the only Global Illumination contributors in a scene had no materials assigned.
    (UUM-153676)

- GI: Fixed minor bug in QRNG_KRONECKER HLSL sampler \(only affecting the QRNG_KRONECKER_ENHANCED_QUALITY mode\).
    (UUM-159504)

- GI: Fixed probe occlusion that could bake incorrectly on macOS with the Unity Compute light baker.
    ([UUM-160178](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160178))

- GI: Fixed shader compiler warnings shown in the Inspector when selecting the Adaptive Probe Volumes VoxelizeScene shader.
    ([UUM-153983](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153983))

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache failing to start on devices with a maximum graphics buffer size below 256 MB.
    (UUM-154300)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache geometry rendering incorrectly after many meshes were added to the scene.

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache GI and the Compute ray tracing backend never hitting geometry on Adreno 650 GPUs with Vulkan.
    (UUM-153355)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache GI ignoring per-instance material overrides from MaterialPropertyBlock and Entities Graphics material property components, so instances that share a material now bounce and emit light with their own override colors.
    (UUM-160842)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache GI rejecting some unobstructed bounce light from punctual lights near triangle edges, which caused slight darkening.
    (UUM-160166)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache global illumination from a Color environment light rendering black after restarting the Editor.
    (UUM-151921)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache Global Illumination not updating in the Scene view while the Editor is idle.
    ([UUM-150016](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150016))

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache Global Illumination permanently leaving some screen pixels without a patch.
    (UUM-153981)

- GI: Fixed Surface Cache reading an incorrect punctual light sample count and frame index.
    (UUM-159743)

- GI: Fixed the Baked Albedo and Baked Emissive Scene view draw modes showing shifted and cropped textures when using the Unity Compute lightmapper.

- GI: Fixed the Render Graph Viewer showing a different set of passes on each capture when the Surface Cache Global Illumination renderer feature is active.
    ([UUM-150016](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150016))

- GI: Fixed the Surface Cache GI renderer feature running an incorrect defrag shader variant on devices that report a compute subgroup size of 2 or 128.
    (UUM-150463)

- GI: Fixed the Unity Compute Light Baker information box in Graphics settings not hiding when the Default Light Baker setting was reverted with Reset or Undo.
    ([UUM-151751](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151751))

- GI: Fixed UnifiedRayTracing adding all meshLOD levels in the Compute path.
    (UUM-159594)

- GI: Make Surface Cache check for inline ray tracing support to match its use of RayQuery \(instead of checking for ray tracing shader support\).
    (UUM-152549)

- GI: Make Surface Cache Volume Override show a warning if the Surface Cache Renderer Feature is missing.
    (UUM-159675)

- GI: Prevent MissingReferenceException in Surface Cache
    ([UUM-154099](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154099))

- GI: Prevent renderers from throwing errors when using shaders that have no meta pass in Surface Cache GI.
    ([UUM-154553](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154553))

- Graph Toolkit: A visualization context will now restore itself on window reopen.
    ([UUM-152695](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152695))

- Graph Toolkit: Context menus closing will no longer block keyboard shortcuts in Graph Windows.
    (UUM-149835)

- Graph Toolkit: Fix Copy/Paste of values in fields inside of nodes.
    ([UUM-151929](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151929))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed a horizontal scrollbar that appeared in the inspector when a state machine transition was selected, even when the content fit the inspector width.
    (UUM-149802)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when setting the Title, Subtitle, Tooltip, DefaultColor or FillAmount of a Node created from script before setting its Position.
    ([UUM-150194](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150194))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where characters typed with an IME, such as Korean or Japanese, were duplicated in string fields on Graph Toolkit nodes.
    ([UUM-130669](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-130669))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where converting a variable node with Set value in graph enabled to a constant left an orphaned wire connected to its input.
    ([UUM-155546](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155546))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where loading a truncated or badly merged graph asset silently dropped nodes, variables and states, and saving the graph made the loss permanent. Such assets are now refused with an error that names the file. \(UUM-154744\).
    ([UUM-154744](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154744))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed an issue where short portal names were cut off because the portal node did not keep a minimum width.
    ([UUM-133912](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133912))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed breadcrumbs that kept the old name after the subgraph node used to open them was renamed.
    ([UUM-141984](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141984))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed bug where GraphLogger messages would be logged twice
    ([UUM-151632](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151632))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed condition and transition icon overrides set from a visualization context disappearing from the Graph Inspector after the selection changed.
    (UUM-151358)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed IPort.Name returning null for some variable node ports, which caused name-based IsConnectionAllowed rules to throw a NullReferenceException.
    ([UUM-151603](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151603))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed MenuContext.ClickedObject being null when right-clicking a node, block, state or condition whose script is missing.
    (UUM-155408)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the Graph Inspector's Narrow the Selection list showing internal type names such as VariableDeclarationBase instead of user-facing names such as Variable.
    ([UUM-154274](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154274))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the graph visualization API so that it no longer throws errors when attempting to customize graph elements that are not visible in the current graph view \(such as elements nested within subgraphs\).
    ([UUM-156358](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156358))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the Item Library showing outdated variable names and types, and warning about duplicate items, after a blackboard variable was renamed or had its type changed.
    ([UUM-140026](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140026))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the Move items in the menus of a state machine transition rule being disabled when the rule was not selected.
    (UUM-158172)

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the name of the Graph Toolkit Window in the Overlay Preferences.
    ([UUM-132386](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132386))

- Graph Toolkit: Fixed the nested fields of a connected expandable port remaining editable in the Inspector.
    (UUM-157441)

- Graph Toolkit: Session.GraphView is now always resolved on access instead of fetching a cached copy. This fixes bugs related to accessing an outdated graph view through the session.
    ([UUM-151202](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151202))

- Graphics: \(Graphics\) RenderBuffer.GetNativeRenderBufferPtr no longer returns null when called immediately after RenderTexture.Create with graphics jobs enabled.

- Graphics: Add a workaround for compiling surface-cache HWRT shaders on MTL.

- Graphics: Added Soft Particle Support for on-tile depth.

- Graphics: Added the boot.config setting `vulkan-pso-max-size-mb` to control the size limit of the persistent pipeline state cache \(0 = unlimited; default remains 50 MB on Android and unlimited on other platforms\). Now when the driver's pipeline cache exceeds the limit, the log now reports the cache size, the limit and the setting to change, instead of only "Failed to get pipeline cache data".
    ([UUM-159420](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159420))

- Graphics: Added warning when the specified Keyword Declaration Override does not match any Keyword Declaration in the project.
    ([UUM-151445](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151445))

- Graphics: Addressed a render-thread freeze when a block in the async upload buffer gets corrupted; the application no longer freezes but will log an error and try to move on. Actual causes for corruption are still under investigation.
    (UUM-150567)

- Graphics: Android: Fixed the sky and indirect lighting rendering magenta with the Unity Graphics Kernel on Vulkan devices that do not support HDR ASTC textures, such as Samsung Xclipse and PowerVR Rogue GPUs.
    (UUM-159653)

- Graphics: Avoid crashing when uploading skinned mesh buffers to the GPU fails using GPU Batched skinning mode. The upload failure now results in a clear error message and skinned meshes may render incorrectly each frame the failure occurs.
    (UUM-141190)

- Graphics: Component Activation/Deactivation tracked by `ObjectDispatcher`
    ([UUM-145787](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145787))

- Graphics: Ensure correct backbuffer dimensions when using multiple displays.
    (UUM-134688)

- Graphics: Ensured that GPU skinning intermediate buffers are in GPU memory to improve performance on discrete GPUs.
    ([UUM-132599](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132599))

- Graphics: Fix crash when importing Texture2DArrays with small dimensions and many elements
    ([UUM-153670](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153670))

- Graphics: Fix the right eye in XR multi-pass selecting different LODs than the left eye when using camera stacking
    (UUM-157818)

- Graphics: Fixed a CPU performance regression when submitting draw calls in Scriptable Render Pipelines.
    (UUM-153988)

- Graphics: Fixed a crash in CommandBuffer.SetComputeConstantBufferParam and CommandBuffer.SetRayTracingConstantBufferParam when the buffer is null. A null buffer now removes the constant buffer that was set on the compute or ray tracing shader.

- Graphics: Fixed a crash in Mesh.GetIndices after Mesh.SetLod, Mesh.SetLods, or MeshData.SetLod accepted a MeshLodRange whose indexStart plus indexCount exceeded the 32-bit range.
    ([UUM-158653](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158653))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash in Mesh.Optimize when a submesh was set with MeshUpdateFlags.DontRecalculateBounds and its vertex range did not cover its indices.
    ([UUM-159311](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159311))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash on quit in players that use split graphics jobs with DirectX 12 on Universal Windows Platform.
    (UUM-152563)

- Graphics: Fixed a crash on Vulkan when pre-warming pipeline state objects with GraphicsStateCollection.WarmUpProgressively.
    (UUM-154071)

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when a build started while occlusion culling was baking in the background.
    ([UUM-114053](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-114053))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when calling Graphics.CreateGraphicsFence or CommandBuffer.CreateGraphicsFence with an undefined GraphicsFenceType value.
    ([UUM-151166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151166))

- Graphics: Fixed a crash when calling Mesh.GetIndices, Mesh.SetIndices or Mesh.SetTriangles after changing Mesh.indexFormat on a mesh with Mesh LODs. Changing Mesh.indexFormat now also resets Mesh.lodCount to one.
    ([UUM-159092](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159092))

- Graphics: Fixed a GPU hang that left the screen black when rendering with the Unity Graphics Kernel on Vulkan on Samsung Xclipse GPUs with Vulkan 1.1 drivers.
    (UUM-159650)

- Graphics: Fixed a GPU hang with DirectX 12 ray tracing under high GPU memory pressure, for example when baking Adaptive Probe Volumes in a scene with a large Terrain.
    ([UUM-130872](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-130872))

- Graphics: Fixed a native memory leak when using the UnityGraphicsKernel \(UGK\) backend with Structured or IndirectArgs buffers.
    (UUM-159134)

- Graphics: Fixed a player crash on quit when cache miss tracing was enabled and a scene had been loaded.
    ([UUM-154784](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154784))

- Graphics: Fixed a potential freeze or crash during scene loads when crunch-compressed cubemaps stream in at half-resolution or lower.
    (UUM-150567)

- Graphics: Fixed a rare memory corruption in the Direct3D 12 renderer when warming up a GraphicsStateCollection.
    (UUM-153131)

- Graphics: Fixed a Z-Fighting issue happening on arm-based macs with depth priming enabled. Re-enabling depth priming for WebGPU builds.
    (UUM-158250)

- Graphics: Fixed an assert "Metal: memoryless texture requires 2D texture." that the Editor logged on Metal when it rendered single-pass XR to memoryless texture arrays.
    (UUM-154442)

- Graphics: Fixed an assertion error logged to the Console when loading RenderDoc in the Editor with the Direct3D 12 graphics API.
    (UUM-115796)

- Graphics: Fixed an Editor crash on DirectX 12 when the Frame Debugger inspected a draw that binds a GraphicsBuffer-backed constant buffer inside a native render pass.
    ([UUM-150243](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150243))

- Graphics: Fixed an InvalidOperationException when a script disposed the destination NativeArray of an in-flight AsyncGPUReadback request on exiting Play Mode.
    ([UUM-143136](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143136))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue on DirectX 12 and Metal where calling ClearRenderTarget inside a render pass replaced custom view and projection matrices set with SetGlobalMatrix, such as those set by RenderingUtils.SetViewAndProjectionMatrices in URP.
    ([UUM-138530](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138530))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue on OpenGL ES and WebGL where a constant buffer bound from script with SetConstantBuffer or SetGlobalConstantBuffer stayed bound for later draws that don't bind it, which then read stale data or, on WebGL, were skipped.

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where a Line Renderer or Trail Renderer using a 2D sprite material became invisible after turning off Preview in the Animation window or after calling Renderer.SetPropertyBlock.
    ([UUM-156620](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156620))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where URP and HDRP did not upgrade materials when you imported a project with -batchmode -quit.
    ([UUM-153768](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153768))

- Graphics: Fixed an issue where warming up a GraphicsStateCollection created with GraphicsState.SetMeshData for a skinned mesh created a pipeline that didn't match the draw, so the pipeline was still created at first draw on Vulkan.
    (UUM-156721)

- Graphics: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory read when updating graphics buffers on Metal.
    (UUM-153132)

- Graphics: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory read when warming up shader variants on Metal.
    (UUM-153134)

- Graphics: Fixed AsyncGPUReadback.RequestIntoNativeArray logging read/write access and undisposable errors for a NativeArray that was used by a completed job.
    ([UUM-102552](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-102552))

- Graphics: Fixed batch mode builds overwriting custom render queue settings in materials.
    ([UUM-147947](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147947))

- Graphics: Fixed crash in `GraphicsCaps:IsFormatSupported` when creating a RenderTexture with an out-of-range RenderTextureFormat value.
    ([UUM-158897](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158897))

- Graphics: Fixed crash in `RenderTarget::SetActive` when Asset import is triggered, and the camera's target buffers were set using `Camera.SetTargetBuffers`.
    ([UUM-148003](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148003))

- Graphics: Fixed DirectX 12 validation errors and possible loss of render target contents in merged native render passes, such as URP Deferred, when not using native graphics jobs, including when a native plugin event flushes GPU work inside the render pass.
    (UUM-157002)

- Graphics: Fixed GLES render pass failure with unsupported MSAA auto-resolve to backbuffer.
    (UUM-152645)

- Graphics: Fixed Graphics State Collection assets not reimporting when a shader they reference changes.

- Graphics: Fixed GraphicsStateCollection.cacheMissCollection becoming null and cache miss tracing stopping after Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets was called.
    (UUM-161021)

- Graphics: Fixed memory corruption when BC-compressed textures are decompressed on the CPU, such as when the GPU does not support the format, and a mip level is smaller than 4x4 or not a multiple of 4 in size.
    (UUM-154799)

- Graphics: Fixed Mesh.CombineMeshes copying the wrong vertices and writing out-of-range indices when a source sub-mesh uses a non-zero baseVertex.
    ([UUM-161368](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161368))

- Graphics: Fixed misaligned warning icon in shader Inspector.
    ([UUM-154699](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154699))

- Graphics: Fixed missing objects and shadows in Quad Views with camera stacking
    ([UUM-150107](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150107))

- Graphics: Fixed multi-second stall on UI thread when backgrounding an app with Vulkan, caused by surface not being released and surfaceDestroyed waiting on the engine callbacks.
    ([UUM-149425](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149425))

- Graphics: Fixed offscreen framebuffer resolution being updated late by one frame on device rotation with OpenGLES.
    ([UUM-157974](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157974))

- Graphics: Fixed reflection probes intermittently missing in URP on some Vulkan Android devices.
    (UUM-155930)

- Graphics: Fixed render graph errors on Vulkan when a memoryless render texture, such as the XR eye depth with Optimize Buffer Discards enabled, is used across several native render passes.
    (UUM-159183)

- Graphics: Fixed Shader Constant Defines UI ignoring first click.
    ([UUM-161026](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161026))

- Graphics: Fixed slow render graph compilation in the Editor and in players with script checks enabled. Pass names longer than 61 bytes are no longer truncated in the Render Graph Viewer.
    (UUM-154008)

- Graphics: Fixed SSAO rendering incorrectly in the right eye when using single-pass multiview XR \(Vulkan / OpenGL ES\)
    (UUM-142984)

- Graphics: Fixed SystemInfo.supportsConservativeRaster returning false and conservative rasterization being unavailable on Vulkan on GPUs that report a maxExtraPrimitiveOverestimationSize of 0, such as AMD GPUs.
    ([UUM-143199](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143199))

- Graphics: Fixed the core render pipeline FoveatedRemapDensity\(\) shader function to correclty return density rather than treating the return value as a UV. Fixed per platform fallbacks for RemapFoveatedRenderingDensity\(\) when it is disabled or not supported to return 1,1 which is the standard density.
    (UUM-150255)

- Graphics: Fixed the Editor freezing during an asset import on Linux when running on OpenGL.

- Graphics: Fixed the image rendering upside down in XR with Tile-Only Mode and MSAA.
    ([UUM-158825](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158825))

- Graphics: Fixed untyped texture in ProbeVolume shader.

- Graphics: Fixed URP reflection probes rendering black on some Vulkan Android devices.
    (UUM-154967)

- Graphics: Fixed use-after-free of Vulkan scratch memory.
    ([UUM-151539](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151539))

- Graphics: Fixed Windows player crash when using Vulkan graphcis API on projects that repeatedly change display settings.
    (UUM-154406)

- Graphics: Fixes a crash in the Windows player with the Vulkan backend when the window is minimized.
    (UUM-160527)

- Graphics: Runtime crash due to a malformed BatchDrawRange has been fixed.
    ([UUM-151686](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151686))

- Graphics: SetVertices\(\) no longer causes a crash if the provided start and length arguments are large enough to cause an integer overflow when added together.
    ([UUM-154564](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154564))

- Graphics: Shader Build Settings Build Profile Override now uses Project Settings as a base.
    (UUM-156533)

- Graphics: Shader Build Settings UI now shows error for invalid Shader Constant Defines.
    ([UUM-153573](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153573))

- Graphics: SkinnedMeshRenderers added through scripting with UpdateWhenOffscreen enabled no longer calculate their bounds on the main thread every frame.

- Graphics: Texture2D.ReadPixels and ScreenCapture could return stale data on AMD GPUs with Vulkan when graphics jobs were enabled and the GPU was busy.
    (UUM-149529)

- Graphics: Unexpected failures in Vulkan swapchain are now correctly logged.
    ([UUM-153744](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153744))

- Graphics: URP Decals shader could rarely produce NaN values on Android platforms when sampling non-normal-map textures set as the Normal Map, especially visible as white flashes when in conjunction with other amplifying effects like Bloom.
    ([UUM-153167](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153167))

- Graphics: Vulkan: Fixed a random crash caused by an invalid shader module when shaders were unloaded and reloaded with graphics jobs enabled.
    (UUM-136086)

- Graphics: When On-Tile Only mode, Deferred rendering, and MSAA are enabled, platforms that support MSAA back buffer now actually fallback to Forward\(+\) at runtime, as described by the Editor Inspector warning.
    ([UUM-155206](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155206))

- Graphics: `SystemInfo.supportsParallelPSOCreation` now correctly returns `true` on<br>
    Android devices with a single core enabling parallel PSO creation.
    (UUM-153032)

- GraphView: Fixed an issue where the Create from Template window selected a different template when a search was cleared.
    (UUM-157464)

- HDRP: Fix shadergraph compilation failure on uv4 input semantic differences.
    (UUM-153267)

- HDRP: Fixed "Default" being added to some Enum Material properties
    ([UUM-134256](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134256))

- HDRP: Fixed a black Game view and Render Graph errors when using the ScreenSpaceAmbientOcclusion Fullscreen Debug Mode with MSAA.
    ([UUM-157335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157335))

- HDRP: Fixed a dark outline appearing around geometry against the sky when MSAA and a cloud layer are enabled.
    ([UUM-150911](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150911))

- HDRP: Fixed an issue where FSR2 upscaler resources leaked GPU memory each time HDRP rebuilt the render pipeline, for example after a quality level change.
    (UUM-154186)

- HDRP: Fixed an issue where HDRP did not release the contexts and textures of upscaler framework upscalers when the render pipeline was rebuilt.
    (UUM-156238)

- HDRP: Fixed an issue where two Diffusion Profiles could silently share the same hash. The second profile now gets a unique hash and a warning is logged.
    ([UUM-158938](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158938))

- HDRP: Fixed CoreCLR lifecycle management on various systems
    (UUM-147914)

- HDRP: Fixed HDRP structures being stripped from builds when Managed Stripping Level is High
    ([UUM-128748](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-128748))

- HDRP: Fixed LOD crossfade dithering in Shader Graph materials, which could show a fully alpha-clipped LOD during the transition.
    ([UUM-154532](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154532))

- HDRP: Fixed Metal warnings about _StencilTexture when transparent Unlit materials with fog are visible in the Scene view in Unlit draw mode.
    ([UUM-142978](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142978))

- HDRP: Fixed overlapping labels in the HDRP Quality Settings panel where quality level tags overlapped with asset names.
    ([UUM-151061](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151061))

- HDRP: Fixed shader compilation errors and incorrect base map results for TerrainLit Shader Graphs.
    ([UUM-153008](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153008))

- HDRP: Fixed the Disable Mip Caching tooltip in the Rendering Debugger so that it refers to the Texture Streamer and Mipmap Streaming instead of internal class names.
    ([UUM-154139](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154139))

- HDRP: Fixed warning in `ScriptableRuntimeReflectionSystemSettings` when re-entering play mode
    ([UUM-150539](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150539))

- HDRP: Fixed water surfaces that disappeared for a moment in the Editor when a scene had water surfaces with different band counts.
    (UUM-141715)

- HDRP: Six-way smoke lit particles now receive shadows cast by volumetric clouds
    ([UUM-159322](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159322))

- IL2CPP: .Equals and .GetHashCode now throw a NotSupportedException for InlineArray types

- IL2CPP: Added support for generic types and generic methods in `UnsafeAccessorAttribute`.

- IL2CPP: Added WebGL ThreadPool::MainThreadScheduleBackgroundJob support.

- IL2CPP: Comparing a `Type` against a generic type definition such as `typeof(List<>)` in generic code could return the wrong result or fail to convert.

- IL2CPP: Error messages for P/Invoke libraries that fail to load on iOS, Linux and macOS no longer list impossible paths, and on iOS an absolute library path's real load failure is reported first.

- IL2CPP: Fixed a C++ compile error when user code calls RuntimeHelpers.InitializeArray with a field handle that does not come directly from an ldtoken.
    ([UUM-154626](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154626))

- IL2CPP: Fixed a crash on exit that could occur in development players with script debugging enabled.
    (UUM-155863)

- IL2CPP: Fixed a crash when calling GetType\(\) through a boxed Nullable&lt;T&gt; that has no value, in generic code built with Faster \(smaller\) builds.
    (UUM-152751)

- IL2CPP: Fixed a crash when passing a string parameter marked as `MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.VBByRefString)` to native code.

- IL2CPP: Fixed a security vulnerability \(CVE-2026-111497\) in the Brotli decoder used by System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream and BrotliDecoder, where decoding untrusted data could allow remote code execution.

- IL2CPP: Fixed Environment.WorkingSet being 0 on the WebGL target.

- IL2CPP: Fixed FileSystemWatcher causing shutdown hang on Linux/Android in some cases

- IL2CPP: Fixed GC marking on InlineArray types.

- IL2CPP: Fixed IL2CPP player builds failing with UnityLinker errors IL1009 and IL1040 when a plugin folder that contains player assemblies also contains an editor-only assembly with an assembly-level attribute from an assembly that is not part of the build.
    ([UUM-158594](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158594))

- IL2CPP: Fixed incorrect results from type checks, casts and GetType on boxed values in shared generic code.

- IL2CPP: Fixed issue were Type.IsAssignableFrom would throw a TypeInitializationException when called on a class whose static constructor had thrown.
    ([UUM-156129](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156129))

- IL2CPP: Fixed issue where memory from a thread object could be kept alive by the GC past its exit
    ([UUM-149541](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149541))

- IL2CPP: Fixed issue where RuntimeTypeHandle equality checks may fail.

- IL2CPP: Fixed marshaling `System.Object` as `VARIANT` when it is being returned from P/Invoke method with `IsPreserveSig=false`.

- IL2CPP: Fixed missing GC write barrier in `Thread.VolatileWrite(ref object, object)`.
    (UUM-155582)

- IL2CPP: Fixed missing incremental GC write barriers on static and ThreadStatic fields on ref struct types.
    ([UUM-153622](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153622))

- IL2CPP: Fixed P/Invoke on Linux Players prepending the Player folder to an absolute `DllImport` library path, which hid the real path and load error in the resulting exception.
    ([UUM-155034](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155034))

- IL2CPP: Fixed rare C++ error that could cause compiler errors converting types at call sites
    (UUM-154470)

- IL2CPP: Fixed truncated and/or incorrect locale information for the WebGL target

- IL2CPP: Moved platform RuntimeProfile's into per platform assemblies
    (UUM-148613)

- IL2CPP: Raise ArrayTypeMismatchException if invalid generic delegate is stored in covariant array.
    (UUM-155605)

- IL2CPP: Re-enabled two tests on WebGL; added support for expecting a TypeInitializationException to be thrown

- IMGUI: Fixed an edited text field displaying its uncommitted text on another element, and keystrokes reaching that element, when a list in an IMGUI inspector is partially scrolled.
    ([UUM-150677](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150677))

- IMGUI: Fixed an edited text field displaying its uncommitted text on another visible element when scrolling a list in an IMGUI inspector.
    ([UUM-150677](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150677))

- IMGUI: Fixed icons and text being clipped inside IMGUI controls at fractional display scales such as 150%.
    (UUM-135622)

- IMGUI: Fixed IMGUI input and hover on a secondary window registering on the main display on QNX and Embedded Linux.
    (UUM-151157)

- IMGUI: Fixed IMGUI text drawing a few pixels past the borders of enclosing scroll views and containers when the text overflowed its control.
    ([UUM-150041](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150041))

- IMGUI: Fixed IMGUI text fields, such as Transform values in the Inspector, sometimes showing another value's text \(for example 0 and 1 swapped\) while a window was being resized.
    ([UUM-147879](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147879))

- IMGUI: Fixed long Editor tooltips, such as the backup confirmation toggle in Project Auditor, showing empty space to the right of their wrapped text.
    ([UUM-152749](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152749))

- IMGUI: Fixed text fields not scrolling to keep the cursor visible when typing text longer than the visible field width
    ([UUM-149213](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149213))

- IMGUI: Fixed the text cursor being vertically misaligned in an empty text field.
    ([UUM-145875](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145875))

- Input: Fixed a regression that added roughly 60 ms to editor project load time when input devices were first detected.
    (UUM-148220)

- Input System: Fix layout merge for controls declared for several variants.
    ([UUM-160829](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160829))

- Input System: Fix MakeUniqueName overflow and Device Simulator help text.

- Input System: Fix misaligned labels in the Input Actions editor properties pane.

- Input System: Fix single clicks generating duplicated touch events, when using Device Simulator and Touch Simulation. When the Device Simulator view is enabled Touch Simulation events are supressed.
    (UUM-151404)

- Input System: Fixed a crash \(ArgumentException/NullReferenceException\) when a touchscreen device connects before EnhancedTouchSupport finishes initializing.
    (UUM-153111)

- Input System: Fixed a NullReferenceException when reopening a device's Device Debug View window in the Input Debugger after it had previously been maximized and closed.
    ([UUM-150951](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150951))

- Input System: Fixed an Input Actions importer error message that incorrectly reported a duplicate Action Map name when the actual problem was a duplicate Action name within an Action Map.
    ([UUM-153706](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153706))

- Input System: Fixed an InputEventTrace with a fixed size dropping an extra event and miscounting its size when it wrapped over events whose size is not a multiple of 4 bytes, such as mouse state events.
    (UUM-159846)

- Input System: Fixed an issue on Windows where clicking or dragging the title bar to refocus a windowed Player registered as a left mouse click in the game.
    ([UUM-149740](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149740))

- Input System: Fixed an overlapping memory copy when removing an element from an InputControlList, which AddressSanitizer reported as memcpy-param-overlap.
    (UUM-151624)

- Input System: Fixed device layout matchers not matching decimal capability values given as strings, for example in JSON layouts, on systems set to a culture such as Swedish or French.
    (UUM-154506)

- Input System: Fixed devices that were disconnected or reconfigured while the application was out of focus staying in the device list.
    (UUM-151588)

- Input System: Fixed exceptions thrown when accessing the control, valueType, or valueSizeInBytes properties of InputAction.CallbackContext after the action or its InputActionMap was disabled from within the callback.
    ([UUM-138896](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-138896))

- Input System: Fixed input events queued from InputSystem.onBeforeUpdate or InputSystem.onDeviceChange on a frame where a device was connected or changed being processed a frame late, or lost when the input event buffer was full.
    (UUM-158878)

- Input System: Fixed Input System actions enabled during startup, for example in an Entities system's OnCreate, being disabled when entering Play Mode with domain reload disabled and in Player builds.
    ([UUM-151403](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151403))

- Input System: Fixed InputSystem.onDeviceCommand subscriptions being ignored after any callback handled a device command.
    (UUM-160201)

- Input System: Fixed issue on macOS where third party tools sending repeated key down events without a key up could result in keys becoming stuck
    ([UUM-151878](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151878))

- Input System: Fixed key names showing garbled characters instead of accented letters with the French \(Standard, AZERTY\) keyboard layout on Windows 11 version 24H2 and later.
    ([UUM-144382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144382))

- Input System: Fixed Keyboard.anyKey.wasPressedThisFrame not returning true when a key is pressed while another key is held, which means it can now be true several times without wasReleasedThisFrame in between.
    ([UUM-144317](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144317))

- Input System: Fixed keys held when a Windows Player lost focus staying pressed after the Player regained focus.
    ([UUM-157482](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157482))

- Input System: Fixed Mouse.clickCount increasing on every pointer move while a mouse button was held on Windows.
    (UUM-158462)

- Input System: Fixed OnMouseOver and OnMouseDrag only being called on frames where the pointer moved, instead of every frame while the pointer is over the Collider. With a touchscreen, the Collider under the last touch position now keeps receiving OnMouseOver after the touch ends, matching the legacy Input Manager.
    ([UUM-153115](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153115))

- Input System: Fixed polling for interaction phases \(such as `WasPerformedThisFrame`\) being missed during `Update` due to interaction timeouts sometimes occurring during the later before-render period when a before-render device \(such as an XR HMD\) is added. This means that timeouts and interactions like Hold may now wait to perform until the next frame instead of the earliest chance during before-render.
    ([UUM-147719](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147719))

- Input System: Fixed the "Location Distance Threshold" label being clipped and misaligned on the Input System Settings project settings page.
    ([UUM-151646](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151646))

- Input System: Fixed the context menu shown when right-clicking an action map, action or composite being renamed in the Input Actions editor, which now only shows Cut, Copy and Paste.
    (UUM-160353)

- Input System: Fixed the error "type is not a supported string value" that was logged in the Console when viewing binding properties in the Input Actions Editor.
    ([UUM-155527](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155527))

- Input System: Fixed the Input Actions editor moving the selection to the next action, and expanding it, when undoing a duplicated binding.
    ([UUM-153990](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153990))

- Input System: Fixed the Input Actions editor window being recreated \(flickering\) when cancelling the unsaved-changes prompt on close; the window now uses the Editor's standard unsaved-changes handling.
    (UUM-154338)

- Input System: Fixed the Input Debugger device window showing a blank window after the device was disconnected and reconnected.
    ([UUM-144312](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144312))

- Input System: Fixed the Input System Package settings window accepting negative and non-finite \(NaN or Infinity\) values for Location Accuracy and Location Distance Threshold instead of clamping them to 0 like the scripting API.
    ([UUM-151645](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151645))

- Input System: Fixed the Player Input component Inspector not showing the action map events after the Behavior is set to Invoke Unity Events, until the GameObject was selected again.
    ([UUM-150956](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150956))

- Input System: Fixed the Processors list for a composite part binding, such as the W key in a WASD composite, offering processors for the composite's value type \(Vector2\) instead of the part's value type \(float\).
    ([UUM-144319](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144319))

- Input System: Fixed the Read more link in the Update Mode warning of the Input System settings opening a documentation page that does not describe Update Mode.
    ([UUM-154791](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154791))

- Input System: Fixed touch input from Unity Remote 5 being ignored when using the Input System package.
    ([UUM-155327](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155327))

- Input System: Fixed UI Toolkit pointer events reporting Shift as pressed after typing a capital letter in the Device Simulator on macOS.
    ([UUM-156797](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156797))

- Input System: Fixed UI Toolkit receiving an extra PointerDownEvent when dragging a finger on a touchscreen with the Input System package.
    ([UUM-155740](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155740))

- Input System: This fixes Windows' Xbox Game Bar and macOS overlays \(Spotlight et al\). On Windows, Steam/Discord/NVIDIA overlays work as always.
    ([UUM-150045](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150045))

- iOS: Fix building UnityFramework directly via command line
    (UUM-151410)

- iOS: Fix GameAssembly ignoring the target OS version from Player Settings
    (UUM-113588)

- iOS: Fixed a crash in `UnityCheckUnloadAndQuit` when a batch-mode player quits while its host process keeps running, for example in an XCTest with a quit handler or in Unity as a Library.
    (UUM-159471)

- iOS: Fixed a crash when `application:openURL` or `application:continueUserActivity` was called before Unity initialized.
    ([UUM-151801](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151801))

- iOS: Fixed crash on FindWorkspaceDocumentForPath when the Xcode process launched by Build And Run is replaced by another application.
    ([UUM-156774](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156774))

- iOS: Fixed incomplete privacy manifest when integrating only UnityFramework.framework \(Unity as a Library\).

- iOS: Fixed Input System wrong touch delta when scene is changed
    ([UUM-149340](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149340))

- iOS: Fixed the privacy manifest in iOS Xcode projects not declaring the data types collected when Diagnostic Data Collection is enabled.
    (UUM-159168)

- iOS: Fixed the secondary display mirroring the device screen instead of showing its own camera view on iOS 27.
    (UUM-149106)

- iOS: Preserve the movie pause state after returning from background
    (UUM-149894)

- iOS: Rename AppDelegate and SceneDelegate class to be consistent with other naming
    (UUM-153724)

- Kernel: Fixed a crash when JobsUtility.CreateJobReflectionData was called with a null Type. An ArgumentNullException is now thrown instead.
    ([UUM-150954](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150954))

- Kernel: Fixed a rare crash in the Editor on Windows when freeing memory during Asset Database startup.
    ([UUM-159502](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159502))

- Kernel: Fixed a stack overflow when converting a large NativeText or UnsafeText to a string, and when appending a large string to a NativeText, UnsafeText, or FixedString.
    ([UUM-124992](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-124992))

- Kernel: Fixed crash in DomainUnloadAutoFree memory tracker.
    ([UUM-144552](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144552))

- Kernel: Fixed crash in DomainUnloadAutoFree memory tracker.
    ([UUM-150681](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150681))

- Kernel: Fixed crash in NativeSlice caused by 32bit integer overflow.
    (UUM-154594)

- Kernel: Fixed crash in UnsafeList::RemoveRange\(\) if count overflows a 32bit integer.
    (UUM-155334)

- Kernel: Fixed FixedString and NativeText Append\(Unicode.Rune\) leaving the string 4 bytes longer with stale bytes when appending an invalid rune.
    ([UUM-158951](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158951))

- Kernel: Fixed FixedString CopyFrom clearing the destination string when it returns CopyError.Truncation.
    ([UUM-158961](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158961))

- Kernel: Fixed FixedString*N*Bytes storing invalid UTF-8 and comparing unequal to the same text when a surrogate pair was appended one char at a time.
    ([UUM-158952](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158952))

- Kernel: Fixed FixedString.Append\(float\) writing a wrong, larger number for subnormal float values.
    ([UUM-158950](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158950))

- Kernel: Fixed FixedString.Substring returning invalid UTF-8 for multi-byte characters by treating startIndex and length as character offsets.
    ([UUM-158963](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158963))

- Kernel: Fixed InvalidOperationException when calling SortJob on a NativeArray that a scheduled job is still writing to.
    ([UUM-158989](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158989))

- Kernel: Fixed InvalidOperationException when scheduling a SortJob or SortJobDefer from Burst compiled code.
    ([UUM-125134](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-125134))

- Kernel: Fixed math.orthonormalize returning a non-orthonormal matrix when the first column was not unit length.
    ([UUM-157877](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157877))

- Kernel: Fixed NativeList and UnsafeList copying the whole allocated capacity instead of only the used elements when the capacity grows.
    ([UUM-129943](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-129943))

- Kernel: Fixed NativeText.Substring\(int, AllocatorManager.AllocatorHandle\) ignoring the allocator argument and allocating the result with the source string's allocator.
    (UUM-159919)

- Kernel: Fixed safety handles accumulating in memory when repeatedly creating and disposing native containers that use a custom allocator.
    ([UUM-134028](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134028))

- Kernel: Fixed the CPU and GPU frame time in the CPU Usage module toolbar not updating when changing frames in the New Timeline view.
    (UUM-156968)

- Kernel: Fixed the safety system naming the container instead of the ParallelWriter when reporting errors for a disposed NativeParallelMultiHashMap.ParallelWriter.

- Kernel: Fixed Unity.Mathematics math.mul\(AffineTransform, float3x3\) multiplying the matrices in the wrong order. The result now matches math.mul\(a, new AffineTransform\(b\)\). Code that relied on the old result may see different output if the matrix does not commute with the AffineTransform rotation.
    ([UUM-158609](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158609))

- Kernel: FixedString operator==\(String\) threw ArgumentException when the string exceeded the FixedString capacity.
    ([UUM-129279](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/argumentexception-is-thrown-when-comparing-fixedstring64bytes-with-a-string-where-the-fixedstring64bytes-is-on-the-left-hand-side-of-equals-equals-1))

- Kernel: pnoise, psrnoise and psrdnoise now repeat with the set period when the coordinates are negative
    ([UUM-158606](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158606))

- Kernel: Quaternion math.exp, math.unitexp, math.log and math.unitlog no longer return NaN when the vector part is zero.
    ([UUM-157957](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157957))

- Kernel: Windows standalone players crashed immediately on startup on CPUs without SSE4.2 support.
    ([UUM-155744](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155744))

- License: Bump ProxyHelper version to allow running on case-sensitive filesystem.
    ([UUM-155895](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155895))

- License: Fixed an issue where the Editor continued without a license, and exited with code 0 in batch mode, when it could not connect to the Unity Licensing Client.
    ([UUM-151817](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151817))

- License: Fixed the license error dialog looping on Retry and close when two Editors of the same version required incompatible Licensing Client versions.
    (UUM-153121)

- Linux: Fixed a crash on Linux when running without an initialized video subsystem, such as in batch mode with -nographics or without a display.
    ([UUM-156559](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156559))

- Linux: Fixed a crash on Linux when the Editor was launched with -nographics but without -batchmode; the Editor now reports the invalid arguments and exits with code 1.
    ([UUM-154576](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154576))

- Linux: Fixed a crash on Linux when the window manager closed the Editor launch screen during project load.
    ([UUM-132175](https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-crash-on-editorlaunchscreen-platformeditorlaunchscreen-setprogress-when-opening-a-project))

- Linux: Fixed characters outside the Basic Multilingual Plane \(such as emoji\) being corrupted when typed into Editor text fields on Linux.
    (UUM-153851)

- Linux: Fixed GameWindow.GetPosition\(\) returning an incorrect value after MoveTo\(\) on Linux X11 sessions.
    (UUM-151056)

- Linux: Fixed intermittent crash in LinuxEditor when switching focus between Editor windows and other applications.
    ([UUM-161324](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161324))

- Linux: Fixed the Editor hanging on the splash screen when a dialog opened while a scene was loading.
    ([UUM-151324](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151324))

- macOS: Allow the system to sleep when Screen.sleepTimeout is set to SleepTimeout.SystemSetting and the System Setting is not Never.
    ([UUM-150222](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150222))

- macOS: Fixed a crash on recent macOS versions that could occur when importing X.509 certificates.
    (UUM-154511)

- macOS: Fixed a top-level menu returning to the wrong position in the menu bar after being removed and restored.

- macOS: Fixed an issue where an exported Xcode project failed to build at the code signing step when Burst debug symbols were included.
    (UUM-156483)

- macOS: Fixed an issue where background Editor windows flickered while a modal dialog was open.
    (UUM-160349)

- macOS: Fixed an issue where dragging the edge or corner of an Editor window on macOS 27 sometimes did not resize it.
    (UUM-160497)

- macOS: Fixed macOS Dedicated Server builds using CoreCLR failing to launch because the CoreCLR library could not be found.
    (UUM-155782)

- macOS: Fixed the Editor slowing down in batch mode and automated test runs on macOS when one of its windows lost focus.

- macOS: Fixed the macOS Editor ignoring internal tick requests while its window was unfocused, which made async and coroutine-based execution run slower than when focused.
    (UUM-151097)

- macOS: Fixed Unity Bug Reporter opening when a process started by the Editor, such as adb, crashed.
    ([UUM-158130](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158130))

- Mono: Fixed a security vulnerability \(CVE-2026-111497\) in the Brotli decoder used by System.IO.Compression.BrotliStream and BrotliDecoder, where decoding untrusted data could allow remote code execution.

- Mono: Fixed WebSocket connections to servers only supporting TLS 1.3.

- Mono: Stopped a crash when using a default GCHandle
    ([UUM-153835](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153835))

- Multiplayer: Fixed a crash on macOS when opening the Edit menu while a Multiplayer Play Mode virtual player was focused.
    ([UUM-155708](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155708))

- Multiplayer: Fixed a NullReferenceException thrown when the Launching Scenario window is opened without an active scenario, which left the window empty.
    ([UUM-151897](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151897))

- Multiplayer: Fixed a stale "This instance is currently active" message shown for an Additional Editor Instance in the Play Mode Scenarios window after the instance was no longer active.
    ([UUM-150414](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150414))

- Multiplayer: Fixed an "Unknown pseudo class" warning being logged in the Console when opening the Multiplayer Center window.
    ([UUM-160252](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160252))

- Multiplayer: Fixed an Additional Editor Instance still showing as "Running" in the Active Scenario window after its player process was terminated externally and the recovery offer was declined.
    ([UUM-151746](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151746))

- Multiplayer: Fixed an error on Windows where Multiplayer Play Mode could fail to save player settings, such as tags, while additional Editor instances were running, because SystemData.json was in use by another process.
    (UUM-155061)

- Multiplayer: Fixed an issue where the Play Mode Scenarios window kept showing an Additional Editor Instance as active after it stopped running.
    ([UUM-150414](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150414))

- Multiplayer: Fixed an issue where the Unity Transport network simulator reordered packets that were sent in the same update with a send delay and no jitter.
    ([UUM-159120](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159120))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Active Scenario window warning for an invalid scenario referring to an "Edit Configuration" button instead of the "Edit Scenario" button.
    ([UUM-155747](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155747))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Focus on Player button icon being barely visible in the Active Scenario window when using the Light Editor Theme.
    ([UUM-144477](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144477))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Focus on Player option in the Multiplayer Play Mode window staying enabled and doing nothing when the player is not active.
    ([UUM-145368](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145368))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Multiplayer Center window letting the divider be dragged until the recommendations panel was hidden.
    ([UUM-145592](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145592))

- Multiplayer: Fixed the Multiplayer Play Mode window and virtual players not working after Multiplayer Play Mode was disabled and re-enabled in Preferences.
    (UUM-153152)

- Multiplayer: Fixed the multiplayer role indicator not appearing in the Hierarchy window when the legacy Hierarchy was disabled.
    ([UUM-153396](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153396))

- Multiplayer: Fixed two "Lifecycle ERROR" exceptions logged on every Editor start and domain reload, which left Multiplayer Play Mode uninitialized, in projects upgraded from an older Multiplayer Play Mode version.
    ([UUM-151881](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151881))

- Multiplayer: Fixing the scaling artifacts of the Multiplayer Center window's genre titles in Bitmap rendering mode.

- Multiplayer: Multiplayer Play Mode additional Editor instance keeps running outdated script code when domain reload is disabled
    ([UUM-151263](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151263))

- Multiplayer: Multiplayer Play Mode clone Editors didn't reload an open scene after an external tool modified the scene file on disk.

- Multiplayer: Multiplayer Play Mode: Fixed the Unity Netcode Play Mode Tools layout option not opening the Play Mode Tools window, in Virtual Players for Netcode 7.0+.
    (UUM-155623)

- Multiplayer: Play Mode Scenario stays in the "Deploying" state when re-entering Play Mode with an additional Editor instance still running.
    ([UUM-151263](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151263))

- Multiplayer: Removed the Tree creation option from the hierarchy menu to prevent asset creation in Multiplayer Playmode Virtual Players, as asset authoring is forbidden.
    ([UUM-148223](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148223))

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Correctly recreate UDP sockets on iOS when they are closed by the OS when an application is in the background for a while. Prevents a condition when the driver seemingly stops receiving packets.
    ([UUM-153993](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153993))

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Fixed a memory leak when closing connections using DTLS or TLS.
    ([UUM-151755](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151755))

- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Fixed an issue where sending packets on UDP connections that don't have a remote peer anymore could eventually result in exhausting the receive queue, causing the server to not be able to receive anything anymore.
    (UUM-151844)
