- Multiplayer: Unity Transport: Fixed an issue with the `NetcodeInterop` layer which would cause connection failures when using NGO transports that use a single buffer for all receive operations \(for example the community Facepunch transport does this\).
    (UUM-151803)

- N/A \(internal\): Changing editor config write mode to atomic.

- N/A \(internal\): Engine Diagnostics: The Editor now saves the latest build settings answer from the remote settings service, encrypted, to Library/Insights/build-settings.bin. It reuses that answer for 24 hours while the service URL, project ID and Editor version stay the same, instead of fetching it again. Android and iOS builds fall back to it when offline, or before the first fetch of a session completes. \(JIRA-ID\)'.

- N/A \(internal\): Fix for test OnRedirect_LoopStopsAndClearsRunning instability.

- N/A \(internal\): Fixes for remote settings issues.

- N/A \(internal\): No release notes, config change

- N/A \(internal\): No release notes, Remote settings cleanup.

- N/A \(internal\): Refactor editor config scan

- N/A \(internal\): Removing unneeded features in remote settings

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed the input gathering and GhostOwner code samples in the Netcode for Entities manual throwing an InvalidOperationException every frame when a client connects to a single-world host.
    ([UUM-159972](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159972))

- Netcode for Entities: Fixed the Netcode for Entities documentation to explain that on a single-world host, the SimulationSystemGroup updates once per frame and only the PredictedSimulationSystemGroup updates at the server tick rate.
    ([UUM-159940](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159940))

- Networking: Changed default random seed device from `/dev/random` to `/dev/urandom`

- Networking: Fixed a crash on Editor, Player, or Virtual Player shutdown when a DownloadHandlerScript or DownloadHandlerStream was still active while the underlying web request system was being torn down.
    (UUM-151294)

- Networking: Fixed the search and summary rows in the Webrequest Profiler shrinking as the request list filled.

- Networking: Fixed UnityWebRequest cookies occasionally reverting to an older value when a slower request completed after a faster concurrent request to the same host had already set a newer cookie.
    ([UUM-150958](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150958))

- Networking: Fixes a bug where streaming upload handler that has no fixed length incorrectly generates underlying call with fixed length
    (UUM-152488)

- Networking: Reject TLS connections to servers using the compromised eMudhra certificates.
    (UUM-158231)

- Networking: Unity Transport: fix a possible failure to re-establish a connection when re-homing \(when the local IP address changes\) if using DTLS.
    (UUM-154831)

- Networking: Unity Transport: Fix a small memory leak that would occur when creating TLS/DTLS sessions.
    ([UUM-151755](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151755))

- Networking: Unity Transport: Fix a stack corruption vulnerability in the WebSocket code.
    (UUM-160664)

- Networking: Unity Transport: Fix out-of-bound memory accesses after receiving an invalid handshake message.
    (UUM-160664)

- Networking: Unity Transport: When a DTLS connection fails because of a wrong certificate, the reported disconnection reason is now the more correct `AuthenticationFailure` instead of `MaxConnectionAttempts`.
    (UUM-159110)

- Networking: UnityWebRequest: Fixed a freeze when quitting a standalone player while an ongoing request was still resolving DNS against an unresponsive or slow DNS server.
    ([UUM-151643](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151643))

- Package: Fixed the "List is empty" placeholder text overlapping toolbar buttons in the Addressables Report window when the window was resized to a short vertical height.
    ([UUM-149865](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149865))

- Package Manager: A package tarball download that fails now reports the package, the host it was fetched from, the bytes transferred against the expected total, the elapsed time and the underlying error code, instead of only the raw stream error \(for example `aborted`\).
    (UUM-147418)

- Package Manager: Added \[Package Manager\] tag to error strings emitted by Package Manager CLI operations.
    ([UUM-154572](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154572))

- Package Manager: Fix importing a .unitypackage through CLI shows limited trust, drag-and-dropping the same package shows full trust
    (UUM-148788)

- Package Manager: Fix nested asset packages from trusted upm packages show untrusted popup \(UUM-159886\)
    (UUM-159886)

- Package Manager: Fix warning messages when Asset package is imported but not trusted in batchmode
    (UUM-148786)

- Package Manager: Fixed a warning about a duplicate "services" extension page that was logged when toggling UI Toolkit Live Reload while the Package Manager window was maximized.
    ([UUM-155986](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155986))

- Package Manager: Fixed an error being logged when cancelling a package import from the trust window or the complete project import dialog.
    (UUM-148792)

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where the export button was active for packages with an invalid path error.
    (UUM-145107)

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where the Package Manager log file stayed at the info level regardless of the requested log level.
    (UUM-153643)

- Package Manager: Fixed an issue where `AssetDatabase.importPackageCancelled` is not raised when Cancel is clicked in the signature window of a non-interactive .unitypackage import
    ([UUM-154997](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154997))

- Package Manager: Fixed exporting a signed .unitypackage failing with "IOException: Stream was too long." when the uncompressed package content exceeded 2 GB, and reduced Editor memory usage during package export and signing.
    ([UUM-153848](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153848))

- Package Manager: Fixed the Editor becoming unresponsive when the package trust window appeared during Editor initialization.
    ([UUM-153544](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153544))

- Package Manager: Fixed the issue where non discoverable packages show up in Unity Registry page incorrectly.
    (UUM-159783)

- Package Manager: Fixed the issue where unity lifecycle deprecation information was not shown correctly in the Package Manager window.
    (UUM-155435)

- Package Manager: Importing a .unityPackage inside a .unityPackage coming from the Asset Store will not get a warning for being unsigned since it is considered coming from a trusted source.
    (UUM-154234)

- Package Manager: Package Manager's invalid signature error message could be lost from the Console if "Clear on Recompile" was enabled and installing the package triggered a script recompile \(UUM-139949\).
    ([UUM-139949](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139949))

- Package Manager: Use Asset database proper function to rename non-compliant assets under perforce control
    (UUM-156873)

- Particles: Fix issue where mesh bounds were not updating.
    ([UUM-160229](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160229))

- Particles: Fixed an Editor crash when emitting from a Particle System that was culled off-screen after assigning a curve with a large number of keys.
    ([UUM-153533](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153533))

- Particles: No longer ignores custom data during particle prewarm.
    ([UUM-160600](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160600))

- Performance Test Framework: Fixed error where RunSettings would not be properly reset when Domain Reload didn't happen at entering playmode.
    (UUM-158376)

- Performance Test Framework: Repeat test-player builds no longer assign a fresh GUID to the PerformanceTestRunInfo.json/PerformanceTestRunSettings.json assets on every build. Previously, deleting these assets' `.meta` files as part of post-build cleanup caused Unity to assign a new GUID on the next build, which the data pipeline treated as a change and triggered a full asset rebuild even when nothing else changed. The `.meta` files are now cached in `Library/` between builds and restored before the assets are rewritten, scoped to builds with test assemblies.

- Performance Test Framework: SampleGroups are now getting cleared of samples and statistics a test used, after its result has been reported. This stops data bleeding in other tests in case tests hold on to SampleGroups for some reason.

- Physics: An "Incremental broadphase integrity check failed" exception thrown every step after enabling `PhysicsStep.IncrementalStaticBroadphase` in a world that was first simulated with no rigid bodies, and missing collisions and incremental broadphase integrity check failures after toggling `PhysicsStep.IncrementalStaticBroadphase` or `IncrementalDynamicBroadphase` off and on again at runtime.
    (UUM-155824)

- Physics: ComputePenetration between a scaled Sphere and Convex Mesh Collider will now return the correct result.
    (UUM-139615)

- Physics: Fix Collider3DEditorBase leaking delegates in the editor
    (UUM-158724)

- Physics: Fixed a typo in the Broadphase Type tooltip in Project Settings &gt; Physics.
    ([UUM-161553](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161553))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where enabling Bake Convex Collision Mesh or Bake Triangle Collision Mesh in the Inspector for a Mesh asset was discarded after changing the selection.
    ([UUM-160148](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160148))

- Physics: Fixed an issue where Joint components wouldn't cause newly connected Rigidbody components to re-evaluate their contacts. So flags such as "enable collisions" could take effect.
    ([UUM-156450](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156450))

- Physics: Fixed Rigidbody.mass accepting a NaN value, which made the body pass through all colliders.
    ([UUM-154143](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154143))

- Physics: Fixed the "Solver Velocity Iterations" label being cut off in the Info tab of the Physics Debugger window.
    ([UUM-161716](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161716))

- Physics 2D: "ManifoldPoint.pointA" and "ManifoldPoint.pointB" both now return of the contact point on each shape.
    ([UUM-153874](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153874))

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsShape.CastRay" and "PhysicsChain.CastRay" both now use the maximum fraction of the ray.
    ([UUM-153697](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153697))

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsWorld.DrawQueryResult" now uses "drawNormal" to decide whether to draw normals, instead of "drawPoint".

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsWorld.DrawShapes" now logs a single warning naming "PhysicsWorld.DrawShapes" when given invalid shapes, instead of one warning per shape naming "PhysicsShape.Draw".
    (UUM-153949)

- Physics 2D: "PhysicsWorld.drawTarget", "PhysicsBody.drawTarget", "PhysicsShape.drawTarget" and "PhysicsJoint.drawTarget" now log a warning and keep their current value when assigned a value that is not a valid "PhysicsWorld.DrawTarget".
    (UUM-154002)

- Physics 2D: "SegmentDistanceResult.distance" and "SegmentDistanceResult.sqrDistance" now return the distance between the closest points and that distance squared correctly.
    ([UUM-153871](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153871))

- Physics 2D: Added missing "PhysicsWorld.DrawPoint" overload accepting a span of points to draw.
    (UUM-155896)

- Physics 2D: Added missing public argument validation for various Physics Core 2D methods.
    ([UUM-156367](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156367))

- Physics 2D: Fix issue with the PhysicsCore2D Profiler where world references when attached to a player are wrong.  Now the name of the world is stored in the frame-data so it isn't invalid when displayed on the Editor.
    (UUM-152394)

- Physics 2D: Fixed "CapsuleGeometry.Intersect", several other geometry "Intersect" methods and "PhysicsQuery.ShapeAndShape" returning the contact manifold for the two geometries in reverse order, with the normal pointing from the second geometry to the first.
    ([UUM-157419](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157419))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsAABB.CastRay" returning an incorrect fraction when "maxFraction" is below 1, and "PhysicsAABB.center" and "PhysicsAABB.extents" warnings naming the wrong member.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsBody.BatchImpulse.ApplyAngularImpulse" waking a sleeping body when "wake" is false.
    ([UUM-157591](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157591))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsComposer" dropping filled areas where the collinear edges of a composed polygon overlap.
    ([UUM-158887](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158887))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsDrawOverride.changed" losing its handlers when entering or exiting Play mode, which stopped Editor tooling such as the 2D physics editing tools from following later changes to the Editor draw override.

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsHandle.AsWorld" so that it throws an exception for an invalid world instead of returning a handle reported as a pool handle.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsLayers" returning out-of-date layer names after "PhysicsCoreSettings2D.physicsLayerNames" was replaced.
    (UUM-158710)

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsLayers" so that "GetLayerMask" includes every layer sharing a name, "GetLayerName" returns an empty string for an unnamed layer, and null or out-of-range arguments throw exceptions that name the correct parameter.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsLayers.GetLayerMask" and "PhysicsLayers.GetLayerOrdinal" returning layer 3 for an empty layer name when physics layers are off.
    ([UUM-157609](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157609))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMask" so that a "LayerMask" including layer 31 no longer sets the upper 32 bits, "AreBitsSet" returns false for an empty mask, a default "ResetBitIterator" includes bit 0, and shift counts outside 0 to 63 throw an exception.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix" returning an incorrect translation on the XZ and ZY transform planes.
    ([UUM-157407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157407))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix2D" and "PhysicsMath.GetRelativePose2D" measuring against the scale and the full 3D rotation of the transform measured from, which placed anything using the result, such as the physics shape of a child of a scaled or tilted transform, away from where that child is.
    (UUM-156741)

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsMath.GetRelativeMatrix2D" placing geometry away from where the target Transform is drawn when the source Transform is turned a half turn about an axis in the 2D plane, the target is under a non-uniform scale, or the target is tilted out of the plane.
    ([UUM-157407](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157407))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsRotate" so that converting to a "Quaternion" no longer doubles the angle, "isNormalized" no longer logs a warning for a rotation that is not normalized, "ToString" labels the angle as degrees, and "Normalized" warnings name the correct method.
    ([UUM-157163](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157163))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsRotate.Rotate" rotating by less than the requested angle, by noticeably less for large angles.
    ([UUM-157606](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157606))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsRotate.RotateVector", "PhysicsRotate.InverseRotateVector", "PhysicsTransform.TransformPoint", "PhysicsTransform.InverseTransformPoint", "PhysicsAABB.center" and "PhysicsAABB.extents" returning a different value on each call for an invalid argument instead of zero.
    ([UUM-157608](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157608))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsTransform" validation warnings naming the wrong method.
    ([UUM-156920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156920))

- Physics 2D: Fixed "PhysicsWorld.drawDepth" being overwritten when a body, shape or joint was drawn, and fixed bodies without a transform object always being drawn at a depth of zero.
    (UUM-155044)

- Physics 2D: Fixed "Rigidbody2D.Slide" from reporting an invalid "startPosition" argument incorrectly displaying the "gravity" argument.
    ([UUM-154949](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154949))

- Physics 2D: Fixed 2D physics drawing still showing in Scene and Game views with Gizmos turned off after exiting Play mode, when the 2D physics draw override has "Hide With Gizmos" selected.

- Physics 2D: Fixed a "PolygonGeometry" getting smaller and moving each time its Shape is changed in the Inspector.
    (UUM-160158)

- Physics 2D: Fixed a bouncing shape rebounding one simulation step after the impact instead of in the same step.
    (UUM-154721)

- Physics 2D: Fixed a joint between two kinematic bodies being handled incorrectly when either body was disabled and enabled again.
    (UUM-158185)

- Physics 2D: Fixed a mover colliding with the back of a chain segment.
    (UUM-159731)

- Physics 2D: Fixed a warning about an invalid NaN angle when a Physics Pose is rotated exactly a half turn about an axis in the 2D plane.

- Physics 2D: Fixed an assertion when calling "Physics2D.CircleCast" with a NaN radius; 2D physics queries now log a warning and return no results when given a NaN argument, or an infinite argument other than a distance.
    ([UUM-161335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161335))

- Physics 2D: Fixed an issue where identically named words expanding the PhysicsCore2D profiler both become expanded. Now any previously expanded world is kept.
    ([UUM-153434](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153434))

- Physics 2D: Fixed an issue where the validity check for "PhysicsPlane" was inverted when calling "PhysicsPlane.GetSeparation\(\)". Now a valid/invalid plane is correctly reported.
    ([UUM-153381](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153381))

- Physics 2D: Fixed callback targets set on physics bodies, chains, shapes and joints leaking memory that was never released.
    (UUM-156561)

- Physics 2D: Fixed contact events in 2D Physics Core reporting a group pair's begin as the first and its end as the last when one of its contacts stopped touching as another started touching in the same step.
    (UUM-161120)

- Physics 2D: Fixed contact recycling not working correctly when a body's center of mass is moved.
    (UUM-152756)

- Physics 2D: Fixed contacts being kept after a continuous collision when the shapes were no longer touching.
    (UUM-159731)

- Physics 2D: Fixed editing a "PhysicsRotate" field in an Inspector array or list changing a different element.
    (UUM-158770)

- Physics 2D: Fixed extrapolated bodies rotating backwards between simulation steps on the XZ and ZY transform planes.

- Physics 2D: Fixed fields of the same 2D physics type, such as list elements, sharing one expanded state in the Inspector.

- Physics 2D: Fixed handlers added to the "PhysicsEvents" static events by code that had since been unloaded still being called after a code reload.

- Physics 2D: Fixed incorrect collision at chain segments that have overlapping neighboring vertices.
    (UUM-159731)

- Physics 2D: Fixed physics callbacks being sent to a destroyed MonoBehaviour set as a "callbackTarget" or "transformWriteCallbackTarget", which threw a "MissingReferenceException".
    (UUM-160675)

- Physics 2D: Fixed physics owned by a DontDestroyOnLoad GameObject being destroyed when a scene was loaded with "LoadSceneMode.Single".
    (UUM-160675)

- Physics 2D: Fixed pre-contact callbacks not being called for shapes that were already resting in contact.
    (UUM-159731)

- Physics 2D: Fixed text typed into the "PolygonGeometry" size and offset fields, and into physics layer names, being overwritten while typing.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PhysicsMask", "PhysicsShape.ContactFilter" and "PhysicsQuery.QueryFilter" inspectors setting the upper 32 bits of the mask when layer 31 or Everything is selected with physics layers off.
    ([UUM-157604](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157604))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PhysicsRotate" field, the "PhysicsUserData" object field and the 32-bit layer mask fields in the Inspector missing the mixed value, prefab override styling or context menu when several objects are selected.
    ([UUM-161477](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161477))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PolygonGeometry" property drawer having no way to select a regular polygon by adding a Shape choice with Size and Offset fields.
    (UUM-158885)

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "PolygonGeometry" property drawer throwing an exception and the Inspector stopping drawing the component when the vertex count is outside the range a polygon allows. A warning is now shown instead.
    ([UUM-160184](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160184))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the "ShowAsPhysicsMask" attribute being ignored on a "PhysicsMask", "PhysicsShape.ContactFilter" or "PhysicsQuery.QueryFilter" field nested in a struct or held in an array or List.
    ([UUM-157616](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157616))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the documentation for "CircleGeometry.isValid", "CapsuleGeometry.isValid", "PolygonGeometry.isValid", "SegmentGeometry.isValid", "ChainSegmentGeometry.isValid", "ChainGeometry.isValid", "PolygonGeometry.Validate" and "CapsuleGeometry.Validate" not stating what each one checks or repairs.
    (UUM-153476)

- Physics 2D: Fixed the IMGUI "PhysicsUserData" drawer not showing its "Vector3Int" field, and IMGUI 2D physics drawers marking every field of a struct as overridden when only one field was.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the layer masks of "PhysicsShape.ContactFilter" and "PhysicsQuery.QueryFilter" showing a mixed value when only another field of the filter differed, and Revert on a mask reverting the whole filter.

- Physics 2D: Fixed the owner and user data of PhysicsCore2D bodies, shapes, chains and joints being lost when a world snapshot is applied, or when a world is created or cloned from one.
    ([UUM-159887](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159887))

- Physics 2D: Fixed the Physics Core Settings field overflowing the Physics Core 2D project settings panel.
    ([UUM-154101](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154101))

- Physics 2D: Fixed triggers not reporting overlaps that the "Either" contact filter mode, or a shared positive group index, allows.
    (UUM-159731)

- Physics 2D: Fixed worlds that use a world definition saved before "PhysicsWorldDefinition.bounceIterations" existed never bouncing.
    ([UUM-158974](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158974))

- Player: Add guard to avoid hitting a debug assert in dev player builds
    (UUM-152365)

- Player: GameObjects cast correct shadows after Shader.globalMaximumLOD is changed at runtime.
    ([UUM-150226](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150226))

- Player: Mitigate FPS lag with Window Standalone player with a high-polling mouse
    ([UUM-147395](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147395))

- Prefabs: Fixed a crash when accessing a VFX Renderer after undoing a Visual Effect component removal that was applied to a Prefab.
    (UUM-155270)

- Prefabs: Fixed a performance regression when saving scenes that contain prefab instances with many overrides.
    (UUM-155134)

- Prefabs: Fixed a Prefab instance override recorded under a field's pre-rename name \(FormerlySerializedAs\) not being removed when applied to the Prefab Asset through the renamed property.
    (UUM-153122)

- Prefabs: Fixed an issue where "Assertion failed on expression: 'fidA \!= fidB'" was logged when entering Play Mode in a scene with a Prefab instance that has the same FileID as an object in its source Prefab, or when importing a corrupt Prefab with such a nested Prefab instance. Such Prefabs now fail to import with an error that identifies the affected object.
    ([UUM-128581](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-128581))

- Prefabs: Fixed Apply to Prefab doing nothing for a field inside a SerializeReference object when another SerializeReference field on the same component was also overridden.
    ([UUM-158627](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158627))

- Profiler: Added pointer-like static fields support to CoreCLR memory snapshots.

- Profiler: BC1 texture compression now reports its stage as `CompressBC1`. Previously the whole stage was recorded under the per-job `CompressBC1Job` marker, which inflated that marker's totals.

- Profiler: Capture primitive static field values for Memory Snapshot on CoreCLR

- Profiler: Fix an error when closing the Standalone Profiler.
    ([UUM-155276](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155276))

- Profiler: Fixed a crash when unloading and reloading the engine after tracing GraphicsStateCollection.
    (UUM-152870)

- Profiler: Fixed a flood of "Unable to find type" errors when quitting the Editor or a Player while a memory snapshot was still being captured.
    (UUM-132737)

- Profiler: Fixed a heap-use-after-free crash on shutdown of a profiled internal thread \(such as the audio thread\) in ASan-instrumented builds.
    (UUM-152639)

- Profiler: Fixed a leftover split handle in the CPU Usage module New Timeline view after turning off Jobs Info.
    ([UUM-155721](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155721))

- Profiler: Fixed a possible crash when a thread that the Profiler had already stopped tracking detached from the scripting runtime.
    (UUM-153135)

- Profiler: Fixed an error being logged in the Mac Editor when closing the Standalone Profiler whilst it was still launching.
    ([UUM-141193](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141193))

- Profiler: Fixed an inconsistent seam line between the image and frame number on Profiler Screenshots module thumbnails.
    ([UUM-152561](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152561))

- Profiler: Fixed an InvalidOperationException that could be thrown in the Profiler window when the Physics \(2D\) module was selected and another Editor window was maximized or restored.
    ([UUM-151943](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151943))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where scrolling the CPU Usage Timeline view with the mouse wheel was much slower than in other Editor views.
    ([UUM-137730](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137730))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where selecting a sample in the CPU Usage module's New Timeline view did not select it in the Timeline and Hierarchy views, and vice versa.
    ([UUM-153387](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153387))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where the New Timeline view's Zoom when changing event setting had no effect.

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where the Project Auditor details panel kept showing an issue after a search or filter hid its row.
    (UUM-161132)

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where the rename field of a capture in the Profiler Captures list overlapped the menu button.
    ([UUM-155106](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155106))

- Profiler: Fixed an issue where the stats in the Rendering Profiler module details pane could not be selected or copied.
    ([UUM-158903](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158903))

- Profiler: Fixed AssemblyLoadContext leak reports sometimes missing the allocation callstack of a leaked object, and a possible Editor crash at startup, when using the -record-managed-allocation-callstacks command line argument.
    (UUM-151702)

- Profiler: Fixed CPU and GPU spikes in Play mode while the Profiler window is open.
    ([UUM-153018](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153018))

- Profiler: Fixed crash when capturing Memory Profiler snapshot in the Editor during ALC unload.

- Profiler: Fixed frame-number labels overlapping in the Screenshots module's timeline view when Game View captures used a narrow aspect ratio.
    ([UUM-152532](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152532))

- Profiler: Fixed Highlights logging exception when profiler data is cleared.
    (UUM-143531)

- Profiler: Fixed increased GPU frame time on Intel Macs using Metal when the Profiler is not recording.
    (UUM-154448)

- Profiler: Fixed InvalidOperationException errors when reopening the Profiler window with the Rendering module selected.
    ([UUM-153207](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153207))

- Profiler: Fixed issues with renaming Profiler captures in specific situations on high-DPI desktop setups.
    ([UUM-143329](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143329))

- Profiler: Fixed mixed profiler callstacks for CoreCLR scripting backend on Mac and Linux.
    (UUM-151679)

- Profiler: Fixed ObjectDisposedException errors thrown when maximizing the Profiler window with the CPU Usage New Timeline view selected.
    ([UUM-153382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153382))

- Profiler: Fixed ObjectDisposedException errors thrown when reopening the Profiler window with the CPU Usage New Timeline view selected.
    ([UUM-152520](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152520))

- Profiler: Fixed Profiler details view potentially showing info for frames that are no longer present in the timeline.
    ([UUM-150694](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150694))

- Profiler: Fixed Profiler search results appearing with a delay when a search result is selected and another frame is viewed.
    ([UUM-133765](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133765))

- Profiler: Fixed ProfilerDriver.LoadProfile returning true and logging no error when the capture file was truncated before the end of the Profiler data stream header.
    ([UUM-150778](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150778))

- Profiler: Fixed ProfilerRecorder.ToArray returning fewer samples than the recorder holds after it wrapped around its capacity.

- Profiler: Fixed the CPU Usage module's New Timeline view not being restored when reopening the Profiler window.

    ([UUM-156653](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156653))
