- Profiler: Fixed the Profiler New Timeline view not updating when the selected frame changed after the Profiler window was docked or undocked.
    (UUM-160481)

- Profiler: Fixed the Profiler window's details view not rendering after restoring the default modules.
    ([UUM-117802](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-117802))

- Profiler: Fixed the Project Auditor's Open in Search button not carrying the search field query over into the Search window.
    ([UUM-147241](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147241))

- Profiler: Fixed the Rendering module chart mode resetting to Rendering when the Profiler window was closed and reopened.
    ([UUM-153203](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153203))

- Profiler: Fixed the Troubleshoot Connection Issues button in the Profiler linking to a documentation page unrelated to connection troubleshooting.
    ([UUM-153354](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153354))

- Profiler: GPU Usage module on Vulkan when graphics jobs are enabled is now not dropping the whole frame if only one sample is not readable.

- Profiler: Sum float/double ProfilerRecorder samples correctly.
    ([UUM-161095](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161095))

- Project Browser: Fixed an issue where pasting copied sub-assets into a folder overwrote existing assets with the same name and broke references to them.
    ([UUM-121005](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-121005))

- Project Browser: Fixed an issue where pinging an asset from an object field cleared the search filter of a locked Project window.
    (UUM-147624)

- QNX: Fixed GameWindow.GetPosition not returning the position passed to GameWindow.MoveTo, and a window wider than its display being reported as on the next display.
    (UUM-159872)

- QNX: Fixed OpenGL ES fullscreen windows stretching the image instead of letterboxing it when the resolution aspect ratio differs from the display.<br>
    Fixed Vulkan fullscreen windows below the display resolution not being upscaled to fill the display on Wayland.<br>
    Fixed mouse, touch and IMGUI positions being offset from the image in letterboxed fullscreen windows.
    (UUM-160897)

- QNX: Fixed the player window not resizing when the resolution or fullscreen mode changed, which left the rendered content stretched or misplaced.
    (UUM-150071)

- Scene Manager: Fixed a performance regression in the Editor where reordering many sibling GameObjects increased frame time.
    (UUM-155593)

- Scene Manager: Fixed player builds failing with CS0234 and CS0246 errors when the project contains a script created from the Scene Template Pipeline template.
    ([UUM-160438](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160438))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed a crash when the Scene view draws more gizmos than the maximum gizmo vertex count, for example while visualizing Occlusion Culling in a large scene.
    ([UUM-153365](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153365))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed a NullReferenceException when drag-selecting in the Scene view after a code reload while the Hierarchy window is closed.

- Scene/Game View: Fixed an error being logged when using "Move To View" while a Scene was the active selection in the Hierarchy.
    ([UUM-144589](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144589))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where moving a GameObject far from the origin with the Move tool made its handle disappear and logged an invalid localPosition \(NaN\) error.
    ([UUM-146738](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146738))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed issue where GameObject Tool Context would show up in UI Staging where it's not applicable.
    (UUM-146798)

- Scene/Game View: Fixed the "GameView reduced to a reasonable size" warning being logged on every repaint instead of once when an oversized resolution is selected.
    ([UUM-159288](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159288))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed the camera frustum gizmo in the Scene view keeping its old shape after the camera's target Render Texture was resized.
    ([UUM-140016](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140016))

- Scene/Game View: Fixed the hidden Scene view Cameras overlay searching the Scene for cameras on every Editor update when the Scene contains no camera.
    ([UUM-161353](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161353))

- Scripting: Added performance of three expensive \[OnCodeLoaded\] sites.
    (UUM-154552)

- Scripting: API Updater supports more edge cases around MovedFrom. Now better supports nested children in generic parents, field named the same as a type, extension methods changing namespace while its various argument types are also changing namespaces.
    ([UUM-152410](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152410))

- Scripting: Dispose the replaced UVCS window updaters when it re-initializes.

- Scripting: Ensure absent autoReferenced property from the assembly definition defaults to true when using MSBuild compilation pipeline
    (UUM-151871)

- Scripting: Fix Utils.ForceCrash not terminating Windows Editor on CoreCLR
    (UUM-149690)

- Scripting: Fixed a "MemoryPool::Deallocate can only be called from the main thread" error being logged in development builds when the delayed call manager was destroyed.
    (UUM-150601)

- Scripting: Fixed a "Mismatched state in DefaultScenario" error when exiting Play Mode after editing a script with "Script Changes While Playing" set to "Recompile After Finished Playing"
    ([UUM-148454](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148454))

- Scripting: Fixed a CS1525 compilation error in generated AutoStaticsCleanup code when a static array field or property is initialized with an array initializer without new.
    ([UUM-160017](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160017))

- Scripting: Fixed a CS8669 warning or error in generated AutoStaticsCleanup code when a static event, field, or property uses a nullable reference type annotation.
    ([UUM-160341](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160341))

- Scripting: Fixed a false OperationCanceledException thrown when awaiting Task.Delay\(\) inside a background-thread Awaitable method.
    ([UUM-150034](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150034))

- Scripting: Fixed a false positive assert in `SerializableManagedRef::AssertKlassMeetsRequirements` that caused a crash when entering Play Mode if a MonoBehaviour held a `[SerializeReference]` field whose concrete type carried the `[ExtensionOfNativeClass]` attribute directly rather than inheriting it from a parent class.
    (UUM-148726)

- Scripting: Fixed a TypeLoadException thrown when a precompiled plugin assembly referencing Hash128 was loaded.
    (UUM-153884)

- Scripting: Fixed Alembic curves and points importers keeping pinned arrays alive after the User ALC unloads.

- Scripting: Fixed aliases conflicting when multiple namespaces with the same name use different alias sources, and the AutoStaticsCleanup is used
    ([UUM-158160](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158160))

- Scripting: Fixed an Editor crash on exit when an \[OnCodeUnloading\] method accessed editor state such as SessionState.
    ([UUM-156589](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156589))

- Scripting: Fixed an issue by making sure we do not overflow int32_t when doing arithmetic.
    (UUM-147603)

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where cancelling a Project Auditor analysis or script compilation with MSBuild compilation enabled did not stop the build running in the MSBuild build host.
    (UUM-156939)

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where cancelling an Awaitable.NextFrameAsync or Awaitable.EndOfFrameAsync awaitable during a frame update could make an unrelated Awaitable complete early with a default result.
    ([UUM-156357](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156357))

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where MonoBehaviours loaded from AssetBundles showed as missing scripts in Players when their assembly was loaded at runtime.
    ([UUM-159333](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159333))

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where MSBuild errors that stopped a script build before compiling were reported with an internal stack trace and no error code when using -enable-msbuild.
    (UUM-157442)

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where opening the Standalone Profiler could trigger a script compilation in the Profiler process, which logged "Could not find a part of the path" errors for package assemblies.
    (UUM-133483)

- Scripting: Fixed an issue where slnx solution files would not generate with msbuild enabled if there is compilation errors in the project
    (UUM-151502)

- Scripting: Fixed AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory and AppContext.BaseDirectory returning an empty string on CoreCLR.

- Scripting: Fixed automatic statics cleanup silently stopping for an entire assembly when a static field of a disposable type had no initializer.
    ([UUM-150516](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150516))

- Scripting: Fixed automatic statics cleanup silently stopping for an entire assembly when a static readonly field had a type that cannot be reset, such as Predicate&lt;T&gt;.
    ([UUM-153257](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153257))

- Scripting: Fixed CompilationPipeline.ParseResponseFile not recognizing -unsafe- in a csc.rsp file, which caused ResponseFileData.Unsafe to ignore an explicit request to disable unsafe code.
    (UUM-154574)

- Scripting: Fixed CS0261 compilation error in generated code when Lifecycle or AutoStaticsCleanup attributes are used in a partial interface, record, or record struct.
    ([UUM-158128](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158128))

- Scripting: Fixed exceptions not being logged when thrown from async Awaitable message methods such as Awake, Start, and OnEnable.
    ([UUM-155273](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155273))

- Scripting: Fixed GC allocation on every GameObject.SendMessage and BroadcastMessage call on CoreCLR.
    (UUM-145473)

- Scripting: Fixed missing Scripting API documentation for Unity.Scripting and Unity.Scripting.LifecycleManagement attributes.

- Scripting: Fixed MonoBehaviour.StartCoroutine reporting that coroutines can only be stopped on a MonoBehaviour instead of started.
    ([UUM-160386](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160386))

- Scripting: Fixed MSBU csproj generation aborting with `DirectoryNotFoundException` when a package's deeply-nested subfolder was still being materialised on disk. The abort left the project graph incomplete, causing `CS0246`/`CS0234` errors in consumers of the never-generated `.gen.csproj` files.
    (UUM-151407)

- Scripting: Fixed MSBuild script compilation failing with MSB4216 when the task host runs ILPostProcessingTask.
    (UUM-151424)

- Scripting: Fixed NullReferenceException in ArrayUtility.ArrayEquals when the first array contained a null element.
    ([UUM-161323](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161323))

- Scripting: Fixed Object.Instantiate with InstantiateParameters accepting a persistent parent; it now logs an error and creates the object without a parent, like the other Instantiate overloads.
    (UUM-150465)

- Scripting: Fixed ObjectPool.CountActive going negative after Clear; CountAll after Clear now includes objects still in use instead of resetting to 0.
    ([UUM-157348](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157348))

- Scripting: Fixed Panel.PublishCursorPosition leak.

- Scripting: Fixed Plane.Equals returning true for planes with different hash codes, which caused HashSet and Dictionary lookups to fail.
    ([UUM-157345](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157345))

- Scripting: Fixed potential editor freezing for long periods when running ScriptUpdater.
    (UUM-154695)

- Scripting: Fixed Progress cancel and pause callbacks registered from C\# keeping the previous AssemblyLoadContext alive after a code reload on CoreCLR.

- Scripting: Fixed Project Auditor reporting no Code issues or compiler warnings when the MSBuild compilation pipeline had all projects cached.
    (UUM-156950)

- Scripting: Fixed script compilation failing with an IOException \("The process cannot access the file ... because it is being used by another process"\) when the MSBuild compilation pipeline is enabled and a compiled assembly in Library/ScriptAssemblies is still held open by another process.
    (UUM-151602)

- Scripting: Fixed serialization issues caused by file based namespaces not being taking into account.
    ([UUM-142256](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142256))

- Scripting: Fixed Terrain leaking on scene GUI content.

- Scripting: Fixed the MSBuild compilation pipeline applying a project's .Build.props and .Build.targets files to every project, instead of only to the projects that reference it.
    (UUM-157019)

- Scripting: Fixed the Smart Strings TimeFormatter writing "1 hore" instead of "1 hora" for Spanish time spans of one hour.
    ([UUM-158914](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158914))

- Scripting: Fixed the statics cleanup analyzer suggesting a fix that failed to compile with CS0260.
    ([UUM-150417](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150417))

- Scripting: Fixed unnecessary MSB3052, MSB4011 and MSB3245 warnings when compiling scripts with the MSBuild compilation pipeline.
    (UUM-157019)

- Scripting: Improved performance of statics analyzer.
    (UUM-155033)

- Scripting: Missing ClickDetector.Cleanup in Panel.Dispose.

- Scripting: Optimize GetSelectionCount object validation in SceneInspector.
    (UUM-155038)

- Scripting: When using msbuild, fixed asmdefs restricting includePlatforms/excludePlatforms to WindowsStandalone64, WindowsStandalone32, macOSStandalone, LinuxStandalone64, or WSA being excluded from player builds, causing CS0234/CS0246 in consumers.
    (UUM-151758)

- Search: ArgumentException thrown when typing `!` as the first character in a Search window query \(UUM-141246\).
    ([UUM-141246](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141246))

- Search: Fix funky apostrophe query when using "Find References in Scene"
    ([UUM-150994](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150994))

- Search: Fixed an "Unknown filter" error when using **Search Same Property Value** on a property stored in a `[SerializeReference]` field, such as Build Profile platform settings.

- Search: Fixed an Editor freeze that could occur when a search indexing task was canceled while the index was being updated.

- Search: Fixed an ObjectDisposedException when a search expression ended a query that was still running.
    (UUM-144924)

- Search: Fixed bug where the Add Scene option was present in the Search window's inspector for scenes located in read-only packages
    ([UUM-139396](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139396))

- Search: Fixed issue where running a saved SearchQuery would throw index out of range exception.
    (UUM-136122)

- Search: Fixed issue where SearchDatabase leaked on domain reload.
    ([UUM-161715](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161715))

- Search: Fixed issues where Asset Store search result items would have empty text when scrolling in List mode.
    ([UUM-140235](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140235))

- Search: Fixed Search event handlers being silently dropped when several handlers belonged to the same object.
    (UUM-151496)

- Search: Fixed search expressions such as count, empty and tonumber returning wrong results or errors while results were still loading.
    (UUM-150890)

- Search: Fixed Search's ObjectField multi-value display.
    ([UUM-147741](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-147741))

- Search: Fixed the query block selector sometimes showing an empty list when its propositions were slow to load.
    (UUM-151497)

- Search: Fixed the Search window Inspector panel not updating when components of the selected GameObject are reordered, added or removed.
    ([UUM-140368](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140368))

- Security: Fixed a potential crash in text shaping caused by a null-pointer dereference in HarfBuzz when memory allocation fails.
    (UUM-153188)

- Serialization: Fixed a bug where switching Asset Serialization Mode to Force Binary could corrupt ScriptableObjects containing certain fields, destroying their serialized data.
    ([UUM-151628](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151628))

- Serialization: Fixed a crash on project load when a \[SerializeReference\] field referenced a type that had been changed from a class to a struct.
    ([UUM-150957](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150957))

- Serialization: Fixed a crash when a scene GameObject was parented under a prefab loaded in a Player or from an AssetBundle, and Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets unloaded that prefab.
    (UUM-150465)

- Serialization: Fixed a StackOverflowException when applying Inspector changes to an object that holds a \[SerializeReference\] chain thousands of nodes deep.
    (UUM-159032)

- Serialization: Fixed an Editor crash when increasing the size of an array field in the Inspector.
    ([UUM-154303](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154303))

- Serialization: Fixed an Editor crash with the error "The file 'MemoryStream' is corrupted\!" when entering a value in an EntityId array field in the Inspector; such fields are now displayed as lists.
    ([UUM-159503](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159503))

- Serialization: Fixed an Editor using the CoreCLR scripting backend writing editor-only fields into player builds
    (UUM-151463)

- Serialization: Fixed an error and corrupted managed references when removing a remapped asset from an importer that uses \[SerializeReference\] fields.
    (UUM-152385)

- Serialization: Fixed an issue where a \[SerializeReference\] field holding an instance of a type that is not available in the Player, such as an Editor-only type, caused the whole script's serialized data to be lost on load; only that reference now loads as null.

- Serialization: Fixed an issue where changing a serialized Object reference field to a list of that type created a list of "None" elements instead of an empty list.
    ([UUM-158335](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158335))

- Serialization: Fixed an issue where Players read wrong values or crashed when loading a \[Serializable\] class or struct that is nested inside a non-serializable class and has fields inside \#if UNITY_EDITOR.
    ([UUM-158652](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158652))

- Serialization: Fixed an unused null entry being added to an object's managed reference registry when you grow an array of a struct that contains a \[SerializeReference\] field.

- Serialization: Fixed Analyzer UAC1016 error flagging a Dictionary with generic types as cannot be serialized incorrectly
    (UUM-155167)

- Serialization: Fixed ManagedReferenceMissingType records that compare equal with the typed Equals method being treated as different by boxed Equals, HashSet, and Dictionary.
    ([UUM-157429](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157429))

- Serialization: Fixed negative zero not being stored when you write it over a positive zero value through SerializedProperty.

- Serialization: Fixed removing an item from a list applying to only one of the selected objects when editing multiple objects in the Inspector.
    ([UUM-153799](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153799))

- Serialization: Fixed slow scene loading when a scene references large prefab assets.
    ([UUM-160168](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160168))

- Serialization: Fixed the "+" button in the Inspector not adding more than one element to bool arrays and lists.
    ([UUM-159259](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159259))

- Serialization: Fixed the serialization rules analyzer reporting a false UAC1001 warning for a non-serializable type used only as a generic type argument of a field that does not serialize it, such as a UnityEngine.Object reference.
    ([UUM-159263](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159263))

- Serialization: Fixed the serialization version warning telling users to "open and re-save" a file to upgrade it, when no such action exists; it now points to AssetDatabase.ForceReserializeAssets, the API that actually performs the upgrade.
    ([UUM-151536](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151536))

- Serialization: SerializeReference fields being given the wrong reference, or none at all, when a serialized array or list of a class type contained a null element.
    (UUM-152214)

- Services: Fixed long cloud project names not being truncated in the Cloud project dropdown in Services settings.
    ([UUM-150192](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150192))

- Services: Fixed the Organization and Cloud project dropdowns in Project Settings &gt; Services opening away from their fields on Windows when monitors use different display scales.
    ([UUM-159338](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159338))

- Shadergraph: Fixed "Missing vertex input" errors logged on Metal when a UI Shader Graph that reads UV1, UV2 or UV3 is open in the Shader Graph window.
    (UUM-158525)

- Shadergraph: Fixed a bug in which the code generated by a SubGraph node used unnecessary implicit precision casts.
    (UUM-152454)

- Shadergraph: Fixed a MissingReferenceException in the Shader Graph importer Inspector after applying the Expose as Shader setting.
    ([UUM-155751](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155751))

- Shadergraph: Fixed a NullReferenceException that prevented a Shader Graph from importing when a Sub Graph it uses was deleted.
    ([UUM-156938](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156938))

- Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where an empty reflected function body could be mishandled.
    ([UUM-161374](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161374))

- Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where materials lost their property values when using Shader Graphs last saved before Shader Graph 5.1 that have properties without a reference name. Values set on such materials while using 7000.0.0a11 or earlier 7000.0 alphas might need to be set again.
    (UUM-153642)

- Shadergraph: Fixed the "implicit truncation of vector type" compiler warning in the Shader Graph Custom Lighting samples.
    ([UUM-155156](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155156))

- Shadergraph: Fixed the Gradient Editor window reopening by itself after undoing an edit to a gradient on a Shader Graph node input port.
    ([UUM-152938](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152938))

- Shaders: **XR/Vulkan/DXC:** Fixed multiview shaders failing to compile or bind correctly when using the DXC shader compiler on Vulkan. The view index is now read from the SPIR-V ViewIndex builtin, so existing shaders work without changes.

- Shaders: Ensuring cached shader data is not dependent on the asset location.
    (UUM-151932)

- Shaders: Fixed "Local keyword space index cache out of sync." errors and possible crashes when the Editor rendered asset previews after a shader reload.
    ([UUM-143941](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143941))

- Shaders: Fixed a crash due to an out-of-range value being supplied to Shader global property APIs.

- Shaders: Fixed a crash when calling RenderingCommandBuffer.SetKeyword with null compute or ray tracing shader.
    ([UUM-158327](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158327))

- Shaders: Fixed a crash when executing a command buffer whose compute or ray tracing shader was destroyed after commands such as `CommandBuffer.SetKeyword` or `CommandBuffer.DispatchCompute` were recorded.
    ([UUM-161535](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161535))

- Shaders: Fixed a crash when executing a RenderingCommandBuffer with a SetKeyword on a null or destroyed material.
    ([UUM-157105](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157105))

- Shaders: Fixed a minor performance regression in the asset database refresh due to recently added shader dependency handling.
    (UUM-154631)

- Shaders: Fixed a player freeze when switching scenes repeatedly with a preloaded ShaderVariantCollection.
    ([UUM-156125](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156125))

- Shaders: Fixed a possible Editor crash when several graphics jobs requested the same warmed-up shader variant at the same time.

- Shaders: Fixed a possible Editor crash when shader messages were read while a shader variant compiled in the background.
    (UUM-154510)

- Shaders: Fixed a problem where the "Preload Shaders After Showing First Scene" option in Graphics settings and Build Profiles was unticked automatically after it was ticked, and "Preload Time Limit Per Frame \(ms\)" then displayed -1.
    ([UUM-151773](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151773))

- Shaders: Fixed a rare crash in the Editor when shader variants were compiled concurrently with native graphics jobs enabled.
    (UUM-153485)

- Shaders: Fixed a rare crash when doing a build.
    ([UUM-148715](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148715))

- Shaders: Fixed an assertion failure when calling Shader.SetGlobalVectorArray with an invalid property ID that was previously used with a different property type.
    ([UUM-161397](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161397))

- Shaders: Fixed an Editor crash on Metal when a shader pass raised its target for a fragment-only keyword \(for example \#pragma target 5.0 with a keyword\) and the GPU Resident Drawer was enabled.
    (UUM-156494)

- Shaders: Fixed an editor crash when a ShaderLab `ColorMask`, `Blend`, or `BlendOp` command used a render target index outside the range 0 to 7 \(inclusive\). These commands now report a shader error instead.
    ([UUM-155318](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155318))

- Shaders: Fixed an Editor crash when importing a shader file that ends with a `#line` directive.
    (UUM-159786)

- Shaders: Fixed an Editor crash when importing a shader file with CR-only line endings that contains a block comment.
    (UUM-159807)

- Shaders: Fixed crash in Editor when an uncompiled or broken shader is being rendered on a Canvas.
    ([UUM-153265](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153265))

- Shaders: Fixed default-created LocalKeyword and LocalKeywordSpace field access crashing the Editor.
    ([UUM-156424](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156424))

- Shaders: Fixed frame time spikes during scene load caused by shadow caster shader variants being compiled before they were drawn.
    ([UUM-139592](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139592))

- Shaders: Fixed icon used when Compute and Ray Tracing shaders are created.
    ([UUM-159117](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159117))

- Shaders: Fixed material variants that kept an old property value after the base material changed, when an intermediate variant locked that property.
    ([UUM-158581](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158581))

- Shaders: Fixed Material.GetPropertyNames crashing when the provided value is outside of the valid enumeration range.
    ([UUM-157945](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157945))

- Shaders: Fixed shaders in the Editor rendering with the magenta error shader for the whole session when the startup shader warm-up missed the shader cache, which happened with the experimental shader variant data after an Editor update or a colour space change.

- Shaders: Fixed the GRD-GPUOCC kernel shader warnings.
    ([UUM-156588](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156588))

- Shaders: Fixed the missing transpose in the float2x4 DOTS instancing loader.
    (UUM-147444)

- Shaders: Fixed the read overlap in the float2x4 DOTS instancing loader.
    (UUM-146222)

- Shaders: Material.mainTexture will return null if the main texture is not present instead of logging an error in the console.
    ([UUM-103940](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-103940))

- Shaders: Shader preprocessor now handles short-circuiting evaluation.
    (UUM-135082)

- Shaders: Shader_feature are not overridden to dynamic_branch anymore in fast build mode to avoid hitting resource limits with the shader. Added also possibility to disable fast build mode per shader in the shader inspector.
    ([UUM-157180](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157180))

- Source Code Build: Fixed Editor source builds failing on machines that use the Turkish region format.
    (UUM-154501)

- SpeedTree: Fixed SpeedTree 7 and 8 assets not reimporting when the active render pipeline changed.

- SpeedTree: Fixed SpeedTree 9 assets importing with empty material texture slots when their textures were imported after them.

- SRP Core: Added a dedicated alpha upscale pass to preserve the alpha channel of the camera output.
    ([UUM-135242](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-135242))

- SRP Core: Added a Hide/Show Overlays button to the Rendering Debugger, which temporarily hides all active debug overlays without losing the current selection.
    ([UUM-150066](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150066))

- SRP Core: Fixed a recurring GC allocation during Camera.Render caused by the GPU Resident Drawer.
    (UUM-151785)

- SRP Core: Fixed an Editor crash caused by a stack overflow when displaying Volume components that expose events, such as the URP Screen Space Reflection and Surface Cache GI overrides.
    (UUM-153887)

- SRP Core: Fixed an issue where the Convert Assets button in the Render Pipeline Converter window stayed disabled when you selected an unconverted item after a partial conversion.
    ([UUM-155720](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155720))

- SRP Core: Fixed Blitter blits that use a custom material sampling at a mip level left over from an earlier blit, which showed an outdated image on some Android devices using OpenGL ES.
    (UUM-154958)

- SRP Core: Fixed DynamicArray.Insert throwing an ArgumentException and corrupting the array when the array was full or had only one free slot.
    ([UUM-159071](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159071))

- SRP Core: Fixed exceptions thrown in the Inspector when adding a Light Anchor component while no Game View is open.
    ([UUM-153999](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153999))

- SRP Core: Fixed help boxes that have a Fix button in Render Pipeline Core, URP and HDRP inspectors using a larger icon and different text alignment than regular help boxes.

- SRP Core: Fixed pass names in the Render Graph Viewer rendering as low-quality Bitmap text when the Editor Default Text Rendering Mode preference was set to Bitmap; they now always use SDF rendering.
    ([UUM-151562](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151562))

- SRP Core: Fixed Render Graph execution errors when a raster render pass binds 8 colour attachments and a depth attachment.
    ([UUM-149931](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149931))

- SRP Core: Fixed render passes not being merged on older iOS devices \(Apple GPU family 3 and below\) once a native pass was considered to be over the on-tile pixel storage limit, which serialized the whole frame into one native pass per render pass.
    ([UUM-148865](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148865))

- SRP Core: Fixed rendering corruption when using async compute with Render Graph, where a texture could be used from both the graphics and compute queues simultaneously.
    ([UUM-141896](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141896))

- SRP Core: Fixed rendering layer mask fields in the Rendering Debugger not listing rendering layers that were defined after the debugger was initialized.

- SRP Core: Fixed the Render Graph closing a native render pass after 8 raster render passes even when they used fewer than 8 native subpasses, which made the next pass, such as the URP UI overlay, load its attachments again from memory.
    ([UUM-158401](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158401))

- SRP Core: Fixed the Rendering Debugger Volume panel showing no volume data when the camera's Volume Trigger is a different GameObject than the camera.
    ([UUM-160872](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160872))

- SRP Core: Fixed the Scan and Convert Assets buttons in the Render Pipeline Converter window being hidden when the window was narrower than its content.
    ([UUM-151491](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151491))

- SRP Core: Fixed use of incorrect API define for DX12 in Common.hlsl. Incorrect SHADER_API_D3D12 to correct SHADER_API_DX12.
    (UUM-159608)

- SRP Core: Fixed Volume override Reset being overwritten by an in-progress, uncommitted field edit instead of resetting to the default value.
    ([UUM-152522](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152522))

- SRP Core: Fixed `TextureCurve.Evaluate` and `TextureCurve.GetTexture` throwing a `NullReferenceException` for a looping curve that was loaded, cloned or deserialized.
    ([UUM-161213](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161213))

- SRP Core: Renamed the Volume override "Toggle All" and "Toggle None" actions to "Enable All" and "Disable All", and updated the ALL/NONE tooltips to match.
    ([UUM-152524](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152524))

- SRP Core: Render Pipeline Converter - Disable foldout items if all child elements are disabled.
    ([UUM-150182](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150182))

- SRP Core: Render Pipeline Converter - Keep state on assembly reloads.
    ([UUM-149926](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149926))

- SRP Core: Render Pipeline Converter - Scan button is always enabled as user can re-scan the project.
    ([UUM-151043](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151043))

- SRP Core: Untangled texture creation status from update count in texture2DAtlas
    ([UUM-139389](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139389))

- Terrain: Fixed a crash in shadow culling when a render pass culled the same Camera a second time while a Terrain with `Draw Instanced` disabled was visible.
    ([UUM-151731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151731))

- Terrain: Fixed a crash when a script updates tree instances on a terrain whose tree prototypes cannot be instanced.
    ([UUM-150692](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150692))

- Terrain: Fixed an "Overflow in memory allocator" fatal error when rendering a terrain with details from a camera extremely far away from it, such as a Scene view restored at a very distant position.
    ([UUM-155070](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155070))

- Terrain: Fixed Create Neighbor Terrains copying only the first terrain layer onto the new neighbor tile.
    ([UUM-151649](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151649))

- Terrain: Fixed grass billboards and SpeedTree7 billboards not receiving real-time lights with the Deferred or Deferred+ rendering path when Use Rendering Layers is enabled.
    ([UUM-156479](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156479))

- Terrain: Fixed terrain painting being lost without any message when a new TerrainData was saved with `AssetDatabase.CreateAsset` after its terrain layers were assigned. A warning now explains the loss and how to avoid it.
    (UUM-155877)

- Terrain: Fixed the Edit Tree window keeping the "Tree has already been selected as a prototype" error and a disabled Apply button after the conflicting tree was removed.
    ([UUM-158698](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158698))

- Terrain: Fixed the Edit Tree, Edit Detail Mesh and Edit Grass Texture windows applying changes to a different prototype when another prototype was removed while the window was open.
    ([UUM-155483](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155483))

- Terrain: Fixed the scene being marked as modified when the Edit Tree window was opened and closed without making changes.
    ([UUM-158703](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158703))

- Terrain: Fixed the texture import warnings in the Terrain Layer Inspector being drawn over by the Color Tint, Opacity as Density and Normal Scale controls when using the Universal Render Pipeline or the High Definition Render Pipeline.
    ([UUM-133166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133166))

- Terrain: Fixed trees placed above or below the Terrain surface moving onto it when `SetHeights`, `SyncHeightmap`, a height brush, painting holes or changing the Terrain size modified another part of the Terrain.
    ([UUM-137127](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-137127))

- Test Framework: Fixed a test run being executed a second time in the same Editor session when a domain reload happened immediately after the run finished, which can occur when a test changes the active build target.

- Test Framework: Fixed Is.Not.AllocatingGCMemory\(\) intermittently reporting GC allocations made by other threads, such as the finalizer thread after GC.Collect\(\), for a delegate that does not allocate.
    (UUM-142851)

- Test Framework: Fixed play mode tests on a device stalling part way through a run when the screen went to sleep or the idle screen started, which deactivated the player and paused it.
    (UUM-134050)

- Test Framework: Fixed reading a test's child index adding an empty childIndex property to tests that have none.

- Test Framework: Fixed tests inherited from a base class in another assembly being reported with the source file and line of an unrelated test.

- Tests: Enabled CanCallNativeStaticLibrary test for tvos.
    (UUM-153120)

- Tests: Fix QualitySettings test instability.
    (UUM-151127)

- Tests: Fixed an instability in the terrain test on Macs.
    (UUM-153508)

- Tests: Upgraded serialization of uGUI test texture metas.

- Text: Added support for the &lt;pos&gt; rich text tag in the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-154252)

- Text: Fixed a FreeType vulnerability \(CVE-2026-23865\) that could cause an out-of-bounds read when loading a malformed variable font.
    ([UUM-140975](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140975))

- Text: Fixed a missing word in the error message logged when importing a font file whose format is unsupported.
    ([UUM-161481](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161481))

- Text: Fixed Advanced Text Generator rendering of hinted SDF font assets \(SDFAA_HINTED\) so glyph shapes and spacing match the standard TextMesh Pro generator.
    (UUM-154232)

- Text: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory read when a TextMesh or legacy UI Text string ended with an unterminated quad tag parameter.
    (UUM-153129)

- Text: Fixed an out-of-bounds memory read when a TextMesh or legacy UI Text string ended with an unterminated rich text tag.
    (UUM-153128)

- Text: Fixed ATG line spacing so lines separated by a line break are spaced like wrapped lines, and the first and last lines are no longer offset by the font's line gap.
    (UUM-154035)

- Text: Fixed Chinese characters not rendering in legacy text on iOS 18.4 and later.
    ([UUM-121660](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-121660))

- Text: Fixed slightly blurry glyphs for font assets using the COLOR_HINTED render mode with the Advanced Text Generator.

- Text: Fixed the Font Asset Migration window not listing font assets imported while the window was open.
    ([UUM-152589](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152589))

- TextCore: Fixed a trailing line break eliding the previous line with Ellipsis overflow.
    (UUM-160446)

- TextCore: Fixed an issue where the **Characters** field in the **Font Subsetting** section of the TextCore Font Asset Inspector and TextMeshPro Font Asset Inspector did not update when a subset was applied from the **Properties** window.
    ([UUM-156933](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156933))

- TextCore: Fixed dynamic font assets polluting the version control system.
    ([UUM-151582](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151582))

- TextCore: Fixed tabs rendering the font's glyph for U+0009, and TextMeshPro reporting whitespace as visible when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextCore: Fixed text without a line break opportunity wrapping at punctuation instead of at the character that overflows.
    (UUM-160445)

- TextCore: Fixed the &lt;space&gt; rich text tag being ignored and stripped from the text when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160487)

- TextCore: Fixed the Font Asset Creator and Sprite Importer windows failing to reload and logging a serialization error when toggling UI Toolkit Live Reload.
    ([UUM-150353](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150353))

- TextCore: Fixed the font atlas of a dynamic Font Asset being included in a player build when the UI Builder was open during the build.
    (UUM-149174)

- TextMeshPro: Added OS font fallbacks support to TextMeshPro. Characters that none of the assigned font assets contain are now rendered using fonts installed on the operating system.
    (UUM-154364)

- TextMeshPro: Bug "TextMeshPro input field doesn't remove characters from first backspace when variant characters are used with the input field
    ([UUM-140383](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140383))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed an IndexOutOfRangeException thrown continuously by the font asset Inspector when an older font asset stores an invalid Atlas Render Mode value.
    ([UUM-150239](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150239))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed an issue where an unclosed rich text tag at the end of the text, such as &lt;br, was parsed as a complete tag.
    ([UUM-158156](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158156))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed centered and right aligned synthetic bold text being shifted by the extra advance of the last character of each line when using Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160440)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed centered text with Ellipsis overflow being shifted when a paragraph ends with a line break or a space hidden at a wrap.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed font asset face info and style edits not applying to text when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed GetPreferredValues measuring a single line when given an infinite height when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160444)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed Max Visible Characters, Max Visible Words and Max Visible Lines being ignored, and TMP_TextInfo.wordInfo not being filled, when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160442)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed non-deterministic Addressables bundle hashes caused by a debug symbols pragma in the TMP mobile SDF shaders.
    ([UUM-153225](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153225))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed right-aligned monospaced text jittering in a Horizontal Layout Group when the last character changes.
    ([UUM-98940](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-98940))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed synthetic bold text being clipped when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160439)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed tab stops ignoring the font asset Tab Multiple when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed TextMeshPro static events, such as OnMissingCharacter, losing handlers registered in InitializeOnLoad or SubsystemRegistration when entering Play Mode.
    ([UUM-158087](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158087))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the Baseline, Midline and Capline vertical alignments being laid out as Middle when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160443)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the caret not moving past an emoji with the arrow keys, and Backspace and Delete removing only half of an emoji, in a TextMeshPro Input Field with Content Type set to Password.
    ([UUM-155145](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155145))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the Editor freezing when a TextMeshPro text with a Content Size Fitter contained a hyphen preceded by a space at narrow widths.
    ([UUM-152921](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152921))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the empty line after a final line break counting in the text height when using the Advanced Text Generator.
    (UUM-160447)

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the font asset Normal Style spacing offset being ignored when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the Font Style \(All Caps, Lowercase, Small Caps, Superscript, Subscript\) being ignored for text without rich text tags when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the line height and glyph position of font assets with a negative ascent line, a positive descent line or a baseline offset when using the Advanced Text Generator.

- TextMeshPro: Fixed the Sprite Asset Inspector glyph preview not updating while editing the Glyph Rect values of a sprite character.
    ([UUM-143484](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143484))

- TextMeshPro: Fixed toggling Is Scale Static on a TextMeshPro text object not marking the scene as modified and not being undoable.
    ([UUM-157119](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157119))

- TextMeshPro: Moves essential TextMesh Pro resources from the `TMP Essential Resources unity package` to `Packages/com.unity.ugui/Package Resources/`
    (UUM-153305)

- Timeline: Fixed a SerializedObjectNotCreatableException error logged during domain reload while a Signal Receiver inspector exists.

- Timeline: Fixed an issue where property changes were not correctly re-evaluated in the Timeline after being added.
    ([UUM-150294](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150294))

- Timeline: Fixed an issue where scrubbing the timeline while in recording mode could trigger additional graph rebuilds
    ([UUM-150705](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150705))

- Timeline: Fixed clips with identical start time could have their order swapped when new clips are added or existing clips start times are modified.
    ([UUM-145582](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-145582))

- Timeline: Fixed timeline preview ownership
    (UUM-137833)

- Timeline: Fixed Timeline window and its assets staying loaded after a scripting domain reload.

- uGUI: Allows LayoutElement to restore previously used float value when disabling and re-enabling a property.
    ([UUM-144461](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144461))

- uGUI: Bug IndexOutOfRangeException is thrown when exiting Prefab mode and then Play mode.
    ([UUM-150797](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150797))

- uGUI: Fixed a crash or corrupted Transform when undoing the addition of a Layout Group component to a GameObject that did not have a RectTransform.
    ([UUM-157935](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157935))

- uGUI: Fixed an issue where the "Too many sprite tiles on Image" error was logged repeatedly while editing a Tiled Image.
    ([UUM-144189](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-144189))

- uGUI: Fixed an issue where UI elements, such as Sliced and Tiled Images and Text, were not updated when the Canvas Reference Pixels Per Unit, Scale Factor, Pixel Perfect or Render Camera changed after they were first drawn.
    ([UUM-151004](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151004))

- uGUI: Fixed an issue where VertexHelper.FillMesh produced an empty mesh when the VertexHelper had vertices but no triangles.
    ([UUM-157436](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157436))

- uGUI: Fixed Blocking Objects raycasts in GraphicRaycaster being silently skipped in IL2CPP builds due to code stripping.
    (UUM-145465)

- uGUI: Fixed CanvasRenderer.absoluteDepth and Graphic.depth returning -1, and raycasts missing the Graphic, on the frame it is reparented to a different Canvas.
    ([UUM-159099](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159099))

- uGUI: Fixed crash when calling SetSecondaryTexture/Size with invalid index.
    ([UUM-154589](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154589))

- uGUI: Fixed GetModifiedMaterial to work with stencil
    ([UUM-159131](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159131), [UUM-159133](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159133))

- uGUI: Fixed Graphic.CrossFadeAlpha and Graphic.CrossFadeColor not applying the target value immediately when called with a duration of 0, which caused the value to be lost if the GameObject was deactivated before the next canvas update.
    ([UUM-158296](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158296))

- uGUI: Fixed GridLayoutGroup.generatedRowCount and generatedColumnCount reporting only the rows or columns that fit in the rect, instead of those the children are placed in, when Constraint is Flexible and the children overflow the rect.
    ([UUM-158920](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158920))

- uGUI: Fixed RectMask2D softening the edges of clipped content when its softness is zero
    ([UUM-150815](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150815))

- uGUI: Fixed the BaseInput component's help button opening a documentation page that doesn't exist.
    ([UUM-143300](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-143300))

- uGUI: Fixed the IME candidate window appearing at the previously focused input field when focus moved between fields.

- uGUI: Fixed tilted/scaled RectMask2D giving incorrect or invalid masking values.
    (UUM-160972, UUM-160973)

- uGUI: Fixed VertexHelper.PopulateUIVertex and SetUIVertex not throwing ArgumentOutOfRangeException for out-of-range indices
    ([UUM-158159](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158159))

- uGUI: Fixed Vertical and Horizontal Layout Groups reporting a preferred size smaller than their largest child when Control Child Size is off.
    ([UUM-159240](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159240))

- uGUI: More robust InputField validation for invalid lengths
    ([UUM-158088](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158088), [UUM-158093](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158093))

- uGUI: sendHoverToParent=false is now defined as "events are sent only to the RaycastTarget directly under the pointer"<br>
    - when false, interacting with a child Label \(raycast target with no listeners\) no longer sends events to its parent Button<br>
    - when false, hover events only fire on the object directly under the pointer
    ([UUM-140563](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-140563))

- uGUI: Use AssetPostprocessor.OnProcessScene instead of IProcessSceneWithReport

- UI: Help links now go to corresponding pipeline help page

- UI Toolkit: Added missing `Equals(object)` support to `AuthoringIdPath`.
    ([UUM-161554](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161554))

- UI Toolkit: Added the Dilate, Sharpness, Outline Color, Outline Dilate, Shadow Color, Shadow Offset and Shadow Softness inputs to the Default SDF Text Shader Graph node, and an SDF Text Parameters node that outputs the element's values.
    (UUM-146538)

- UI Toolkit: Adding a Standard Element from the GameObject menu now adds it to the UXML Template GameObject set as Default Parent instead of creating a new one.
    ([UUM-151620](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151620))

- UI Toolkit: Changed the Grid section's Flow, Justify Items and Justify Self options to be icons instead of text.
    ([UUM-154166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154166))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Accessing invalid property" errors in the Console when discarding UI asset changes while one of the asset's elements was selected.
    ([UUM-153242](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153242))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Create Template..." in the UI Viewport not carrying the element's name over to the new template instance, which broke USS selectors and UQuery lookups targeting that name.
    ([UUM-151671](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151671))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Create Template..." in the UI Viewport silently overwriting a UXML file that the selected element instantiates, which destroyed the file's contents through a circular reference.
    ([UUM-151655](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151655))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "display: grid" elements ignoring their own min-width, min-height, max-width, and max-height when their size is content-based.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "display: grid" elements placing centered or end-aligned tracks outside the container when a maximum size makes it smaller than the available space.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "fr" and stretched "auto" grid tracks not expanding into the space granted by the grid container's min-width or min-height when the container is otherwise sized by its content.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "fr" grid tracks in a content-sized grid container expanding to fill the available space instead of sizing to their content.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "fr" grid tracks whose flex factors sum below 1 taking all of the free space instead of their fraction of it.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Give this element a name" in the Inspector Overrides menu doing nothing for an unnamed element from a nested UXML document in a UI stage.
    (UUM-157281)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Invalid value for image texture Keyword" warnings being logged when a stylesheet sets the `--unity-image` custom property to `none`.
    ([UUM-153254](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153254))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "IsoLangToDelegate not found for mk" error when a Smart String plural is formatted for Macedonian, and fixed counts such as 111 selecting the wrong plural form.
    ([UUM-161885](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161885))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "minmax\(\)" grid tracks staying at their minimum size when the same axis also contains an "fr" track.
    ([UUM-150538](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150538))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Property is not associated with a valid UXML attribute description." errors when selecting a read-only element in the UI Viewport with two Inspector windows open.
    ([UUM-155032](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155032))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "TwoPaneSplitView needs exactly 2 children" errors in the Console when dragging an element into a Two Pane Split View in the Hierarchy window.
    ([UUM-152708](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152708))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed "Unpack Template" in the UI Viewport discarding attribute overrides that target elements inside nested template instances.
    ([UUM-151606](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151606))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a "SelectorAccelerationCache: association already exists" error being logged when a style sheet is attached to a UI Document both directly and through @import.
    ([UUM-151721](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151721))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a bound Vector4Field overwriting the W value of other selected objects when X, Y or Z was edited with multiple objects selected.
    ([UUM-157590](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157590))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a crash in NativeSelectorMatcher when a StyleSheet asset containing a malformed style value was deleted while a VisualElement still referenced it.
    ([UUM-153734](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153734))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a crash when a Smart String template referenced itself, directly or through another template. Smart Strings now reports a FormattingException that names the template.
    ([UUM-158418](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158418))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a crash when a UXML file instantiates a template that instantiates it back.
    ([UUM-155265](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155265))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a crash when calling Painter2D.Fill\(\) on a path with very large coordinates, such as an arc with a radius of 1e20.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a crash when the Editor sat idle in UI Staging Mode with filters applied to an element.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a Cursor UXML attribute losing its hotspot of \(1, 1\), which was imported as \(0, 0\).
    ([UUM-159484](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159484))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a custom cursor image flickering or reverting to the default cursor when hovering UI in the UI Viewport preview.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a duplicated element being given the same authoring ID as the original, which caused an error when the UXML file was reimported.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a dynamic height ListView leaving the last row unreachable after reordering a row to the end of a scrolled list.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a Gradient UXML attribute loading with different alpha values after saving when an alpha key had a very large value.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed a hex or named color being lost from a UXML Color list attribute when an rgb\(\) color follows it.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where undoing the deletion of a selector in the Style Sheets window restored it at the wrong position.
    ([UUM-149380](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149380))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where we were calculating the popup index before the validate code had run.
    ([UUM-157589](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157589))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed an ObjectDisposedException when undoing the addition of a Localized String or Localized String List variable in a Variables Group asset.
    ([UUM-157973](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157973))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed an out-of-bounds stack read when binding UI Toolkit style properties to animation curves.

- UI Toolkit: Fixed animation-duration and animation-delay using seconds instead of milliseconds when their value comes from a custom property through var\(\).
    ([UUM-154595](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154595))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed auto margins having no effect on a child element of a grid container.
