    ([UUM-160913](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160913))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed TypeConversion.TryConvert storing the bit pattern of a float or double instead of its value when converting to an enum.
    ([UUM-158097](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158097))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Builder adding a duplicate attribute to Attribute Overrides when editing an override on an element whose name contains a space, which made the UXML file invalid.
    ([UUM-149755](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149755))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Builder failing to open a USS file when a var\(\) fallback contained a function with an empty argument.
    ([UUM-157166](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157166))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Builder keeping discarded style sheet changes after going back more than one level from a template opened in context.
    (UUM-153328)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Builder marking the UXML and USS files as unsaved when it discarded an invalid or empty rename.
    ([UUM-154717](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154717))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Builder Save not writing elements added in the Hierarchy before the UI Builder window opened.
    ([UUM-156841](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156841))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Toolkit content in the Game view disappearing after importing an asset.
    ([UUM-146120](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146120))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Toolkit panels not receiving pointer events after a script reload in Play mode when the scene has an EventSystem.
    ([UUM-156105](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156105))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UI Toolkit UI in the scene not responding to input after entering Play mode while a prefab with a screen-space UI Document or Panel Renderer was open in Prefab Mode.
    ([UUM-158909](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158909))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UIAnimationClip staying at its authored values instead of animating in a built player.
    (UUM-153273)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UIToolkitInteroperabilityBridge allocating GC memory every frame for each UI Toolkit panel.
    ([UUM-158158](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158158))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed undo not removing a style class from an element that has other classes.
    ([UUM-153885](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153885))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed Undo restoring a partially typed value instead of the previous value in some UI Builder Inspector style fields.
    ([UUM-159163](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159163))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed unpacked template elements losing their inline styles in UI Builder when Unpack Instance is undone and redone.
    ([UUM-155125](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155125))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed unpacking a template instance copying its authoring ID onto the element that replaced it.
    (UUM-151460)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed unsaved elements of a new UI Document being lost after entering and exiting Play Mode.
    ([UUM-156727](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156727))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed unsaved UXML changes being discarded in UI Builder when "Open selector in IDE" was used while the style sheet had unsaved changes.
    ([UUM-156629](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156629))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed USS files and UXML inline styles losing all of their styles when a declaration ended with an extra semicolon.
    ([UUM-157228](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157228))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed USS filter functions with more than four arguments not applying; the extra arguments are now ignored and a warning is logged.
    (UUM-155773)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed USS variables not working inside gradient background definitions.
    ([UUM-148506](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-148506))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UXML changes no longer syncing between UI Builder windows after one of them was maximized and restored.
    ([UUM-151080](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151080))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed UXML changes saved in UI Builder not appearing in the Game view for a Panel Renderer after Play mode was entered and exited.
    ([UUM-149762](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149762))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VectorUtils.Bounds returning bounds that are too small for some curved Bezier segments.
    ([UUM-158205](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158205))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VectorUtils.FlipVerticesInBounds moving vertices outside the bounds when the bounds do not start at y = 0.
    ([UUM-158215](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158215))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VectorUtils.MakeArc returning a quarter circle when the sweep angle is 0.
    ([UUM-158218](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158218))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VectorUtils.MakeArc returning an empty path when the sweep angle is a full circle.
    ([UUM-158219](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158219))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VectorUtils.SceneNodeBounds ignoring the closing curve of closed contours.
    ([UUM-158209](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158209))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VectorUtils.SegmentsLength measuring the closing curve of a closed path as a straight line.
    ([UUM-158208](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158208))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed VisualElement.generateVisualContent allocating memory on every read on a TextElement with an added handler.
    ([UUM-158709](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158709))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed world-space picking selecting the last child of a curved element when another child had its curvature set as an inline style.
    ([UUM-157962](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157962))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed world-space UI Toolkit panels not responding to XR input in players when the application window loses focus.
    (UUM-146634)

- UI Toolkit: Fixed z-index animated through an AnimationClip applying an incorrect value, which moved the element above its siblings.
    ([UUM-150198](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150198))

- UI Toolkit: Fixed `SharedTableData.AddKey`, `ResourceTable.AddStringEntry`, and `DistributedUIDGenerator.MachineId` rejecting or altering their input without reporting it.
    ([UUM-151776](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151776))

- UI Toolkit: Improved grid inspector layout in UI Toolkit inspector.
    (UUM-153423)

- UI Toolkit: Improved performance of hash code generation in GetTransitionHashCode
    (UUM-153205)

- UI Toolkit: Improved performance of Smart Strings plural and choose formatters.
    (UUM-153944)

- UI Toolkit: Improved the performance of updating changed elements in large panels.
    (UUM-156828)

- UI Toolkit: Templates created from the Hierarchy with "Create Template" could not be opened in the UI Stage after being selected.
    ([UUM-151369](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151369))

- UI Toolkit: The Resource Tables window now opens on the last selected table collection, or the first one, instead of showing no collection.

- UI Toolkit: UI Builder: Fixed the data source path completer popup in the binding window sometimes staying open after the window was moved.
    (UUM-132416)

- UI Toolkit: Updated the inspector field in stage to match the existing renaming behavior.
    ([UUM-153274](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153274))

- Unity Physics: Broadphase.Tree.Clone\(\) now allocates node storage that is sized to its capacity rather than the source tree's Length. This fixes an OutOfRange error that may be observed when a full multi-threaded broadphase rebuild is performed on a densely-packed, cloned world.
    (UUM-157069)

- Unity Physics: Fixed a NullReferenceException when calling Collider.ToMesh\(\) on a sphere or capsule collider, or a compound collider containing one, in Edit Mode or when entering Play Mode.
    (UUM-160642)

- Unity Physics: Fixed the behavior of the combine functions for converted materials so they match the built-in physics one. This change is opt-in and can be enabled from the Unity.Physics project settings pane.
    ([UUM-132643](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-132643))

- Unity Physics: Vertex counting to select the correct rendering index format for a convex mesh that has more than 65535 vertices when using a combined mesh
    (UUM-158827)

- Unity Physics: `Collider.ToMesh()` leaked an intermediate mesh for every convex, mesh and terrain collider it converted. These meshes are now destroyed once they are combined into the returned mesh.
    (UUM-158827)

- URP: check actual built-in materials, instead of just their names, when converting assets.
    (UUM-157570)

- URP: Check materials is all Renderers, not just MeshRenderers, when scanning for items to convert.
    (UUM-157571)

- URP: Ensure command line URP converter scanning saves its report to a persistent location.
    (UUM-155565)

- URP: Fix a bug where having the material inspector and scene view on screen together causes error spam when using screen space reflections.
    ([UUM-152569](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152569))

- URP: Fix a bug where reloading a scene would cause certain `Blitter` functions to stop working.
    (UUM-153092)

- URP: Fix a bug where URP attempts to use a stripped variant when using deferred rendering with additional light shadows disabled, but shadowmasks enabled.
    ([UUM-153600](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153600))

- URP: Fix Data-driven Lens Flare occlusion under an orthographic camera
    (UUM-142763)

- URP: Fix missing SpeedTree7 bark on PowerVR Vulkan with a never-firing clip
    (UUM-155486)

- URP: Fix MSAA error in the Rendering Debugger Motion Vector view
    ([UUM-142923](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-142923))

- URP: Fixed "Default" being added to some Enum Material properties
    ([UUM-134256](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-134256))

- URP: Fixed "Shader is not supported" log messages when post-processing shaders are stripped but not used
    ([UUM-158025](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158025))

- URP: Fixed a bug where rays heading towards the camera would require unnecessarily large Max Ray Steps to find a hit in URP Screen Space Reflections, when using Hierarchical Marching mode.
    (UUM-159190)

- URP: Fixed a bug where reloading a scene that contains embedded meshes causes Surface Cache GI to stop working.
    (UUM-152963)

- URP: Fixed a crash on some iOS devices when using the Forward+ rendering path with Render Scale not set to 1.
    ([UUM-151281](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151281))

- URP: Fixed a macro redefinition warning in custom shaders that define REQUIRES_WORLD_SPACE_POS_INTERPOLATOR before including Core.hlsl.
    ([UUM-155102](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155102))

- URP: Fixed a performance regression caused by redundant clears triggering full-screen quad draws when opaque pass is skipped
    (UUM-153597)

- URP: Fixed a performance regression on tile-based GPUs when a camera stack contains overlay cameras that render no geometry.

- URP: Fixed a performance regression when using per-light soft shadows quality.
    (UUM-156462)

- URP: Fixed a potentially uninitialized variable shader warning logged on Metal and Vulkan when using the Surface Cache GI renderer feature.
    ([UUM-154619](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154619))

- URP: Fixed a typo in the Camera Inspector message shown when a Pixel Perfect Camera overrides the projection settings.
    ([UUM-161661](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161661))

- URP: Fixed AlphaUpscaleHistory being allocated when Alpha Processing is disabled
    ([UUM-154311](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154311))

- URP: Fixed ambient occlusion settings being lost after upgrading a project in which the Screen Space Ambient Occlusion Renderer Feature is only on a non-default Renderer.
    ([UUM-159977](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159977))

- URP: Fixed an 'Ignoring duplicate keyword line' warning shown in the Inspector when selecting the URP FallbackLoading shader.
    ([UUM-153934](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153934))

- URP: Fixed an assertion in the console when selecting the Render Deferred Lighting pass in the Frame Debugger.
    (UUM-149901)

- URP: Fixed an exception thrown after setting a camera's viewport rect to a near-zero size and back while alpha processing and a temporal upscaler were enabled.
    ([UUM-155712](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155712))

- URP: Fixed an issue where a prefab opened in Prefab Mode kept contributing to Surface Cache global illumination after exiting Prefab Mode.
    (UUM-151920)

- URP: Fixed an issue where a Render Pipeline Converter scan did not list assets that were created or modified just before the scan started.
    (UUM-154992)

- URP: Fixed an issue where changing Shadow Layers with multiple Lights selected only updated the first Light, and undo did not revert it. Lights in scenes saved before this fix might keep outdated shadow layers if they were edited this way; set their Shadow Layers again to update them.
    ([UUM-157356](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157356))

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter in batch mode reported animation clip conversions that failed with an exception as successful, which hid them from Project Auditor's remaining issues list.
    (UUM-155521)

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter reported prefabs as converted, but lost their changes when it also converted objects in scenes that were not open.
    ([UUM-160400](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160400))

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter's Animation Clip converter sometimes did not report animation clips used by an Animator Controller, so repeated scans of an unchanged project returned different results.
    (UUM-154762)

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter's Material Reference Converter skipped GameObjects in scenes that were not open.
    (UUM-157664)

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline Converter's Post-Processing Stack v2 converter skipped GameObjects in scenes that were not open.
    ([UUM-151409](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151409))

- URP: Fixed an issue where the Renderer List and Culling samples in the URP RenderGraph Samples logged errors every frame when used with a 2D Renderer that has Depth/Stencil Buffer disabled.
    ([UUM-157948](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157948))

- URP: Fixed baked main light shadows in Distance Shadowmask mode appearing close to the camera, instead of at the shadow Max Distance, on frames where no realtime shadow casters are drawn.
    (UUM-159165)

- URP: Fixed blocky gaps at the edges of spot lights in Forward+ and Deferred+ when the light is attached to the camera.
    ([UUM-154016](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154016))

- URP: Fixed Bloom intensity getting overridden by Screen Space Lens Flare intensity.
    (UUM-158619)

- URP: Fixed camera normals and rendering layers textures not being available to shaders when the prepass is skipped.

- URP: Fixed custom shaders failing to compile and rendering magenta when calling ApplyTonemap or ApplyColorGrading from PostProcessing/Common.hlsl.
    ([UUM-154325](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154325))

- URP: Fixed DBuffer decal error when the prepass is required but skipped due to being empty.
    ([UUM-156468](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156468))

- URP: Fixed downsampled Screen Space Ambient Occlusion sampling normals and blue noise at texel corners, which made the result differ between GPUs.
    (UUM-154972)

- URP: Fixed Editor flickering and blank windows when a camera's Output Texture is a Render Texture with Shadow Sampling Mode set to Compare Depths.
    ([UUM-155764](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155764))

- URP: Fixed emission being lost when converting a Standard \(Specular setup\) material to URP
    ([UUM-159486](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159486))

- URP: Fixed Forward+ rendering only the clear color on some OpenGL ES 3 devices when a camera has no additional lights or reflection probes.

- URP: Fixed forward-only materials, such as Complex Lit, not receiving additional light shadows when the Rendering Path is set to Deferred and Transparent Receive Shadows is disabled.
    ([UUM-155328](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155328))

- URP: Fixed GC allocations in Surface Cache GI when many entities are spawned, destroyed, or change materials in the same frame.
    (UUM-154342)

- URP: Fixed incorrect normals and specular highlights on Shader Graph terrain materials in the Deferred and Deferred+ rendering paths, on terrains that need more than one splatmap.
    (UUM-153010)

- URP: Fixed incorrect wording in the Rendering Debugger warning shown when the STP upscaling filter is not enabled, which referred to a single debug view instead of multiple.
    ([UUM-154136](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154136))

- URP: Fixed lights missing from parts of the screen with the Forward+ or Deferred+ rendering path for some spot light orientations and for spot lights close to the camera's near plane.

- URP: Fixed long player build times in 2D projects caused by unneeded Rendering Layer Mask shader variants in Sprite Lit, Sprite Custom Lit, and VFX Graph shaders.
    (UUM-158073)

- URP: Fixed Medium quality soft shadows costing as much GPU time as High quality on Apple GPUs.
    ([UUM-158437](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158437))

- URP: Fixed missing shadows on certain shadow casting point and spot lights, in scenes with a large number of lights using the Deferred renderer when Transparent Receive Shadows was enabled.
    (UUM-134596)

- URP: Fixed multi-display support in standalone URP 2D/3D applications.
    (UUM-155833)

- URP: Fixed NullReferenceExceptions from MaterialPostprocessor when an empty or corrupt .mat file was imported.
    ([UUM-157340](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157340))

- URP: Fixed objects using Lit, Simple Lit, Complex Lit, Baked Lit and Terrain Lit materials not rendering in the Player when the REFLECTION_PROBE_ROTATION keyword was compiled as a dynamic branch, for example with Fast Build enabled.
    ([UUM-157641](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157641))

- URP: Fixed point and spot lights disappearing near screen edges and the camera near plane in Forward+ and Deferred+.
    ([UUM-146356](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146356))

- URP: Fixed Project Auditor URP settings issues for Quality Levels naming the Quality Level instead of the assigned URP asset.
    (UUM-155704)

- URP: Fixed Screen Space Ambient Occlusion Renderer Feature error spam when tile-only mode is enabled.
    ([UUM-155149](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155149))

- URP: Fixed scripts that subclass BaseShaderGUI failing to compile with error CS0122.
    ([UUM-153745](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153745))

- URP: Fixed shader warnings that appeared on the URP Baked Lit shader.
    ([UUM-153793](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153793))

- URP: Fixed Shadow Caster 2D on a Sprite Skin keeping the unflipped shadow when the sprite was flipped while its bones were not moving.
    ([UUM-158409](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158409))

- URP: Fixed ShadowUtils.GetMaxTileResolutionInAtlas returning a tile resolution too large to fit the requested number of tiles when the atlas dimensions are not powers of two.
    ([UUM-161073](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161073))

- URP: Fixed spot lights behind the camera losing the top of their lit area when using the Forward+ or Deferred+ rendering path.
    ([UUM-159132](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159132))

- URP: Fixed Surface Cache GI repeatedly logging "BeginDrain without a matching EndDrain" assertions after an exception occurred during the surface cache world update.
    (UUM-152840)

- URP: Fixed Surface Cache GI throwing "SurfaceCacheEntityChangeBridge supports only a single consumer" exceptions when the renderer feature is used by more than one renderer.
    (UUM-152840)

- URP: Fixed Surface Cache Global Illumination only lighting part of the screen when the Render Scale is not 1.
    (UUM-154747)

- URP: Fixed Surface Cache Global Illumination producing incorrect or missing bounce lighting in player builds that use vertex compression.
    (UUM-155591)

- URP: Fixed terrain becoming progressively brighter in the Forward and Forward+ rendering paths when a Shader Graph terrain shader is used with more than four terrain layers.
    ([UUM-149876](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149876))

- URP: Fixed terrain becoming progressively darker in the Deferred and Deferred+ rendering paths when a Shader Graph terrain shader is used with more than four terrain layers.
    ([UUM-149876](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149876))

- URP: Fixed the 2D Renderer creating a depth and stencil buffer in players when Disable Depth and Stencil Buffers is enabled in Player Settings.
    ([UUM-150232](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150232))

- URP: Fixed the Disable Mip Caching tooltip in the Rendering Debugger so that it refers to the Texture Streamer and Mipmap Streaming instead of internal class names.
    ([UUM-154139](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154139))

- URP: Fixed the Migrate to URP wizard's Fix step and the Built-in to URP batch converter modifying the scene template materials inside the URP package.
    ([UUM-161532](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161532))

- URP: Fixed the Pixel Perfect Camera and Parametric to Freeform Light converters in the Render Pipeline Converter skipping objects in scenes that are not open.
    (UUM-158152)

- URP: Fixed the Pixel Perfect Camera converter in the Render Pipeline Converter staying disabled and reporting the 2D Pixel Perfect package as not installed when it was installed.
    (UUM-154777)

- URP: Fixed the Project Auditor URP0001 issue message, which was garbled when no URP asset was assigned to a Quality Level, so it now names the Quality Level.
    ([UUM-155562](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155562))

- URP: Fixed the Render Face dropdown being shown and editable on Shader Graph Lit and Unlit materials even when Allow Material Override was disabled, where it had no effect.
    ([UUM-152719](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152719))

- URP: Fixed the Render Pipeline Converter defaulting its Target Pipeline to Universal Render Pipeline \(2D Renderer\) instead of Universal Render Pipeline \(Universal Renderer\).
    ([UUM-151492](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151492))

- URP: Fixed the Render Pipeline Converter's Material Reference Converter failing with a NullReferenceException on Particle Systems when the replacement material is unavailable; it now reports which material could not be replaced.
    (UUM-151534)

- URP: Fixed the Screen Space Reflection volume override being available in render pipelines other than URP.
    ([UUM-161070](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161070))

- URP: Fixed transparent materials with alpha clipping rendering fully opaque when drawn by the Render Objects renderer feature with MSAA enabled.
    ([UUM-141725](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141725))

- URP: Fixed transparent objects not rendering in DirectX 12 Players when the Screen Space Shadows renderer feature is enabled and Main Light is disabled.
    ([UUM-146731](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-146731))

- URP: Fixed UAL0010 and UAL0013 warnings in the script that Create &gt; Scripting &gt; URP Post-process Volume Scripts creates when domain reload is disabled.
    ([UUM-155119](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155119))

- URP: Fixed _GBUFFER_NORMALS_OCT being defined in custom shaders that don't declare it.
    ([UUM-155097](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155097))

- URP: Fixes a bug where seams appear on box projected reflection probes on platforms with half precision \(mobile\).
    ([UUM-141981](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141981))

- URP: Migrate SSAO settings into each quality level's volume profile
    (UUM-153567)

- URP: Strip the SSAO variants that pair GTAO with a reconstructed normals source.
    (UUM-157568)

- URP: The Slope-Scale Depth Bias mode no longer produces shadow acne at low depth bias values or texel-aligned stripes on surfaces facing the light head-on. The slider now maps to a slope that never drops below the legacy mode's fixed slope, and a small fixed caster depth offset covers head-on receivers while the caster normal bias mode is active. The legacy mode is unchanged.
    (UUM-154794)

- URP: Unclamped exposure values could break. To solve this it is now clamped at +- 20
    ([UUM-152523](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152523))

- URP: Warn and skip temporal upscalers if MSAA is enabled.
    ([UUM-160358](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160358))

- Version Control: Fixed a crash in UnityYAMLMerge when merging a file with a malformed object header; the tool now reports the error and falls back to the configured text merge tool.

- VFX Graph: Added an issue where the GPU could crash during editor time object destruction.
    (UUM-159682)

- VFX Graph: Addressed an issue where closing a window when multiple were open sometimes resulted in an exception.
    ([UUM-159289](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-159289))

- VFX Graph: Addressed issue where a crash could occur when rapidly instancing / destroying new game objects with VFX components.
    ([UUM-157121](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157121))

- VFX Graph: Allow renaming a category changing only the capitalisation
    ([UUM-151393](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151393))

- VFX Graph: Creating a property with a category name now works as expected instead of not showing the property
    ([UUM-151382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151382))

- VFX Graph: Fix "InvalidOperationException: Sequence contains more than one matching element" error thrown when creating a new Property with an existing attribute name
    ([UUM-151379](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151379))

- VFX Graph: Fix depth collisions sliding
    ([UUM-99470](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-99470))

- VFX Graph: Fixed a crash when entering Play mode with a Visual Effect Graph that uses Automatic bounds.
    ([UUM-155587](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155587))

- VFX Graph: Fixed a GPU crash on certain platforms when a Visual Effect Graph Shader Graph output draws indirectly, for example with sorting, culling, or a Mesh Count above 1.
    (UUM-161030)

- VFX Graph: Fixed a GPU crash or corrupted rendering with sorted Visual Effect Graph outputs when some instances of an effect were outside a camera's view, especially with several cameras.
    (UUM-149758)

- VFX Graph: Fixed a misleading warning when renaming a custom attribute to a built-in attribute name.
    ([UUM-155519](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155519))

- VFX Graph: Fixed a parameter added from the Blackboard appearing with a delay, and an exception when the VFX Graph window was reloaded right after adding one.

- VFX Graph: Fixed a subgraph operator output property with a Range mode which could not be expanded and whose port was invisible until an edge was dragged to it.
    ([UUM-153543](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153543))

- VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where duplicate enum names on a uint property in the VFX Graph Blackboard appeared only once in the dropdown, by appending a number to names that already exist.
    ([UUM-157968](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157968))

- VFX Graph: Fixed Blackboard property order being lost when a VFX Graph is reopened, reversed when several properties are dragged together, and ignored when pasting into a selected category.
    ([UUM-155317](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155317))

- VFX Graph: Fixed Custom HLSL blocks and operators generating invalid shader code when the HLSL source starts with whitespace.
    ([UUM-158603](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-158603))

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicate property and category names that end in a number, such as "New Vector 3", having that number incremented instead of getting a counter in parentheses, such as "New Vector 3 \(1\)".
    ([UUM-157382](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157382))

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a category giving its properties names longer than the property name length limit.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a custom attribute whose name starts or ends with an underscore, such as "_heat", removing that underscore from the copy's name.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a property or category with a name at the length limit leaving two spaces before the counter.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a property, category or custom attribute whose name ends in a large number, such as "Group \(2147483647\)", giving the copy a negative number.

- VFX Graph: Fixed duplicating a property, category or custom attribute whose name ends in a number almost as long as the length limit replacing the whole name with the counter.

- VFX Graph: Fixed error when trying to convert a selection to a block subgraph when that selection contains sticky notes or a group.
    ([UUM-153537](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153537))

- VFX Graph: Fixed numeric fields being dirtied when their labels are clicked on.
    ([UUM-151050](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151050))

- VFX Graph: Fixed opening a graph giving a property the name of a later property, which then got a new name instead.

- VFX Graph: Fixed opening a graph removing leading and trailing underscores from property names and giving duplicate property names an underscore counter.
    ([UUM-155586](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155586))

- VFX Graph: Fixed property and category names keeping leading and trailing whitespace other than spaces, such as tabs, which opening a graph now also removes from existing property names.

- VFX Graph: Fixed renaming a category will move its position to the end of the list when saving
    ([UUM-151383](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151383))

- VFX Graph: Fixed the Blackboard category rename error not explaining why the name is invalid.
    ([UUM-157409](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157409))

- VFX Graph: Fixed the space dropdown arrow being shown as "..." with an incorrect tooltip on Blackboard properties.
    ([UUM-157379](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157379))

- VFX Graph: Fixed the VFX Graph Discussions link in the Visual Effect Graph window opening a missing page.
    ([UUM-154090](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154090))

- VFX Graph: Fixed the Visual Effect Graph shader compilation preferences leaving visual effects compiled with the previous setting when the recompilation prompt was declined.

- VFX Graph: Fixed typo in sort mode tooltip.
    ([UUM-161747](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-161747))

- VFX Graph: Fixed UAL0010 and UAL0013 warnings logged when importing the Output Event Helpers sample.
    ([UUM-155538](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155538))

- VFX Graph: Prevent attaching a Visual Effect from a prefab in the Project window to the VFX window.
    ([UUM-153765](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153765))

- VFX Graph: Prevent attaching a Visual Effect from a prefab in the Project window to the VFX window.
    ([UUM-154540](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-154540))

- VFX Graph: Renaming a Category to a reserved name now logs an error message instead of failing silently
    ([UUM-151381](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151381))

- VFX Graph: SDF baker: Prevent writing out of bounds past the triangleIDs buffer when a mesh exceeds the triangle list size estimate
    ([UUM-152713](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152713))

- VFX Graph: SDF baker: Prevent writing out of bounds past the voxel grid when a mesh face lies exactly on the bake box boundary
    ([UUM-152711](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-152711))

- VFX Graph: VFX graph editor tabs now correctly lose their "dirty" asterisk when changes are undone to the last save point.
    ([UUM-153536](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153536))

- Video: Fix seeking when looping with skipOnDrop disabled, which could stop playback on Android
    (UUM-153527)

- Video: Fixed a crash with DirectX 12 when changing the URL of a playing VideoPlayer on Windows.
    ([UUM-141179](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-141179))

- Video: Fixed a stack-use-after-scope memory safety issue that could occur when importing video clips.
    (UUM-152642)

- Video: Fixed libvpx x86 SIMD files not being compiled with their instruction set on QNX x64.

- Video: Fixed potential crash when opening multiple webcam sessions.
    (UUM-155060)

- Video: Make VideoPlayer_TimeReference_Works pass by fixing the way we detect when to run loopPointReached.
    (UUM-148456)

- Video: Updated libvpx to the most recent version

- Video: VideoClip.width and VideoClip.height now reports full coded size in case of H.264 or H.265 clips with a container crop on Windows. The behaviour is now aligned with OSX.
    ([UUM-150233](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-150233))

- VisionOS: Fixed a crash in `UnityCheckUnloadAndQuit` when a batch-mode player quits while its host process keeps running, for example in an XCTest with a quit handler or in Unity as a Library.
    (UUM-159471)

- VisionOS: Fixed exporting visionOS Xcode project fails after disabling Symlink Sources.
    (UUM-154567)

- Web: Added warnings when scripted change to maximum memory size results in memory size clamping or switch to WebAssembly 2023 target
    ([UUM-151511](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151511))

- Web: Fix Mouse and Touchscreen being registered on Input System startup when not present
    (UUM-150405)

- Web: Fix UnityHttpMessageHandler leaking its Abort\(\) cancellation registration
    ([UUM-157566](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-157566))

- Web: Fixed AudioClip.GetData writing past the end of the destination array, which could crash the player or corrupt memory, when the clip reused the internal sound of a destroyed clip with more channels.
    ([UUM-160614](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-160614))

- Web: Fixed AudioClip.samples and AudioClip.frequency returning 0 while an AudioClip's audio data was still loading. They now report the imported clip's values until loading has finished.
    (UUM-154571)

- Web: Fixed bug where building a WebGL player with both WebAssembly 64 and multithreading enabled would not fail early.
    ([UUM-151065](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151065))

- Web: Fixed Compass permission request to originate from a user gesture to work properly on Safari.
    (UUM-150597)

- Web: In Web builds that use AudioMixers, some audio errors were previously firing, and have now been resolved.
    (UUM-154231)

- WebGL: Fixed a bug where two views of the same WebGPU device filter became out of sync, resulting in an ArgumentOutOfRangeException exception.
    ([UUM-153928](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153928))

- WebGL: Fixed issues with async GPU readback on WebGPU when source texture format was different to destination format.
    (UUM-161129)

- WebGL: Fixed Screen.dpi returning 96 instead of the display's DPI in Web builds when the matchWebGLToCanvasSize template setting was disabled.

- WebGL: Fixed WebGL builds reporting an incorrect Screen.currentResolution when the matchWebGLToCanvasSize template setting was disabled.
    ([UUM-133885](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-133885))

- WebGL: Removed verbose log messages triggered when adding elements to a WebGPU device filter.
    ([UUM-153805](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153805))

- WebGL: WebGPU Fix compressed texture mipmaps.
    ([UUM-155238](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-155238))

- WebGL: WebGPU fix for incorrectly mapped ASTC sRGB formats.
    (UUM-154502)

- WebGL: WebGPU: improve memory usage of uniform buffers and dynamic vertex buffers
    (UUM-152972)

- WebGL: \[WebGPU\] improve cacheability of shaders by not including source location in the compiled shader.
    (UUM-151724)

- Windows: Added DisplayInfo.RegisterLayoutChangedCallback and DisplayInfo.UnregisterLayoutChangedCallback, which notify the application when a display is connected, disconnected or reconfigured.

- Windows: Builds using Visual Studio solution option now once again can be built for any architecture from Visual Studio when using IL2CPP scripting backend, rather than just the one selected at build time in Unity.

- Windows: Fixed a crash on Windows when the Editor ran in batch mode and could not create its main window.
    ([UUM-153339](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153339))

- Windows: Fixed a top-level menu returning to the wrong position in the menu bar after being removed and restored.

- Windows: Fixed Auto Graphics API being disabled and the graphics API list being forced to Direct3D11 when opening a project that has no recorded Editor version.
    (UUM-147208)

- Windows: Fixed display activation failing after introducing the GameWindow API.

- Windows: Fixed GameWindow reporting client rendering area position instead of window frame position.

- Windows: Fixed keys staying pressed in Windows Standalone players after opening and closing the Steam Overlay.
    (UUM-154774)

- Windows: Fixed lag when dragging an object over docked window tabs in the Windows Editor.
    ([UUM-156431](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156431))

- Windows: Reduced size of Windows builds.
    (UUM-153350)

- Windows: Removed extraneous files under `CoreCLR\native` in builds when using Create Visual Studio solution build setting.
    ([UUM-156109](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-156109))

- Windows: The Input System now reports the pressure value the Windows pointer API provides.
    (UUM-150401)

- Windows: Windows: Fixed Mouse.current and Pen.current staying set when no mouse or pen is attached, or after the device is unplugged. Mouse.current is now null when no mouse is attached.
    (UUM-150403)

- Windows: Windows: Fixed Touchscreen.current being set on machines without touch hardware, and not updating when a touchscreen is attached or removed.
    (UUM-150406)

- XR: Fixed an issue where URP scenes rendered black on Meta Quest 2 with Vulkan and MSAA, for example scenes with decals and no transparent objects.
    (UUM-132022)

- XR: Fixed depth being submitted to the XR depth swapchain at the wrong scale
    ([UUM-149514](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149514))

- XR: Fixed incorrect "\!" icon displayed in XR Plug-in Management Help Box, while in Light Mode
    ([UUM-149347](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-149347))

- XR: Fixed missing depth for Quad Views inset region static objects during Application Spacewarp motion vector reprojection pass \(affection motion synthesis near object edges\).
    (UUM-158842)

- XR: Fixed Quad Views motion vector reuse optimization for Application SpaceWarp to correctly handle eye-tracking on capable platforms, avoiding jittering artifacts in the inset region.
    (UUM-153661)

- XR: Fixed regression where fragment density maps weren't attached to a Vulkan render pass.

- XR: Fixed SubsystemWithProvider.Start not throwing an ArgumentNullException when called after the subsystem was destroyed, which restarted the destroyed provider.
    ([UUM-151411](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-151411))

- XR: Legacy Input Helpers: Fixed compiler warnings when this package is imported into Unity 7.

- XR: Now when using intermediate textures in URP \(such as with post-processing\), the XR eye texture will no longer force MSAA when enabled which previously incurred a performance cost with no additional benefit due to already resolving to 1x sampling before the final pass.
    (UUM-155648)

- XR: OnDemandRendering.effectiveRenderFrameRate returns the correct refresh rate on XR devices instead of a default value.

- XR: Quad Views: Fixed issue regarding post processing being applied to both the inner and outer passes separately.
    (UUM-153422)

- XR: Quad Views: Fixed issue where lights were culled within view when forward+ was enabled, causing artifacts in the periphery.
    ([UUM-153433](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-153433))

- XR: Rendering no longer stops on Quest 2 with OpenGL ES3 when a scene changes the MSAA level.
    (UUM-153015)

- XR: The XR app view could become locked to the tracking origin and frozen in place when the application regained focus after the headset went to sleep or a system menu was opened.
    ([UUM-139700](https://issuetracker.unity.com/api/v1.0/redirects/guid/UUM-139700))

- XR: XR Core Utilities: Fixed the Project Validation window so that it never tries to display `NaN` amount of progress to the Editor's progress bar.

- XR: XR Plug-in Management: Fixed issues related to the AutoStaticsCleanup lifecycle attribute, ensuring that statics are properly reset as required by CoreCLR.

- XR: XR Plug-in Management: Fixed the merge behavior of \[ManifestRequirement.OverrideElements\]\(xref:Unity.XR.Management.AndroidManifest.Editor.ManifestRequirement.OverrideElements\), as used by `IAndroidManifestRequirementProvider`:<br>
    - Repeatable elements identified by name, such as `meta-data`, `uses-feature` and `activity`, with a `"name"` key only overwrite elements with a matching `"name"` key. Elements that can only occur once, such as `application`, are still overwritten in place when a `"name"` key is given.<br>
    - Elements with conflicting true/false values for the `"required"` key resolve to `"true"`.<br>
    - Elements with conflicting values for the `"version"` key resolve to the highest provided version number.

- [com.unity.external-dependency-manager@2.1.1](https://docs.unity3d.com/Packages/com.unity.external-dependency-manager@2.1//changelog/CHANGELOG.html)

**Packages deprecated**

- com.unity.2d.pixel-perfect - "The 2D Pixel Perfect package for the Built-In Render Pipeline is deprecated. Remove this package and add the URP Pixel Perfect Camera component instead. For more information, refer to https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/urp/2d-pixelperfect-prep-sprites.html."

- com.unity.connect.share - "The WebGL Publisher package is deprecated and no longer maintained. In Unity 6.1 and newer, upload Web builds to Unity Play directly from File > Build Profiles: select the Web platform and choose Publish to Play."
